local Players = game:GetService("Players")

local Workspace = game:GetService("Workspace")

-- Global settings table
_G.HubData = {
    InvisCounters = false,
    CustomAnims = false
}

-- Shared table to clean up connection loops for custom presets
local Cons = {}

-- List of known Counter Animation IDs to block
local CounterAnimationIDs = {
    ["12447705141"] = true, 
    ["10467554224"] = true, 
    ["118270485922095"] = true, 
    ["15311685628"] = true,     
    ["12351854556"] = true,     
}

-- Safely get the Animator
local function GetAnimator()
    local char = LocalPlayer.Character
    if char then
        local hum = char:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
        if hum then
            return hum:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator")
        end
    end
    return nil
end

-- Check if an animation is a counter based on ID or Name
local function IsCounterTrack(track)
    if not track or not track.Animation then return false end
    
    local animId = tostring(track.Animation.AnimationId):match("%d+")
    local animName = track.Name:lower()
    
    if CounterAnimationIDs[animId] or animName:find("counter") or animName:find("vessel_counter") then
        return true
    end
    return false
end

-- Helper function to find the closest enemy player
local function GetClosestEnemy()
    local closestPlayer = nil
    local shortestDistance = math.huge
    local myChar = LocalPlayer.Character
    local myRoot = myChar and myChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    
    if not myRoot then return nil end
    
    for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if player ~= LocalPlayer and player.Character then
            local enemyRoot = player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            local enemyHum = player.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            
            if enemyRoot and enemyHum and enemyHum.Health > 0 then
                local distance = (myRoot.Position - enemyRoot.Position).Magnitude
                if distance < shortestDistance then
                    shortestDistance = distance
                    closestPlayer = player
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return closestPlayer
end

local function GetVictimModel(hitAttr)
    if not hitAttr then return nil end
    local name = hitAttr:match("([^;]+)")
    if not name then return nil end
    local player = Players:FindFirstChild(name)
    if player and player.Character then return player.Character end
    local live = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Live")
    if live then
        local dummy = live:FindFirstChild(name)
        if dummy then return dummy end
    end
    return Workspace:FindFirstChild(name)
end

-- ==========================================
-- ANIMATION LOGIC & PRESETS
-- ==========================================
local CurrentAnimTrack = nil
local CurrentWalkId = nil
local RunningConnection = nil
local TargetCustomAssetId = "" 

local function ClearCurrentAnimation()
    if CurrentAnimTrack then
        CurrentAnimTrack:Stop()
        CurrentAnimTrack:Destroy()
        CurrentAnimTrack = nil
    end
    if RunningConnection then
        RunningConnection:Disconnect()
        RunningConnection = nil
    end
end

local function PlayCustomAnim(assetId)
    ClearCurrentAnimation()
    CurrentWalkId = nil 

    local animator = GetAnimator()
    if animator then
        local anim = Instance.new("Animation")
        anim.AnimationId = "rbxassetid://" .. assetId
        
        CurrentAnimTrack = animator:LoadAnimation(anim)
        CurrentAnimTrack.Priority = Enum.AnimationPriority.Action4 
        CurrentAnimTrack.Looped = true
        CurrentAnimTrack:Play()
    end
end

local function SetupWalkAnimation(assetId)
    ClearCurrentAnimation()
    CurrentWalkId = assetId

    local char = LocalPlayer.Character
    if not char then return end
    local humanoid = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    local animator = GetAnimator()

    if humanoid and animator then
        local anim = Instance.new("Animation")
        anim.AnimationId = "rbxassetid://" .. assetId

        local function handleRunning(speed)
            if CurrentWalkId ~= assetId then return end 
            
            if speed > 0.5 then 
                for _, track in pairs(animator:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()) do
                    if track.Name == "WalkAnim" or track.Name == "RunAnim" or track.Animation.AnimationId:find("run") or track.Animation.AnimationId:find("walk") then
                        if track ~= CurrentAnimTrack then
                            track:Stop(0.1)
                        end
                    end
                end

                if not CurrentAnimTrack then
                    CurrentAnimTrack = animator:LoadAnimation(anim)
                    CurrentAnimTrack.Priority = Enum.AnimationPriority.Action4 
                    CurrentAnimTrack.Looped = true
                    CurrentAnimTrack:Play(0.2) 
                elseif not CurrentAnimTrack.IsPlaying then
                    CurrentAnimTrack:Play(0.2)
                end
            else
                if CurrentAnimTrack and CurrentAnimTrack.IsPlaying then
                    CurrentAnimTrack:Stop(0.2) 
                end
            end
        end

        handleRunning(humanoid.MoveDirection.Magnitude)
        RunningConnection = humanoid.Running:Connect(handleRunning)
    end
end

LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
    task.wait(0.5) 
    if CurrentWalkId then
        SetupWalkAnimation(CurrentWalkId)
    end
end)

local IdlePresets = { ["Fly"] = "17124061663", ["Helicopter"] = "17862998594" }
local WalkPresets = {
    ["Helicopter"] = "17862998594", ["Girly Walk"] = "17861862787", ["Kazotsky"] = "17861870996",
    ["Hop Walk"] = "17861872519", ["Sassy Walk"] = "17861893094", ["Sword Walk"] = "17120635926",
    ["Yandere"] = "17086054994", ["March"] = "15962443652", ["Hunter"] = "15962326593",
}

local IdleNames = {}
for name, _ in pairs(IdlePresets) do table.insert(IdleNames, name) end
local WalkNames = {}
for name, _ in pairs(WalkPresets) do table.insert(WalkNames, name) end

AnimationSection:Button({
    Title = '🛑 Stop Current Animation',
    Callback = function() 
        ClearCurrentAnimation()
        CurrentWalkId = nil
    end,
})

AnimationSection:Input({
    Title = "Custom Animation ID",
    Placeholder = "Enter Asset ID here...",
    Callback = function(text)
        TargetCustomAssetId = text:gsub("%D", "")
    end
})

AnimationSection:Button({
    Title = '▶️ Play Custom Animation ID',
    Callback = function()
        if TargetCustomAssetId ~= "" then PlayCustomAnim(TargetCustomAssetId) else print("Please enter a valid Animation ID first!") end
    end,
})

AnimationSection:Dropdown({
    Title = "Select Idle Preset",
    Multi = false,
    Values = IdleNames, 
    Callback = function(selected)
        local assetId = IdlePresets[selected]
        if assetId then PlayCustomAnim(assetId) end
    end,
})

AnimationSection:Dropdown({
    Title = "Select Walk Preset",
    Multi = false,
    Values = WalkNames, 
    Callback = function(selected)
        local assetId = WalkPresets[selected]
        if assetId then SetupWalkAnimation(assetId) end
    end,
})

AnimationSection:Toggle({
    Title = 'Invisible Counters',
    Value = false, 
    Callback = function(state) 
        _G.HubData.InvisCounters = state
        if state then
            local animator = GetAnimator()
            if animator then
                for _, track in pairs(animator:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()) do
                    if IsCounterTrack(track) then track:Stop() end
                end
            end
        end
    end
})

local AnimationConnection = nil
local function HookAnimationListener(char)
    if AnimationConnection then AnimationConnection:Disconnect() end
    local hum = char:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 5)
    local animator = hum and hum:WaitForChild("Animator", 5)
    
    if animator then
        AnimationConnection = animator.AnimationPlayed:Connect(function(track)
            if _G.HubData.InvisCounters and IsCounterTrack(track) then
                task.wait() 
                track:Stop(0)
            end
        end)
    end
end

if LocalPlayer.Character then HookAnimationListener(LocalPlayer.Character) end
LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(HookAnimationListener)

AnimationSection:Button({ Title = 'Invisible Block', Callback = function() print("Invisible Block Active") end })

-- === AUTO FARM ===

-- Services & Shared Variables
local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
local Workspace = game:GetService("Workspace")
local player = Players.LocalPlayer

-- Target Selection Settings
local targetType = "Player" -- "Player" or "NPC"
local selectedPlayer = nil

-- Farm States
local autoFarmV1Active = false
autoFarmV2Active = false

-- V1 Orbit Variables
local orbitConnection = nil
local orbitRadius = 4.6
local orbitSpeed = 100

-- V2 Variables
local v2Thread = nil

-- Helper Functions
local function getRootPart(char)
    local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    return hum and hum.RootPart
end

local function getWeakestDummy()
    local dummy = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Weakest Dummy") 
        or (Workspace:FindFirstChild("Live") and Workspace.Live:FindFirstChild("Weakest Dummy"))
        or (Workspace:FindFirstChild("Map") and Workspace.Map:FindFirstChild("Weakest Dummy"))
    
    if not dummy then
        for _, child in ipairs(Workspace:GetChildren()) do
            if child.Name == "Weakest Dummy" and child:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
                return child
            end
        end
    end
    return dummy
end

local function getTargetCharacter()
    if targetType == "Player" then
        return selectedPlayer and selectedPlayer.Character
    elseif targetType == "NPC" then
        return getWeakestDummy()
    end
    return nil
end

local function getPlayerNames()
    local list = {}
    for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if p ~= player then
            table.insert(list, p.Name)
        end
    end
    return list
end

-- ==================== AUTO FARM V1 LOGIC (ORBIT) ====================
local function stopOrbit()
    if orbitConnection then
        orbitConnection:Disconnect()
        orbitConnection = nil
    end
end

local function startOrbit()
    stopOrbit()
    if not autoFarmV1Active then return end

    local angle = 0
    orbitConnection = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(delta)
        local localChar = player.Character
        local targetChar = getTargetCharacter()
        
        if not (localChar and targetChar and targetChar.Parent) then return end

        local localRoot = getRootPart(localChar)
        local targetRoot = targetChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local targetHum = targetChar:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

        if not (localRoot and targetRoot and targetHum and targetHum.Health > 0) then return end

        angle = angle + (delta * orbitSpeed)
        local offset = Vector3.new(math.cos(angle) * orbitRadius, 0, math.sin(angle) * orbitRadius)
        localRoot.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(targetRoot.Position + offset, targetRoot.Position)
    end)
end

-- ==================== AUTO FARM V2 LOGIC (FOLLOW BEHIND) ====================
local function stopV2()
    autoFarmV2Active = false
    v2Thread = nil
end

local function startV2()
    stopV2()
    autoFarmV2Active = true
    
    v2Thread = task.spawn(function()
        while autoFarmV2Active do
            RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
            
            local localChar = player.Character
            local targetChar = getTargetCharacter()

            if localChar and targetChar and targetChar.Parent then
                local localRoot = getRootPart(localChar)
                local targetRoot = targetChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                local targetHum = targetChar:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

                -- Only teleport behind if target is alive and valid
                if localRoot and targetRoot and targetHum and targetHum.Health > 0 then
                    localRoot.CFrame = targetRoot.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0, 3)
                end
            end
        end
    end)
end

-- Tab Setup (Replace Window:Tab with your hub's tab creation method)

-- ==================== TARGET SELECTION SECTION ====================
local TargetSection = FarmTab:Section({
    Title = "Target Settings",
    Opened = true
})

TargetSection:Dropdown({
    Title = "Target Type",
    Values = {"Player", "NPC (Weakest Dummy)"},
    Value = "Player",
    Callback = function(selected)
        if selected == "NPC (Weakest Dummy)" then
            targetType = "NPC"
        else
            targetType = "Player"
        end
    end
})

local PlayerDropdown = TargetSection:Dropdown({
    Title = "Select Target Player",
    Values = getPlayerNames(),
    Value = nil,
    Callback = function(selected)
        selectedPlayer = Players:FindFirstChild(selected)
    end
})

-- Player Connections
Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(function()
    if PlayerDropdown and PlayerDropdown.SetValues then
        PlayerDropdown:SetValues(getPlayerNames())
    end
end)

Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(removedPlayer)
    if PlayerDropdown and PlayerDropdown.SetValues then
        PlayerDropdown:SetValues(getPlayerNames())
    end
    if selectedPlayer == removedPlayer then
        selectedPlayer = nil
    end
end)

-- ==================== AUTO FARM MODES ====================
local FarmSection = FarmTab:Section({
    Title = "Auto Farm Modes",
    Opened = true
})

-- Auto Farm V1 Toggle
FarmSection:Toggle({
    Title = "Enable Auto Farm V1 (Orbit)",
    Value = false,
    Callback = function(state)
        autoFarmV1Active = state
        if state then
            if targetType == "Player" and not selectedPlayer then
                warn("Auto Farm V1: Select a player first!")
                return
            elseif targetType == "NPC" and not getWeakestDummy() then
                warn("Auto Farm V1: Weakest Dummy not found!")
                return
            end
            startOrbit()
        else
            stopOrbit()
        end
    end
})

-- Auto Farm V2 Toggle
FarmSection:Toggle({
    Title = "Enable Auto Farm V2 (Follow Behind)",
    Value = false,
    Callback = function(state)
        if state then
            if targetType == "Player" and not selectedPlayer then
                warn("Auto Farm V2: Select a player first!")
                return
            elseif targetType == "NPC" and not getWeakestDummy() then
                warn("Auto Farm V2: Weakest Dummy not found!")
                return
            end
            startV2()
        else
            stopV2()
        end
    end
})

-- Configuration state
local Config = {
    AutoDash = false,
    DashInterval = 2.0,
}

-- Core dash execution function
local function simulateDash()
    VirtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(true, Enum.KeyCode.Q, false, game)
    task.wait(0.05)
    VirtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(false, Enum.KeyCode.Q, false, game)
end

-- WindUI Toggle element
TargetSection:Toggle({
    Title = "Auto Dash",
    Description = "Automates Q-Dash movement",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(state)
        Config.AutoDash = state
    end,
})

-- Execution loop for Auto Dash
task.spawn(function()
    local lastDashTime = 0
    while true do
        if Config.AutoDash then
            local currentTime = tick()
            if currentTime - lastDashTime >= Config.DashInterval then
                simulateDash()
                lastDashTime = currentTime
            end
        end
        task.wait()
    end
end)

-- Configuration state
local Config = {
    AutoPunch = false,
    PunchDelay = 0.12,
}

-- Target GUI lookup function
local function getPunchButtonPosition()
    local punchBtn = PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("PunchButton", true) 
    if punchBtn and punchBtn:IsA("GuiObject") then
        local pos = punchBtn.AbsolutePosition
        local size = punchBtn.AbsoluteSize
        return Vector2.new(pos.X + (size.X / 2), pos.Y + (size.Y / 2))
    end
    return nil
end

-- Core punch execution function
local function simulatePunch()
    local targetPos = getPunchButtonPosition()
    if targetPos then
        VirtualInputManager:SendMouseButtonEvent(targetPos.X, targetPos.Y, 0, true, game, 0)
        task.wait(0.02)
        VirtualInputManager:SendMouseButtonEvent(targetPos.X, targetPos.Y, 0, false, game, 0)
    else
        local mousePos = game:GetService("UserInputService"):GetMouseLocation()
        VirtualInputManager:SendMouseButtonEvent(mousePos.X, mousePos.Y, 0, true, game, 0)
        task.wait(0.02)
        VirtualInputManager:SendMouseButtonEvent(mousePos.X, mousePos.Y, 0, false, game, 0)
    end
end

-- WindUI Toggle element
TargetSection:Toggle({
    Title = "Auto Punch",
    Description = "Spams the PunchButton UI element",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(state)
        Config.AutoPunch = state
    end,
})

-- Execution loop for Auto Punch
task.spawn(function()
    while true do
        if Config.AutoPunch then
            simulatePunch()
            task.wait(Config.PunchDelay)
        end
        task.wait()
    end
end)

-- Global Config and Task Manager
_G.DeathVoidFlags = _G.DeathVoidFlags or {
    Enabled = false,
    TeleportDelay = 2.1,
    VoidDuration = 1.0
}

_G.DeathVoidConnections = _G.DeathVoidConnections or {}

mainTab:Toggle({
    Title = "twin fang void",
    Value = false,
    Callback = function(state)
        local Players = game:GetService("Players")
        local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
        local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
        
        local TARGET_ANIM_ID = 18896229321
        local inVoid = false

        _G.DeathVoidFlags.Enabled = state

        -- Disconnect existing connections when toggling
        for _, conn in pairs(_G.DeathVoidConnections) do
            if conn then conn:Disconnect() end
        end
        table.clear(_G.DeathVoidConnections)

        if not state then return end

        pcall(function()
            workspace.FallenPartsDestroyHeight = -50000
        end)

        ------------------------------------------------------------------------
        -- CORE LOGIC
        ------------------------------------------------------------------------

        local function applyAntiDeath(char)
            local hum = char:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 5)
            local hrp = char:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 5)
            if not hum or not hrp then return end

            local lastHP = hum.Health

            local renderConn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
                if hum and hum.Parent then
                    lastHP = hum.Health
                end
            end)
            table.insert(_G.DeathVoidConnections, renderConn)

            local hpConn = hum:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Health"):Connect(function()
                if _G.DeathVoidFlags.Enabled and hum.Health <= 0 and hrp.Position.Y <= 0 then
                    hum.Health = lastHP
                end
            end)
            table.insert(_G.DeathVoidConnections, hpConn)
        end

        local function setupDeathCounter(char)
            local hum = char:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 5)
            if not hum then return end

            local animConn = hum.AnimationPlayed:Connect(function(track)
                if not _G.DeathVoidFlags.Enabled or inVoid then return end
                
                local animId = track.Animation and track.Animation.AnimationId
                local animNum = animId and tonumber(animId:match("%d+"))
                
                if animNum == TARGET_ANIM_ID then
                    inVoid = true

                    task.spawn(function()
                        local hrp = char:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 3)
                        if not hrp then inVoid = false return end

                        task.wait(_G.DeathVoidFlags.TeleportDelay)

                        if hrp and hrp.Parent then
                            local returnCFrame = hrp.CFrame
                            local targetDepth = -5000
                            
                            local platform = Instance.new("Part")
                            platform.Size = Vector3.new(30, 2, 30)
                            platform.Anchored = true
                            platform.Transparency = 1
                            platform.CanCollide = true
                            platform.Position = Vector3.new(hrp.Position.X, targetDepth - 1, hrp.Position.Z)
                            platform.Parent = workspace
                            
                            hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                            hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
                            hrp.CFrame = platform.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, 4, 0)
                            
                            track:AdjustSpeed(0)
                            
                            task.wait(_G.DeathVoidFlags.VoidDuration)
                            
                            if hrp and hrp.Parent then
                                hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                                hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
                                hrp.CFrame = returnCFrame
                            end
                            
                            if track then
                                track:Stop()
                            end
                            
                            if platform then 
                                platform:Destroy() 
                            end
                            
                            local currentCam = workspace.CurrentCamera
                            if currentCam then
                                if hum then 
                                    currentCam.CameraSubject = hum
                                elseif hrp then 
                                    currentCam.CameraSubject = hrp 
                                end
                                currentCam.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Custom
                            end
                        end
                        
                        inVoid = false
                    end)
                end
            end)
            table.insert(_G.DeathVoidConnections, animConn)
        end

        local function HookCharacter(char)
            applyAntiDeath(char)
            setupDeathCounter(char)
        end

        -- Initialize for current character & future respawns
        if LocalPlayer.Character then HookCharacter(LocalPlayer.Character) end
        local charAddedConn = LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(HookCharacter)
        table.insert(_G.DeathVoidConnections, charAddedConn)
    end,
})

-- Global Config and Connections Manager
_G.DeathVoidConnections = _G.DeathVoidConnections or {}
_G.DeathVoidState = _G.DeathVoidState or false

mainTab:Toggle({
    Title = "Anti-Death & Death Counter ",
    Value = false,
    Callback = function(state)
        local Players = game:GetService("Players")
        local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
        local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer

        local TARGET_ANIM_ID = 11343250001
        local inVoid = false

        _G.DeathVoidState = state

        -- Clear active connections when toggling off/re-enabling
        for _, conn in pairs(_G.DeathVoidConnections) do
            if conn then conn:Disconnect() end
        end
        table.clear(_G.DeathVoidConnections)

        if not state then return end

        -- Safe FallenPartsDestroyHeight setup
        pcall(function()
            workspace.FallenPartsDestroyHeight = -50000
        end)

        ------------------------------------------------------------------------
        -- 1. ANTI-DEATH LOGIC
        ------------------------------------------------------------------------
        local function applyAntiDeath(char)
            local hum = char:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 5)
            local hrp = char:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 5)
            if not hum or not hrp then return end

            local lastHP = hum.Health

            local renderConn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
                if hum and hum.Parent then
                    lastHP = hum.Health
                end
            end)
            table.insert(_G.DeathVoidConnections, renderConn)

            local hpConn = hum:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Health"):Connect(function()
                if _G.DeathVoidState and hum.Health <= 0 and hrp.Position.Y <= 0 then
                    hum.Health = lastHP
                end
            end)
            table.insert(_G.DeathVoidConnections, hpConn)
        end

        ------------------------------------------------------------------------
        -- 2. DEATH COUNTER VOID TELEPORT LOGIC
        ------------------------------------------------------------------------
        local function setupDeathCounter(char)
            local hum = char:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 5)
            if not hum then return end

            local animConn = hum.AnimationPlayed:Connect(function(track)
                if not _G.DeathVoidState or inVoid then return end

                local animId = track.Animation and track.Animation.AnimationId
                local animNum = animId and tonumber(animId:match("%d+"))

                if animNum == TARGET_ANIM_ID then
                    local targetDepth = -5000
                    local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

                    if hrp then
                        inVoid = true

                        -- Store precise return position
                        local returnCFrame = hrp.CFrame

                        -- Temporary void platform
                        local platform = Instance.new("Part")
                        platform.Size = Vector3.new(20, 1, 20)
                        platform.Anchored = true
                        platform.Transparency = 1
                        platform.CanCollide = true
                        platform.Position = Vector3.new(hrp.Position.X, targetDepth - 0.5, hrp.Position.Z)
                        platform.Parent = workspace

                        -- Reset velocity before teleporting
                        hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                        hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
                        hrp.CFrame = platform.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0)

                        -- Return delay
                        task.delay(6, function()
                            if hrp and hrp.Parent then
                                hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                                hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero

                                -- Force position over multiple frames to bypass physics glitching
                                for i = 1, 5 do
                                    hrp.CFrame = returnCFrame
                                    RunService.RenderStepped:Wait()
                                end
                            end

                            if platform then
                                platform:Destroy()
                            end

                            -- Reset Camera
                            local currentCam = workspace.CurrentCamera
                            if currentCam then
                                if hum then
                                    currentCam.CameraSubject = hum
                                elseif hrp then
                                    currentCam.CameraSubject = hrp
                                end
                                currentCam.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Custom
                            end

                            inVoid = false
                        end)
                    end
                end
            end)
            table.insert(_G.DeathVoidConnections, animConn)
        end

        -- Hook current character and future respawns
        local function HookCharacter(char)
            applyAntiDeath(char)
            setupDeathCounter(char)
        end

        if LocalPlayer.Character then HookCharacter(LocalPlayer.Character) end
        local charAddedConn = LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(HookCharacter)
        table.insert(_G.DeathVoidConnections, charAddedConn)
    end,
})

-- === GAROU SECTION ===

garouTab:Toggle({
    Title = "Lethal Tech",
    Value = false,
    Callback = function(isEnabled)
        local Players = game:GetService("Players")
        local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
        local VirtualInputManager = game:GetService("VirtualInputManager")

        _G.LethalTechEnabled = isEnabled

        -- Cleanup active connections and instances from previous state runs
        if _G.LethalTechConns then
            for _, conn in ipairs(_G.LethalTechConns) do
                if typeof(conn) == "RBXScriptConnection" then
                    conn:Disconnect()
                end
            end
        end
        _G.LethalTechConns = {}

        local function cleanupLethalParts()
            if _G.LethalAlignPos then
                _G.LethalAlignPos:Destroy()
                _G.LethalAlignPos = nil
            end
            if _G.LethalAlignOrient then
                _G.LethalAlignOrient:Destroy()
                _G.LethalAlignOrient = nil
            end
            if _G.LethalRenderStep then
                _G.LethalRenderStep:Disconnect()
                _G.LethalRenderStep = nil
            end
            if _G.LethalPart then
                _G.LethalPart:Destroy()
                _G.LethalPart = nil
            end

            local player = Players.LocalPlayer
            local char = player and player.Character
            local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if hrp then
                for _, child in ipairs(hrp:GetChildren()) do
                    if child:IsA("Attachment") or child.Name == "HasSnapped" then
                        child:Destroy()
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        cleanupLethalParts()

        if not isEnabled then return end

        local player = Players.LocalPlayer
        if not player then return end

        -- Config & Animation IDs
        local TARGET_ANIMATIONS = {
            ["10503381238"] = true,
            ["13379003796"] = true
        }

        local maxDetectionRadius = 120
        local dashDelay = 0.32
        local attachDuration = 0.45
        local isExecuting = false

        --------------------------------------------------------------------------------
        -- Target & Movement Logic
        --------------------------------------------------------------------------------

        local function getNearestTarget(myRoot)
            local closest = nil
            local shortestDist = maxDetectionRadius
            local liveFolder = workspace:FindFirstChild("Live")

            if liveFolder then
                for _, child in ipairs(liveFolder:GetChildren()) do
                    if child:IsA("Model") and child ~= player.Character then
                        local targetRoot = child:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                        local targetHum = child:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

                        if targetRoot and targetHum and targetHum.Health > 0 then
                            local dist = (targetRoot.Position - myRoot.Position).Magnitude
                            if dist < shortestDist then
                                shortestDist = dist
                                closest = targetRoot
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
            return closest
        end

        local function lockToTarget(targetRoot)
            if not targetRoot or not targetRoot.Parent then return end

            local char = player.Character
            local myRoot = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if not myRoot or not myRoot.Parent then return end

            cleanupLethalParts()

            local followPart = Instance.new("Part")
            followPart.Size = Vector3.new(0.5, 0.5, 0.5)
            followPart.Transparency = 1
            followPart.Anchored = true
            followPart.CanCollide = false
            followPart.Name = "LethalPart"
            followPart.Parent = workspace
            _G.LethalPart = followPart

            local att0_pos = Instance.new("Attachment", myRoot)
            local att1_pos = Instance.new("Attachment", followPart)
            local att0_rot = Instance.new("Attachment", myRoot)
            local att1_rot = Instance.new("Attachment", followPart)

            local alignPos = Instance.new("AlignPosition")
            alignPos.Attachment0 = att0_pos
            alignPos.Attachment1 = att1_pos
            alignPos.RigidityEnabled = true
            alignPos.Responsiveness = 200
            alignPos.MaxForce = math.huge
            alignPos.Parent = myRoot
            _G.LethalAlignPos = alignPos

            local alignOrient = Instance.new("AlignOrientation")
            alignOrient.Attachment0 = att0_rot
            alignOrient.Attachment1 = att1_rot
            alignOrient.RigidityEnabled = true
            alignOrient.Responsiveness = 200
            alignOrient.MaxTorque = math.huge
            alignOrient.Parent = myRoot
            _G.LethalAlignOrient = alignOrient

            local renderConn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
                if targetRoot and targetRoot.Parent and myRoot and myRoot.Parent then
                    local targetPos = targetRoot.Position
                    local targetCFrame = CFrame.new(targetPos.X, targetPos.Y, targetPos.Z) * CFrame.Angles(math.rad(85), 0, 0)
                    
                    followPart.CFrame = targetCFrame

                    if not myRoot:FindFirstChild("HasSnapped") then
                        myRoot.CFrame = targetCFrame
                        local snapVal = Instance.new("BoolValue")
                        snapVal.Name = "HasSnapped"
                        snapVal.Parent = myRoot
                    end
                end
            end)
            _G.LethalRenderStep = renderConn
            table.insert(_G.LethalTechConns, renderConn)
        end

        local function executeLethalTech()
            if isExecuting or not _G.LethalTechEnabled then return end
            isExecuting = true

            task.spawn(function()
                task.wait(dashDelay)

                -- Send Dash Key Press
                VirtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(true, Enum.KeyCode.Q, false, game)
                VirtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(false, Enum.KeyCode.Q, false, game)

                local char = player.Character
                local myRoot = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if myRoot then
                    local targetRoot = getNearestTarget(myRoot)
                    if targetRoot then
                        lockToTarget(targetRoot)
                        task.delay(attachDuration, cleanupLethalParts)
                    end
                end

                task.wait(0.5)
                isExecuting = false
            end)
        end

        --------------------------------------------------------------------------------
        -- Character Event Connection
        --------------------------------------------------------------------------------

        local function setupCharacter(char)
            if not _G.LethalTechEnabled or not char then return end

            local humanoid = char:WaitForChild("Humanoid")
            local animConn = humanoid.AnimationPlayed:Connect(function(track)
                if not _G.LethalTechEnabled or isExecuting then return end

                local animId = string.match(track.Animation.AnimationId or "", "%d+")
                if animId and TARGET_ANIMATIONS[animId] then
                    executeLethalTech()
                end
            end)
            table.insert(_G.LethalTechConns, animConn)
        end

        if player.Character then
            setupCharacter(player.Character)
        end

        local charAddedConn = player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
            task.wait(0.1)
            setupCharacter(char)
        end)
        table.insert(_G.LethalTechConns, charAddedConn)
    end,
})


garouSection:Toggle({
    Title = "Flowing + Grasp",
    Value = false,
    Callback = function(isEnabled)
        -- Clear previous active frame loops
        if _G.FlowGraspConn then
            _G.FlowGraspConn:Disconnect()
            _G.FlowGraspConn = nil
        end

        if isEnabled then
            local Players = game:GetService("Players")
            local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
            local TweenService = game:GetService("TweenService")
            local player = Players.LocalPlayer

            -- Runtime State Tracker Flags
            local isTweening = false
            local lastPlaying = false

            _G.FlowGraspConn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
                if not _G.FlowGraspConn then return end

                local char = player.Character
                local humanoid = char and char:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
                local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if not humanoid or not hrp then return end

                -- Detect target combat sequence activation track
                local isPlaying = false
                for _, track in ipairs(humanoid:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()) do
                    if track.Animation and track.Animation.AnimationId == "rbxassetid://12273188754" then 
                        isPlaying = true 
                        break 
                    end
                end

                if isPlaying and not isTweening and not lastPlaying then
                    isTweening = true 
                    lastPlaying = true
                    
                    task.delay(1.8, function()
                        -- Recalculate runtime state checks before launching movement
                        if not player.Character or not player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then 
                            isTweening = false 
                            return 
                        end
                        
                        local currentHrp = player.Character.HumanoidRootPart
                        local fwdCF = currentHrp.CFrame + currentHrp.CFrame.LookVector * 24
                        
                        local tween = TweenService:Create(currentHrp, TweenInfo.new(0.1, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {CFrame = fwdCF})
                        tween:Play() 
                        tween.Completed:Wait()

                        -- Handle tool verification and packet sequence firing
                        local tool = player.Backpack:FindFirstChild("Hunter's Grasp") or (player.Character and player.Character:FindFirstChild("Hunter's Grasp"))
                        local remote = player.Character and player.Character:FindFirstChild("Communicate")
                        
                        if tool and remote then 
                            remote:FireServer({["Tool"] = tool, ["Goal"] = "Console Move"}) 
                        end
                        isTweening = false
                    end)
                elseif not isPlaying then 
                    lastPlaying = false 
                end
            end)
        end
    end,
})


garouSection:Toggle({
    Title = "Whirlwind Dunk Auto-Teleport",
    Value = false,
    Callback = function(isEnabled)
        if _G.TeleportAnimConnection then 
            _G.TeleportAnimConnection:Disconnect() 
            _G.TeleportAnimConnection = nil 
        end
        
        if isEnabled then
            local isTeleporting = false
            local lastTrack = nil
            
            _G.TeleportAnimConnection = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
                local character = LocalPlayer.Character
                if not character or isTeleporting then return end
                
                local hum = character:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Humanoid")
                local root = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if not hum or not root then return end
                
                for _, track in ipairs(hum:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()) do
                    if track.Animation and track.Animation.AnimationId == "rbxassetid://12296113986" then
                        if lastTrack == track then return end
                        lastTrack = track
                        isTeleporting = true
                        
                        task.delay(1, function()
                            local enemy = GetClosestEnemy()
                            if enemy and enemy.Character then
                                local enemyRoot = enemy.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                                if enemyRoot and root and root.Parent then
                                    root.CFrame = enemyRoot.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 60, 0)
                                end
                            else
                                if root and root.Parent then
                                    root.CFrame = root.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 60, 0)
                                end
                            end
                            isTeleporting = false
                        end)
                        break
                    end
                end
                
                if lastTrack and not lastTrack.IsPlaying then 
                    lastTrack = nil 
                end
            end)
        end
    end,
})

garouSection:Toggle({
    Title = "hunter slam",
    Desc = "make your hunter grasp finisher slam the player ar the last",
    Value = false,
    Callback = function(isEnabled)
        -- Clear prior character connections/cleanup state
        if _G.EmoteDashCharConn then
            _G.EmoteDashCharConn:Disconnect()
            _G.EmoteDashCharConn = nil
        end

        -- Global control reference flag
        _G.EmoteDashEnabled = isEnabled

        if isEnabled then
            local Players = game:GetService("Players")
            local Workspace = game:GetService("Workspace")

            local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
            local Camera = Workspace.CurrentCamera

            local TARGET_ANIMATION_ID = "12447247483"

            local isActionActive = false
            local loadedAnims = {}

            local function getCharacterAndHumanoid()
                local Character = LocalPlayer.Character
                local Humanoid = Character and Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                return Character, Humanoid
            end

            local function stopAllAnimations()
                local _, humanoid = getCharacterAndHumanoid()
                local animator = humanoid and humanoid:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator")
                if animator then
                    pcall(function()
                        for _, track in ipairs(animator:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()) do
                            track:Stop()
                        end
                    end)
                end
            end

            local function playAnimation(animationId)
                local _, humanoid = getCharacterAndHumanoid()
                if humanoid then
                    local animator = humanoid:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator") or Instance.new("Animator", humanoid)
                    local animation = loadedAnims[animationId] or Instance.new("Animation")
                    if not loadedAnims[animationId] then
                        animation.AnimationId = "rbxassetid://" .. tostring(animationId)
                        loadedAnims[animationId] = animation
                    end
                    local success, track = pcall(function() return animator:LoadAnimation(animation) end)
                    if success and track then
                        track.Priority = Enum.AnimationPriority.Action
                        track:Play()
                        pcall(function() track:AdjustSpeed(1.2) end)
                    end
                end
            end

            local function turnCamera(degrees)
                if not isActionActive then
                    isActionActive = true
                    local character, _ = getCharacterAndHumanoid()
                    local hrp = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    if hrp then
                        pcall(function()
                            hrp.CFrame = hrp.CFrame * CFrame.Angles(0, math.rad(degrees), 0)
                            local cameraPosition = Camera and Camera.CFrame.Position or hrp.Position + Vector3.new(0, 5, 0)
                            local cameraY = cameraPosition.Y
                            local offsetVector = cameraPosition - hrp.Position
                            local horizontalOffset = Vector3.new(offsetVector.X, 0, offsetVector.Z)
                            local rotatedOffset = CFrame.fromAxisAngle(Vector3.yAxis, math.rad(degrees)) * horizontalOffset
                            local newCameraLookAt = hrp.Position + rotatedOffset
                            if Camera and Camera:IsA("Camera") then
                                Camera.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(Vector3.new(newCameraLookAt.X, cameraY, newCameraLookAt.Z), hrp.Position)
                            end
                        end)
                    end
                    isActionActive = false
                end
            end

            local function applyBodyVelocity(directionX, distanceMultiplier, verticalForce, maxSpeed, duration)
                if not isActionActive then
                    isActionActive = true
                    local character, _ = getCharacterAndHumanoid()
                    local hrp = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    if hrp then
                        local bodyVelocity = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
                        local velocityVector = hrp.CFrame.RightVector * directionX * distanceMultiplier + Vector3.new(0, verticalForce, 0)
                        if velocityVector.Magnitude == 0 then velocityVector = Vector3.new(0, 1, 0) end
                        bodyVelocity.Velocity = velocityVector.Unit * maxSpeed
                        bodyVelocity.MaxForce = Vector3.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge)
                        bodyVelocity.Parent = hrp
                        task.delay(duration, function() pcall(function() bodyVelocity:Destroy() end) end)
                    end
                    isActionActive = false
                end
            end

            local function EMOTE_DASH_FUNCTION()
                if not _G.EmoteDashEnabled then return end
                stopAllAnimations()
                pcall(function() playAnimation(10480793962) end)
                pcall(function() turnCamera(90) end)
                pcall(function() applyBodyVelocity(1, 38, 8, 95, 0.27) end)
            end

            local function setupAnimationListener(character)
                if not character then return end
                local humanoid = character:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 5)
                local animator = humanoid and humanoid:WaitForChild("Animator", 5)
                
                if animator then
                    animator.AnimationPlayed:Connect(function(animationTrack)
                        if not _G.EmoteDashEnabled then return end
                        local animId = animationTrack.Animation and animationTrack.Animation.AnimationId
                        if animId and (animId:find(TARGET_ANIMATION_ID) or animId == "rbxassetid://" .. TARGET_ANIMATION_ID) then
                            EMOTE_DASH_FUNCTION()
                        end
                    end)
                end
            end

            if LocalPlayer.Character then
                setupAnimationListener(LocalPlayer.Character)
            end
            
            _G.EmoteDashCharConn = LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(setupAnimationListener)
        end
    end,
})


garouSection:Toggle({
    Title = "Surf Tech Auto-Boost",
    Value = false,
    Callback = function(isEnabled)
        if _G.SurfTechConnection then 
            _G.SurfTechConnection:Disconnect() 
            _G.SurfTechConnection = nil 
        end
        
        if isEnabled then
            local tweenService = game:GetService("TweenService")
            local targetAnimId = "rbxassetid://12309835105"
            
            local function listenToCharacter(character)
                local humanoid = character:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 10)
                if not humanoid then return end
                
                local animTrackConnection
                animTrackConnection = humanoid.AnimationPlayed:Connect(function(track)
                    if not _G.SurfTechConnection then 
                        if animTrackConnection then animTrackConnection:Disconnect() end
                        return 
                    end
                    
                    if track.Animation and track.Animation.AnimationId == targetAnimId then
                        task.wait(1.1)
                        
                        local rootPart = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                        if rootPart then
                            local tweenInfo = TweenInfo.new(0.1, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
                            local targetCFrame = rootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 2, -10)
                            
                            tweenService:Create(rootPart, tweenInfo, {CFrame = targetCFrame}):Play()
                        end
                    end
                end)
            end

            if LocalPlayer.Character then
                listenToCharacter(LocalPlayer.Character)
            end
            
            _G.SurfTechConnection = LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(listenToCharacter)
        end
    end,
})

garouSection:Toggle({
    Title = "Kiba Lethal",
    Value = false,
    Callback = function(isEnabled)
        -- Clear previous active connection wrappers
        if _G.LethalDashHeartbeat then
            _G.LethalDashHeartbeat:Disconnect()
            _G.LethalDashHeartbeat = nil
        end
        if _G.LethalDashPartCleanup then
            pcall(function() _G.LethalDashPartCleanup:Destroy() end)
            _G.LethalDashPartCleanup = nil
        end

        if isEnabled then
            local Players = game:GetService("Players")
            local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
            local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer

            -- Configuration Options Matrix
            local SETTINGS = {
                AnimID = "rbxassetid://12296113986",
                DashAnimID = "rbxassetid://10479335397",
                DetectRadius = 20,
                EarlyFire = 0.365
            }

            -- Dynamic Core Tracker Variables
            local isTrackingAction = false
            local animationStarted = false
            local actionFired = false

            -- Initialize Base Range Visual Part
            local rangeDetectorPart = Instance.new("Part")
            rangeDetectorPart.Size = Vector3.new(SETTINGS.DetectRadius * 2, 0.1, SETTINGS.DetectRadius * 2)
            rangeDetectorPart.CanCollide = false
            rangeDetectorPart.Anchored = true
            rangeDetectorPart.CastShadow = false
            rangeDetectorPart.Transparency = 1
            rangeDetectorPart.Material = Enum.Material.Air
            rangeDetectorPart.Parent = workspace
            _G.LethalDashPartCleanup = rangeDetectorPart

            -- Core Utility: Safely fetch standard BaseParts from targeting characters
            local function getPrimaryBasePart(model)
                return model:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") 
                    or model:FindFirstChild("Torso") 
                    or model:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso") 
                    or model:FindFirstChildOfClass("BasePart")
            end

            -- Target Identifier Engine (Detect players & models in operational bounds)
            local function findValidCombatTarget(myRoot)
                local currentTarget = nil
                local currentMinDistance = SETTINGS.DetectRadius

                -- Check Player Characters
                for _, targetPlayer in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                    if targetPlayer ~= LocalPlayer and targetPlayer.Character then
                        local tRoot = getPrimaryBasePart(targetPlayer.Character)
                        local tHumanoid = targetPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                        if tRoot and tHumanoid and tHumanoid.Health > 0 then
                            local distance = (tRoot.Position - myRoot.Position).Magnitude
                            if distance < currentMinDistance then
                                currentMinDistance = distance
                                currentTarget = targetPlayer.Character
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end

                -- Check Workspace Non-Player Dummies
                for _, obj in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
                    if obj:IsA("Model") and obj ~= LocalPlayer.Character and not Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(obj) then
                        local tRoot = getPrimaryBasePart(obj)
                        local tHumanoid = obj:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                        if tRoot and tHumanoid and tHumanoid.Health > 0 then
                            local distance = (tRoot.Position - myRoot.Position).Magnitude
                            if distance < currentMinDistance then
                                currentMinDistance = distance
                                currentTarget = obj
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end

                return currentTarget
            end

            -- Math Matrix Position Processor
            local function calculateTargetCFrame(targetRoot)
                return CFrame.new(targetRoot.Position - Vector3.new(0, 3, 0)) * CFrame.Angles(math.rad(90), 0, 0)
            end

            -- Force Position Assembly Setter
            local function forceAnchorFrame(myRoot, targetRoot)
                local computedCFrame = calculateTargetCFrame(targetRoot)
                for _ = 1, 6 do
                    myRoot.CFrame = computedCFrame
                    myRoot.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                    myRoot.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
                end
            end

            -- Sequence Processing Thread Logic
            local function executeLethalSequence(myHumanoid, myRoot, targetChar, targetRoot)
                local savedAutoRotate = myHumanoid.AutoRotate
                myHumanoid.AutoRotate = false
                myHumanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics)

                -- Sequence Trigger Phase
                forceAnchorFrame(myRoot, targetRoot)
                local character = LocalPlayer.Character
                if character and character:FindFirstChild("Communicate") then
                    character.Communicate:FireServer({
                        Dash = Enum.KeyCode.W,
                        Key = Enum.KeyCode.Q,
                        Goal = "KeyPress"
                    })
                end
                forceAnchorFrame(myRoot, targetRoot)

                local dashStarted = false
                local durationElapsed = 0
                local heartbeatConnection

                heartbeatConnection = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
                    if not _G.LethalDashHeartbeat then
                        heartbeatConnection:Disconnect()
                        return
                    end

                    durationElapsed = durationElapsed + dt
                    local animator = myHumanoid:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator")
                    local dashAnimPlaying = false

                    if animator then
                        for _, track in ipairs(animator:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()) do
                            if track.Animation and track.Animation.AnimationId == SETTINGS.DashAnimID then
                                dashAnimPlaying = true
                                dashStarted = true
                                break
                            end
                        end
                    end

                    -- Keep aligning positions to moving targets
                    local actualTargetRoot = getPrimaryBasePart(targetChar)
                    if actualTargetRoot then
                        for _ = 1, 3 do
                            myRoot.CFrame = calculateTargetCFrame(actualTargetRoot)
                            myRoot.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                            myRoot.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
                        end
                    end

                    -- Check completion boundary conditions
                    if (dashStarted and not dashAnimPlaying) or durationElapsed >= 0.75 then
                        heartbeatConnection:Disconnect()
                        myHumanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running)
                        myHumanoid.AutoRotate = savedAutoRotate
                        isTrackingAction = false
                        actionFired = false
                        animationStarted = false
                    end
                end)
            end

            -- Continuous State Evaluation Tick Loop
            _G.LethalDashHeartbeat = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
                if not _G.LethalDashHeartbeat then return end
                if isTrackingAction then return end

                local character = LocalPlayer.Character
                if not character then return end
                
                local myRoot = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                local myHumanoid = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                if not myRoot or not myHumanoid then return end

                local animator = myHumanoid:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator")
                if not animator then return end

                -- Sync spatial orientation reference block part
                rangeDetectorPart.CFrame = CFrame.new(myRoot.Position.X, myRoot.Position.Y - 2, myRoot.Position.Z)

                local targetTrack = nil
                for _, track in ipairs(animator:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()) do
                    if track.Animation and track.Animation.AnimationId == SETTINGS.AnimID then
                        targetTrack = track
                        break
                    end
                end

                if targetTrack then
                    animationStarted = true
                    actionFired = false
                    if targetTrack.Length > 0 then
                        local timeRemaining = targetTrack.Length - targetTrack.TimePosition
                        if timeRemaining <= SETTINGS.EarlyFire then
                            actionFired = true
                            animationStarted = false
                            
                            local targetChar = findValidCombatTarget(myRoot)
                            if not targetChar then actionFired = false return end
                            
                            local targetRoot = getPrimaryBasePart(targetChar)
                            if not targetRoot then actionFired = false return end

                            isTrackingAction = true
                            executeLethalSequence(myHumanoid, myRoot, targetChar, targetRoot)
                        end
                    end
                else
                    if animationStarted and not actionFired then
                        actionFired = true
                        animationStarted = false
                        
                        local targetChar = findValidCombatTarget(myRoot)
                        if not targetChar then actionFired = false return end
                        
                        local targetRoot = getPrimaryBasePart(targetChar)
                        if not targetRoot then actionFired = false return end

                        isTrackingAction = true
                        executeLethalSequence(myHumanoid, myRoot, targetChar, targetRoot)
                    elseif not animationStarted then
                        actionFired = false
                    end
                end
            end)
        else
            -- Process Safe Controls Variable Reset 
            pcall(function()
                local character = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer.Character
                local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                if humanoid then
                    humanoid.PlatformStand = false
                    humanoid.AutoRotate = true
                end
            end)
        end
    end,
})


garouSection:Toggle({
    Title = "Upper + Grasp",
    Value = false,
    Callback = function(isEnabled)
        -- Clear previous connections
        if _G.UpperGraspRenderConn then
            _G.UpperGraspRenderConn:Disconnect()
            _G.UpperGraspRenderConn = nil
        end

        if isEnabled then
            local TweenService = game:GetService("TweenService")
            local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
            local Players = game:GetService("Players")
            local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer

            -- Runtime State Tracker Flags
            local isTweening = false
            local lastPlaying = false
            local cooldown = false

            _G.UpperGraspRenderConn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
                if not _G.UpperGraspRenderConn then return end

                local character = LocalPlayer.Character
                local hrp = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
                if not character or not hrp or not humanoid then return end

                -- Detect target combat sequence activation track
                local isPlaying = false
                for _, track in ipairs(humanoid:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()) do
                    if track.Animation and track.Animation.AnimationId == "rbxassetid://10503381238" then
                        isPlaying = true
                        break
                    end
                end

                if isPlaying and not isTweening and not lastPlaying and not cooldown then
                    isTweening = true
                    lastPlaying = true
                    cooldown = true

                    task.delay(0.18, function()
                        local target = nil
                        local shortest = 7
                        local live = workspace:FindFirstChild("Live")
                        
                        if live then
                            for _, model in ipairs(live:GetChildren()) do
                                if model:IsA("Model") and model ~= character then
                                    local torso = model:FindFirstChild("Torso") or model:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso")
                                    if torso then
                                        local dist = (hrp.Position - torso.Position).Magnitude
                                        if dist <= shortest then
                                            shortest = dist
                                            target = torso
                                        end
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        end

                        -- Execute calculated teleports on execution alignment
                        if target then
                            local targetPos = target.Position + Vector3.new(0, 8, 0)
                            TweenService:Create(hrp, TweenInfo.new(0.1, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear), {
                                CFrame = CFrame.new(targetPos)
                            }):Play()
                        end

                        -- Network fire tools handling payload packets
                        local tool = LocalPlayer.Backpack:FindFirstChild("Hunter's Grasp")
                        local remote = character and character:FindFirstChild("Communicate")
                        if tool and remote then
                            remote:FireServer({["Tool"] = tool, ["Goal"] = "Console Move"})
                        end

                        isTweening = false
                        task.delay(15, function() 
                            cooldown = false 
                        end)
                    end)
                elseif not isPlaying then
                    lastPlaying = false
                end
            end)
        end
    end,
})


-- ==========================================
-- MONSTER GAROU TAB
-- ==========================================

monstergarouTab:Toggle({
    Title = "Binding Lix",
    Value = false,
    Callback = function(isEnabled)
        --// Clean up existing connections
        if _G.FreezeTechMainConnection then
            _G.FreezeTechMainConnection:Disconnect()
            _G.FreezeTechMainConnection = nil
        end
        if _G.FreezeTechAnimConnection then
            _G.FreezeTechAnimConnection:Disconnect()
            _G.FreezeTechAnimConnection = nil
        end
        if _G.FreezeTechRenderConnection then
            _G.FreezeTechRenderConnection:Disconnect()
            _G.FreezeTechRenderConnection = nil
        end

        if isEnabled then
            local Players = game:GetService("Players")
            local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
            local VirtualInputManager = game:GetService("VirtualInputManager")
            local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer

            --// CONFIGURATION
            local DASH_DELAY = 0.236
            local FREEZE_DELAY = 0.2
            local FREEZE_DURATION = 0.67
            local COOLDOWN_TIME = 5
            local DISABLE_DURATION = 5
            local TILT_ANGLE = 0

            local TARGET_ANIMATION = "95034083206292"
            local DISABLE_ANIMATION = "10479335397"

            --// STATE TRACKING
            local inCooldown = false
            local disabledTimer = false

            --// FREEZE MECHANIC
            local function performFreeze(char, duration)
                local humanoid = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                local root = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if not humanoid or not root then return end

                local freezePos = root.Position
                local freezeLook = root.CFrame.LookVector
                local start = tick()

                humanoid.AutoRotate = false
                humanoid.PlatformStand = true

                local bodyGyro = Instance.new("BodyGyro")
                bodyGyro.Parent = root
                bodyGyro.MaxTorque = Vector3.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge)
                bodyGyro.P = 50000
                bodyGyro.D = 1000

                local bodyPos = Instance.new("BodyPosition")
                bodyPos.Parent = root
                bodyPos.MaxForce = Vector3.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge)
                bodyPos.P = 50000
                bodyPos.D = 1000
                bodyPos.Position = freezePos

                if _G.FreezeTechRenderConnection then 
                    _G.FreezeTechRenderConnection:Disconnect() 
                end

                _G.FreezeTechRenderConnection = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
                    if not char.Parent then
                        if _G.FreezeTechRenderConnection then _G.FreezeTechRenderConnection:Disconnect() end
                        bodyGyro:Destroy()
                        bodyPos:Destroy()
                        return
                    end

                    if tick() - start >= duration then
                        if _G.FreezeTechRenderConnection then _G.FreezeTechRenderConnection:Disconnect() end
                        bodyGyro:Destroy()
                        bodyPos:Destroy()
                        humanoid.PlatformStand = false
                        humanoid.AutoRotate = true
                        return
                    end

                    bodyPos.Position = freezePos

                    local targetCFrame = CFrame.lookAt(freezePos, freezePos + freezeLook) 
                        * CFrame.Angles(math.rad(35), 0, math.rad(TILT_ANGLE))

                    bodyGyro.CFrame = targetCFrame

                    root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                    root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero

                    humanoid.PlatformStand = true
                end)
            end

            --// ANIMATION LISTENER
            local function listenToCharacter(char)
                local humanoid = char:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 10)
                if not humanoid then return end
                local animator = humanoid:WaitForChild("Animator", 10)
                if not animator then return end

                if _G.FreezeTechAnimConnection then 
                    _G.FreezeTechAnimConnection:Disconnect() 
                end

                _G.FreezeTechAnimConnection = animator.AnimationPlayed:Connect(function(track)
                    local anim = track.Animation
                    if not anim then return end

                    local animId = tostring(anim.AnimationId)

                    -- Disable Sequence Detection
                    if string.find(animId, DISABLE_ANIMATION) then
                        if not disabledTimer then
                            disabledTimer = true
                            task.delay(DISABLE_DURATION, function()
                                disabledTimer = false
                            end)
                        end
                        return
                    end

                    -- Target Sequence Detection
                    if string.find(animId, TARGET_ANIMATION) then
                        if inCooldown or disabledTimer then return end

                        -- Wait for track finish
                        track.Stopped:Wait()

                        inCooldown = true

                        task.wait(DASH_DELAY)
                        if not char.Parent then
                            inCooldown = false
                            return
                        end

                        -- Trigger Q Dash
                        VirtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(true, Enum.KeyCode.Q, false, game)
                        task.wait(0.03)
                        VirtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(false, Enum.KeyCode.Q, false, game)

                        task.wait(FREEZE_DELAY)
                        if char.Parent then
                            performFreeze(char, FREEZE_DURATION)
                        end

                        task.delay(COOLDOWN_TIME, function()
                            inCooldown = false
                        end)
                    end
                end)
            end

            -- Attach listener to current character
            if LocalPlayer.Character then
                listenToCharacter(LocalPlayer.Character)
            end

            -- Attach listener on respawn
            _G.FreezeTechMainConnection = LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(listenToCharacter)

        else
            --// Reset character state on disable
            pcall(function()
                local character = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer.Character
                local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                if humanoid then
                    humanoid.PlatformStand = false
                    humanoid.AutoRotate = true
                end
            end)
        end
    end,
})

monstergarouTab:Toggle({
    Title = "Binding Supa",
    Value = false,
    Callback = function(isEnabled)
        --// Clean up active connections
        if _G.MiniSupaMainConnection then
            _G.MiniSupaMainConnection:Disconnect()
            _G.MiniSupaMainConnection = nil
        end
        if _G.MiniSupaAnimConnection then
            _G.MiniSupaAnimConnection:Disconnect()
            _G.MiniSupaAnimConnection = nil
        end
        if _G.MiniSupaFollowConnection then
            _G.MiniSupaFollowConnection:Disconnect()
            _G.MiniSupaFollowConnection = nil
        end
        if _G.MiniSupaFreezeConnection then
            _G.MiniSupaFreezeConnection:Disconnect()
            _G.MiniSupaFreezeConnection = nil
        end

        if isEnabled then
            local Players = game:GetService("Players")
            local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
            local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer

            --// CONFIGURATION
            local CONFIG = {
                DASH_DURATION = 0.15,
                FOLLOW_OFFSET = 2.5,
                ANGLE_TILT = math.rad(55),
                STICK_RANGE = 18,
                COOLDOWN_TIME = 4,
                ANIMATION_IDS = {"10503381238", "13379003796", "95034083206292"}
            }

            local inCooldown = false

            --// HELPER FUNCTIONS
            local function getClosestTarget(myRoot)
                local targetModel = nil
                local shortestDist = CONFIG.STICK_RANGE

                for _, obj in pairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
                    if obj:IsA("Model") and obj ~= LocalPlayer.Character then
                        local hrp = obj:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                        if hrp then
                            local success, dist = pcall(function() 
                                return (myRoot.Position - hrp.Position).Magnitude 
                            end)
                            if success and dist and dist < shortestDist then
                                targetModel = obj
                                shortestDist = dist
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
                return targetModel
            end

            local function fireCommunicateRemotes(char)
                pcall(function()
                    local data = {{Dash = Enum.KeyCode.W, Key = Enum.KeyCode.Q, Goal = "KeyPress"}}
                    local comm = char:FindFirstChild("Communicate")
                    if comm then
                        comm:FireServer(unpack(data))
                    end
                end)

                local function findNilInstance(name, className)
                    for _, inst in pairs(getnilinstances()) do
                        if inst.ClassName == className and inst.Name == name then
                            return inst
                        end
                    end
                    return nil
                end

                pcall(function()
                    local data = {{Goal = "delete bv", BV = findNilInstance("moveme", "BodyVelocity")}}
                    local comm = char:FindFirstChild("Communicate")
                    if comm then
                        comm:FireServer(unpack(data))
                    end
                end)
            end

            local activeBillboard = nil
            local function showCooldownGui(charModel, duration)
                if activeBillboard then activeBillboard:Destroy() activeBillboard = nil end
                local head = charModel:FindFirstChild("Head") or charModel:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if not head then return end

                local gui = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
                gui.Name = "TempCooldown"
                gui.Size = UDim2.new(0, 60, 0, 12)
                gui.Adornee = head
                gui.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 2.2, 0)
                gui.AlwaysOnTop = true
                gui.Parent = charModel

                local bg = Instance.new("Frame")
                bg.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
                bg.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(20, 20, 30)
                bg.BackgroundTransparency = 0.4
                bg.BorderSizePixel = 0
                bg.Parent = gui

                local corner1 = Instance.new("UICorner", bg)
                corner1.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 3)

                local fill = Instance.new("Frame")
                fill.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
                fill.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 70, 70)
                fill.BorderSizePixel = 0
                fill.Parent = bg

                local corner2 = Instance.new("UICorner", fill)
                corner2.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 3)

                local label = Instance.new("TextLabel")
                label.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
                label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                label.Text = tostring(duration)
                label.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
                label.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
                label.TextSize = 9
                label.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.3
                label.Parent = bg

                activeBillboard = gui
                local startTime = tick()
                while tick() - startTime < duration do
                    if not gui.Parent then break end
                    local elapsed = tick() - startTime
                    local remaining = duration - elapsed
                    fill.Size = UDim2.new(remaining / duration, 0, 1, 0)
                    label.Text = tostring(math.ceil(remaining))
                    task.wait(0.05)
                end

                if gui then gui:Destroy() end
                if activeBillboard == gui then activeBillboard = nil end
            end

            --// MAIN TECH LOGIC
            local function performStickDash(char)
                local humanoid = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if not humanoid or not hrp then return end

                local targetModel = getClosestTarget(hrp)
                if not targetModel then return end
                local targetHRP = targetModel:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if not targetHRP then return end

                local oldProps = {
                    WalkSpeed = humanoid.WalkSpeed,
                    JumpPower = humanoid.JumpPower,
                    PlatformStand = humanoid.PlatformStand,
                    AutoRotate = humanoid.AutoRotate
                }

                if _G.MiniSupaFreezeConnection then _G.MiniSupaFreezeConnection:Disconnect() end
                _G.MiniSupaFreezeConnection = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
                    if hrp and hrp.Parent then
                        hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                        hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
                    end
