                    if humanoid and humanoid.Parent then humanoid.WalkSpeed = 0 end
                end)

                fireCommunicateRemotes(char)
                task.wait(0.2)

                pcall(function() humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics) end)
                if hrp then hrp.CFrame = hrp.CFrame * CFrame.Angles(CONFIG.ANGLE_TILT, 0, 0) end

                local dashStart = tick()
                if _G.MiniSupaFollowConnection then _G.MiniSupaFollowConnection:Disconnect() end
                _G.MiniSupaFollowConnection = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
                    if not hrp or not hrp.Parent or not targetHRP or not targetHRP.Parent then
                        if _G.MiniSupaFollowConnection then _G.MiniSupaFollowConnection:Disconnect() end
                        return
                    end

                    if tick() - dashStart < CONFIG.DASH_DURATION then
                        local unitVector = (targetHRP.Position - hrp.Position).Unit
                        local targetPos = targetHRP.Position - unitVector * CONFIG.FOLLOW_OFFSET
                        hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(targetPos) * CFrame.Angles(CONFIG.ANGLE_TILT, 0, 0)
                    end
                end)

                task.wait(CONFIG.DASH_DURATION)

                if _G.MiniSupaFreezeConnection then _G.MiniSupaFreezeConnection:Disconnect() _G.MiniSupaFreezeConnection = nil end
                if _G.MiniSupaFollowConnection then _G.MiniSupaFollowConnection:Disconnect() _G.MiniSupaFollowConnection = nil end

                pcall(function()
                    if humanoid and humanoid.Parent then
                        humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp)
                        humanoid.WalkSpeed = oldProps.WalkSpeed
                        humanoid.JumpPower = oldProps.JumpPower
                        humanoid.PlatformStand = oldProps.PlatformStand
                        humanoid.AutoRotate = oldProps.AutoRotate
                    end
                end)
            end

            --// ANIMATION LISTENER
            local function onAnimationPlayed(track)
                local anim = track and track.Animation
                if not anim then return end

                local animId = tostring(anim.AnimationId or "")
                for _, id in ipairs(CONFIG.ANIMATION_IDS) do
                    if string.find(animId, id, 1, true) then
                        task.spawn(function()
                            if track.IsPlaying then
                                track.Stopped:Wait()
                            end

                            if not inCooldown then
                                inCooldown = true
                                local char = LocalPlayer.Character
                                if char then
                                    task.spawn(function() showCooldownGui(char, CONFIG.COOLDOWN_TIME) end)
                                    task.spawn(function() performStickDash(char) end)
                                end

                                task.wait(CONFIG.COOLDOWN_TIME)
                                inCooldown = false
                            end
                        end)
                        return
                    end
                end
            end

            local function listenToCharacter(char)
                local humanoid = char:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 10)
                if not humanoid then return end

                if _G.MiniSupaAnimConnection then _G.MiniSupaAnimConnection:Disconnect() end
                _G.MiniSupaAnimConnection = humanoid.AnimationPlayed:Connect(onAnimationPlayed)

                local animator = humanoid:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator")
                if animator then
                    animator.AnimationPlayed:Connect(onAnimationPlayed)
                end
            end

            if LocalPlayer.Character then
                listenToCharacter(LocalPlayer.Character)
            end

            _G.MiniSupaMainConnection = LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(listenToCharacter)

        else
            --// Reset humanoid state on toggle turn off
            pcall(function()
                local char = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer.Character
                local humanoid = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                if humanoid then
                    humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp)
                    humanoid.AutoRotate = true
                end
            end)
        end
    end,
})


-- ==========================================
-- FAST TO GET COSMIC GAROU
-- ==========================================

FastcosmicSection:Toggle({
    Title = "Stick Hunters Mark",
    Description = "This stick hunters mark sticks to the enemy if you use it",
    Value = false,
    Callback = function(isEnabled)
        --// Clean up active connections and state
        if _G.StickHuntersMarkMainConnection then
            _G.StickHuntersMarkMainConnection:Disconnect()
            _G.StickHuntersMarkMainConnection = nil
        end
        if _G.StickHuntersMarkAnimConnection then
            _G.StickHuntersMarkAnimConnection:Disconnect()
            _G.StickHuntersMarkAnimConnection = nil
        end

        _G.StickHuntersMarkActive = isEnabled
        _G.StickHuntersMarkIsSticking = false

        if isEnabled then
            local Players = game:GetService("Players")
            local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
            local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer

            -- Target Hunters Mark Animation ID
            local TARGET_ANIM_ID = "rbxassetid://85025226664507"

            --// TARGET FINDER
            local function getTarget()
                for _, plr in pairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                    if plr ~= LocalPlayer and plr.Character then
                        local hum = plr.Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
                        local hrp = plr.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                        if hum and hum.Health > 0 and hrp then
                            return plr.Character
                        end
                    end
                end

                for _, obj in pairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
                    if obj:IsA("Model") and obj ~= LocalPlayer.Character then
                        local isPlayer = Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(obj)
                        if not isPlayer then
                            local hum = obj:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
                            local hrp = obj:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                            if hum and hum.Health > 0 and hrp then
                                return obj
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end

                return nil
            end

            --// ANIMATION LISTENER
            local function setupAnimListener(char)
                local hum = char:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 5)
                if not hum then return end
                local animator = hum:WaitForChild("Animator", 5)
                if not animator then return end

                if _G.StickHuntersMarkAnimConnection then
                    _G.StickHuntersMarkAnimConnection:Disconnect()
                end

                _G.StickHuntersMarkAnimConnection = animator.AnimationPlayed:Connect(function(track)
                    if track.Animation and track.Animation.AnimationId == TARGET_ANIM_ID then
                        if _G.StickHuntersMarkActive and not _G.StickHuntersMarkIsSticking then
                            _G.StickHuntersMarkIsSticking = true
                            task.delay(2, function()
                                _G.StickHuntersMarkIsSticking = false
                            end)
                        end
                    end
                end)
            end

            --// MAIN STICK LOOP
            task.spawn(function()
                while _G.StickHuntersMarkActive do
                    RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
                    pcall(function()
                        if not _G.StickHuntersMarkIsSticking then return end

                        local target = getTarget()
                        if target then
                            local targetHum = target:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
                            local targetHRP = target:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

                            if targetHum and targetHum.Health > 0 and targetHRP then
                                repeat
                                    RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
                                    if not _G.StickHuntersMarkIsSticking or not _G.StickHuntersMarkActive then break end

                                    local char = LocalPlayer.Character
                                    if char then
                                        local myHRP = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                                        if myHRP then
                                            myHRP.CFrame = targetHRP.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0, 3)
                                        end
                                    end
                                until not targetHum or targetHum.Health <= 0 or not _G.StickHuntersMarkActive or getTarget() ~= target or not _G.StickHuntersMarkIsSticking
                            end
                        end
                    end)
                end
            end)

            if LocalPlayer.Character then
                setupAnimListener(LocalPlayer.Character)
            end

            _G.StickHuntersMarkMainConnection = LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(setupAnimListener)
        end
    end,
})

do
    -- Auto Godslayer TP
    local _autoGodslayerActive = false
    local _autoGodslayerConn   = nil
    local _autoGodslayerTarget = nil
    local GODSLAYER_ANIM_ID    = "107484339495811"

    local function _getMarkedPlayers()
        local marked = {}
        local live = workspace:FindFirstChild("Live")
        if not live then return marked end
        local myChar = lp.Character
        for _, slot in pairs(live:GetChildren()) do
            if slot ~= myChar and slot:FindFirstChild("Ok") then
                table.insert(marked, slot)
            end
        end
        return marked
    end

local _autoGodslayerActive = false
local _autoGodslayerConn   = nil
local _autoGodslayerTarget = nil
local GODSLAYER_ANIM_ID    = "107484339495811"
local _hasTeleported       = false -- Debounce flag to prevent per-frame spam

local function _getMarkedPlayers()
    local marked = {}
    local live = workspace:FindFirstChild("Live")
    if not live then return marked end
    local myChar = player.Character
    for _, slot in pairs(live:GetChildren()) do
        if slot ~= myChar and slot:FindFirstChild("Ok") then
            table.insert(marked, slot)
        end
    end
    return marked
end

mainTab:Toggle({
    Title = "Auto Cosmic Garou",
    Desc = "Teleports to marked targets when Godslayer animation plays",
    Flag = "AutoGodslayerTPFlag",
    Value = _autoGodslayerActive,
    Callback = function(val)
        _autoGodslayerActive = val

        if _autoGodslayerConn then 
            _autoGodslayerConn:Disconnect() 
            _autoGodslayerConn = nil 
        end

        if not val then
            _autoGodslayerTarget = nil
            _hasTeleported = false
            return
        end

        _autoGodslayerConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
            local c = player.Character
            if not c then return end
            if c:GetAttribute("AbsoluteImmortal") then return end
            
            local hum = c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if not hum or hum.Health <= 0 then return end
            
            local animator = hum:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator")
            if not animator then return end

            local isPlayingGodslayer = false

            for _, track in pairs(animator:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()) do
                local animId = track.Animation and tostring(track.Animation.AnimationId) or ""
                
                -- Check if ID contains the Godslayer asset string
                if animId:find(GODSLAYER_ANIM_ID) then
                    isPlayingGodslayer = true
                    
                    if track.TimePosition >= 0.30 and not _hasTeleported then
                        _hasTeleported = true -- Mark as executed for this move cycle
                        
                        -- Verify current target is still marked, else re-pick
                        local live = workspace:FindFirstChild("Live")
                        local targetStillMarked = false
                        if _autoGodslayerTarget and live then
                            targetStillMarked = _autoGodslayerTarget.Parent == live and _autoGodslayerTarget:FindFirstChild("Ok") ~= nil
                        end

                        if not targetStillMarked then
                            local marked = _getMarkedPlayers()
                            _autoGodslayerTarget = #marked > 0 and marked[1] or nil
                        end

                        if not _autoGodslayerTarget then return end

                        local tr = _autoGodslayerTarget:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                        local r = c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                        if tr and r then
                            local dest = tr.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0, 2)
                            
                            r.CFrame = dest
                            r.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                            r.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
                            
                            if _shpSupported then
                                pcall(function() sethiddenproperty(r, "PhysicsRepRootPart", tr) end)
                                task.delay(0.1, function()
                                    pcall(function() sethiddenproperty(r, "PhysicsRepRootPart", nil) end)
                                end)
                            end
                        end
                    end
                    break
                end
            end

            -- Reset the teleport trigger when the animation finishes
            if not isPlayingGodslayer then
                _hasTeleported = false
                _autoGodslayerTarget = nil
            end
        end)
    end,
})

-- ==========================================
-- AUTO TECH SECTION (COMBINED)
-- ==========================================

local VirtualInputManager = game:GetService("VirtualInputManager")
local function PerformAutoDash()
    task.spawn(function()
        VirtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(true, Enum.KeyCode.Q, false, game)
        task.wait(0.05)
        VirtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(false, Enum.KeyCode.Q, false, game)
    end)
end

AutoTechTab:Toggle({
    Title = "Auto Wall Combo",
    Value = false,
    Callback = function(isEnabled)
        if _G.AutoWallConnection then 
            _G.AutoWallConnection:Disconnect() 
            _G.AutoWallConnection = nil 
        end
        
        if isEnabled then
            local Players = game:GetService("Players")
            local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
            
            local ValidAnimations = {
                ["rbxassetid://10469643643"] = true,
                ["rbxassetid://10470104242"] = true,
                ["rbxassetid://10503381238"] = true,
                ["rbxassetid://18716478642"] = true,
                ["rbxassetid://15162694192"] = true,
                ["rbxassetid://16552234590"] = true,
                ["rbxassetid://18716647575"] = true,
                ["rbxassetid://18716001119"] = true,
                ["rbxassetid://18715820628"] = true,
                ["rbxassetid://18716149223"] = true,
                ["rbxassetid://134775406437626"] = true,
                ["rbxassetid://18715747362"] = true,
                ["rbxassetid://17325537719"] = true,
                ["rbxassetid://13294471966"] = true,
                ["rbxassetid://13295936866"] = true,
                ["rbxassetid://13378708199"] = true,
                ["rbxassetid://13379003796"] = true,
                ["rbxassetid://14136436157"] = true,
                ["rbxassetid://17889290569"] = true,
            }
            
            local Debounce = false
            
            local function listenToCharacter(character)
                local humanoid = character:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 10)
                local rootPart = character:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 10)
                local communicate = character:WaitForChild("Communicate", 10)
                local animator = humanoid and humanoid:WaitForChild("Animator", 10)
                
                if not (humanoid and rootPart and communicate and animator) then return end
                
                local animTrackConnection
                animTrackConnection = animator.AnimationPlayed:Connect(function(track)
                    if not _G.AutoWallConnection then 
                        if animTrackConnection then animTrackConnection:Disconnect() end
                        return 
                    end
                    
                    if Debounce then return end
                    
                    local animation = track.Animation
                    if not animation then return end
                    
                    local animationId = animation.AnimationId
                    if not ValidAnimations[animationId] then return end
                    
                    Debounce = true
                    humanoid.AutoRotate = false
                    
                    local attachment = Instance.new("Attachment")
                    attachment.Parent = rootPart
                    
                    local align = Instance.new("AlignOrientation")
                    align.Attachment0 = attachment
                    align.Mode = Enum.OrientationAlignmentMode.OneAttachment
                    align.RigidityEnabled = true
                    align.Responsiveness = 200
                    align.MaxTorque = math.huge
                    align.CFrame = rootPart.CFrame * CFrame.Angles(math.rad(-90), 0, 0)
                    align.Parent = rootPart
                    
                    task.spawn(function()
                        for i = 1, 40 do
                            if not _G.AutoWallConnection then break end
                            
                            pcall(function()
                                communicate:FireServer({
                                    Goal = "Wall Combo"
                                })
                            end)
                            
                            task.wait()
                        end
                    end)
                    
                    task.wait(0.5)
                    
                    if align then align:Destroy() end
                    if attachment then attachment:Destroy() end
                    
                    if humanoid and humanoid.Parent then
                        humanoid.AutoRotate = true
                    end
                    
                    task.wait(1)
                    Debounce = false
                end)
            end

            if LocalPlayer.Character then
                listenToCharacter(LocalPlayer.Character)
            end
            
            _G.AutoWallConnection = LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(listenToCharacter)
        end
    end,
})

AutoTechTab:Toggle({
    Title = "Gojo Tech",
    Value = false,
    Callback = function(isEnabled)
        local Players = game:GetService("Players")
        local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
        local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer

        _G.GojoTechEnabled = isEnabled
        local gojoDebounce = false

        -- Animation ID updated to match source ("10503381238")
        local MINI_UPPERCUT_ID = "10503381238" 

        local function getClosestTarget()
            local closestChar, dist = nil, 18
            if not LocalPlayer.Character or not LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then return nil end
            local myPos = LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position

            local function evaluateModel(obj)
                if obj:IsA("Model") and obj ~= LocalPlayer.Character and obj:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") and obj:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") and obj.Humanoid.Health > 0 then
                    local d = (myPos - obj.HumanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude
                    if d < dist then
                        dist = d; closestChar = obj
                    end
                end
            end

            for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                if player ~= LocalPlayer and player.Character then evaluateModel(player.Character) end
            end
            local liveFolder = workspace:FindFirstChild("Live")
            if liveFolder then
                for _, obj in ipairs(liveFolder:GetChildren()) do 
                    if not Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(obj) then evaluateModel(obj) end 
                end
            end
            return closestChar
        end

        local function setupGojoTech(character)
            if not character then return end
            local humanoid = character:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 10)
            local myHrp = character:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 10)
            if not humanoid or not myHrp then return end
            local animator = humanoid:WaitForChild("Animator", 5)
            if not animator then return end

            if _G.GojoConn then _G.GojoConn:Disconnect() end

            _G.GojoConn = animator.AnimationPlayed:Connect(function(animTrack)
                if not _G.GojoTechEnabled or gojoDebounce then return end
                
                task.wait(0.05)
                if not animTrack or not animTrack.Animation then return end
                
                local currentAnimId = tostring(animTrack.Animation.AnimationId or "")
                if currentAnimId:find(MINI_UPPERCUT_ID, 1, true) then
                    local targetChar = getClosestTarget()
                    if targetChar and targetChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
                        local targetHrp = targetChar.HumanoidRootPart
                        
                        if (myHrp.Position - targetHrp.Position).Magnitude < 18 then
                            gojoDebounce = true
                            
                            -- Wait 0.35s for the uppercut launch
                            task.wait(0.35) 
                            
                            local followDuration = 1.3 
                            local startTime = tick()
                            local connection
                            local dynamicTilt = CFrame.Angles(math.rad(15), 0, math.rad(5))
                            
                            connection = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
                                if tick() - startTime > followDuration or not targetHrp or not targetHrp.Parent or targetChar.Humanoid.Health <= 0 or not _G.GojoTechEnabled then
                                    connection:Disconnect()
                                    myHrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
                                    gojoDebounce = false
                                    return
                                end
                                
                                local targetPos = targetHrp.Position
                                local underPosition = Vector3.new(targetPos.X, targetPos.Y - 4.5, targetPos.Z)
                                
                                myHrp.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(underPosition, targetPos) * dynamicTilt
                                myHrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                                myHrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
                            end)
                            
                            -- Continuous Q spam during Air Follow
                            task.spawn(function()
                                while tick() - startTime <= followDuration and _G.GojoTechEnabled do
                                    PerformAutoDash()
                                    task.wait(0.1) 
                                end
                            end)
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)
        end

        if isEnabled then
            if LocalPlayer.Character then setupGojoTech(LocalPlayer.Character) end
            if _G.GojoCharAdded then _G.GojoCharAdded:Disconnect() end
            _G.GojoCharAdded = LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(setupGojoTech)
        else
            if _G.GojoConn then _G.GojoConn:Disconnect() _G.GojoConn = nil end
            if _G.GojoCharAdded then _G.GojoCharAdded:Disconnect() _G.GojoCharAdded = nil end
            gojoDebounce = false
        end
    end,
})

_G.AutoVoidKillState = false

AutoTechTab:Toggle({
    Title = "Auto Void Kill",
    Value = false,
    Callback = function(state)
        local Players = game:GetService("Players")
        local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer

        _G.AutoVoidKillState = state

        -- Kill existing thread loop if active
        if getgenv().AutoVoidLoop then
            coroutine.close(getgenv().AutoVoidLoop)
            getgenv().AutoVoidLoop = nil
        end

        if not state then
            -- Reset camera when toggled off
            local char = LocalPlayer.Character
            if char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") then
                workspace.CurrentCamera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Custom
                workspace.CurrentCamera.CameraSubject = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            end
            return
        end

        -- Disable void kill line
        pcall(function()
            workspace.FallenPartsDestroyHeight = 0/0
        end)

        local animations = {
            ["rbxassetid://12273188754"] = 1.311,
            ["rbxassetid://12296113986"] = 1.2,
            ["rbxassetid://95034083206292"] = 1.3,
            ["rbxassetid://94638356008696"] = 1.3,
            ["rbxassetid://93546004428904"] = 1.3,
            ["rbxassetid://14048285180"] = 1.3,
            ["rbxassetid://13813099821"] = 1.3,
            ["rbxassetid://18182425133"] = 1.3
        }

        local function matchAnim(id)
            return animations[id] ~= nil
        end

        getgenv().AutoVoidLoop = coroutine.create(function()
            while _G.AutoVoidKillState do
                task.wait()
                local char = LocalPlayer.Character
                local humanoid = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                local animator = humanoid and humanoid:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator")

                if char and humanoid and hrp and animator then
                    for _, track in pairs(animator:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()) do
                        local animId = track.Animation and track.Animation.AnimationId
                        if animId and matchAnim(animId) then
                            task.wait(animations[animId])

                            local doTech = true
                            local lastCF = hrp.CFrame

                            local stopConn
                            stopConn = track.Stopped:Connect(function()
                                doTech = false
                                if stopConn then stopConn:Disconnect() end
                            end)

                            repeat
                                task.wait()
                                if not _G.AutoVoidKillState then break end
                                workspace.CurrentCamera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Scriptable
                                hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(-1200, -460, 67)
                                hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                                hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
                            until not doTech or not _G.AutoVoidKillState

                            task.wait(0.1)
                            if hrp and hrp.Parent then
                                hrp.CFrame = lastCF
                            end

                            workspace.CurrentCamera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Custom
                            workspace.CurrentCamera.CameraSubject = humanoid
                            task.wait(1)
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end)

        coroutine.resume(getgenv().AutoVoidLoop)

        game:GetService("StarterGui"):SetCore("SendNotification", {
            Title = "Auto Void Kill",
            Text = "Enabled and scanning animations...",
            Duration = 3
        })
    end,
})


AutoTechTab:Toggle({
    Title = "Mini Supa Tech",
    Value = false,
    Callback = function(isEnabled)
        local Players = game:GetService("Players")
        local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
        local SoundService = game:GetService("SoundService")
        local Debris = game:GetService("Debris")
        local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer

        _G.MiniSupaTechEnabled = isEnabled

        local soundId = "rbxassetid://122470447847332"
        local config = {
            DASH_DURATION = 0.15,
            FOLLOW_OFFSET = 2.5,
            ANGLE_TILT = math.rad(55),
            STICK_RANGE = 18,
            ANIMATION_IDS = {"10503381238", "13379003796"}
        }

        local isTechActive = false
        local tempBillboard = nil

        local function getNearestTarget(rootPart)
            if not rootPart then return nil end
            local bestTarget = nil
            local shortestDist = config.STICK_RANGE

            for _, desc in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
                if desc:IsA("Model") and desc ~= LocalPlayer.Character then
                    local targetHrp = desc:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    if targetHrp then
                        local success, dist = pcall(function()
                            return (rootPart.Position - targetHrp.Position).Magnitude
                        end)
                        if success and dist and dist < shortestDist then
                            bestTarget = desc
                            shortestDist = dist
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
            return bestTarget
        end

        local function createCooldownIndicator(character, duration)
            if tempBillboard then tempBillboard:Destroy() tempBillboard = nil end

            local adorneePart = character:FindFirstChild("Head") or character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if not adorneePart then return end

            local billboard = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
            billboard.Name = "TempCooldown"
            billboard.Size = UDim2.new(0, 60, 0, 12)
            billboard.Adornee = adorneePart
            billboard.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 2.2, 0)
            billboard.AlwaysOnTop = true
            billboard.Parent = character

            local bg = Instance.new("Frame")
            bg.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
            bg.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(20, 20, 30)
            bg.BackgroundTransparency = 0.4
            bg.BorderSizePixel = 0
            bg.Parent = billboard

            local bgCorner = Instance.new("UICorner")
            bgCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 3)
            bgCorner.Parent = bg

            local fillBar = Instance.new("Frame")
            fillBar.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
            fillBar.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 70, 70)
            fillBar.BorderSizePixel = 0
            fillBar.Parent = bg

            local fillCorner = Instance.new("UICorner")
            fillCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 3)
            fillCorner.Parent = fillBar

            local label = Instance.new("TextLabel")
            label.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
            label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            label.Text = tostring(duration)
            label.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
            label.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
            label.TextSize = 9
            label.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.3
            label.Parent = bg

            tempBillboard = billboard

            task.spawn(function()
                local startTime = tick()
                while tick() - startTime < duration do
                    if not billboard.Parent or not _G.MiniSupaTechEnabled then break end
                    local elapsed = tick() - startTime
                    local remaining = duration - elapsed
                    local progress = remaining / duration

                    fillBar.Size = UDim2.new(progress, 0, 1, 0)
                    label.Text = string.format("%.1f", remaining)
                    task.wait(0.05)
                end

                if billboard then billboard:Destroy() end
                if tempBillboard == billboard then tempBillboard = nil end
            end)
        end

        local function performMiniSupaTech()
            local char = LocalPlayer.Character
            if not char then return end

            local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            local root = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if not hum or not root then return end

            local targetModel = getNearestTarget(root)
            if not targetModel then return end

            local targetRoot = targetModel:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if not targetRoot then return end

            local savedState = {
                WalkSpeed = hum.WalkSpeed,
                JumpPower = hum.JumpPower,
                PlatformStand = hum.PlatformStand,
                AutoRotate = hum.AutoRotate
            }

            local freezeConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
                if root then
                    root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                    root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
                end
                if hum then hum.WalkSpeed = 0 end
            end)

            -- Perform auto dash input
            PerformAutoDash()
            task.wait(0.15)

            pcall(function()
                hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics)
                if root then
                    root.CFrame = root.CFrame * CFrame.Angles(config.ANGLE_TILT, 0, 0)
                end
            end)

            local duration = config.DASH_DURATION
            local startTime = tick()

            local followConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
                if tick() - startTime < duration and targetRoot and root then
                    local direction = (targetRoot.Position - root.Position).Unit
                    local targetPos = targetRoot.Position - direction * config.FOLLOW_OFFSET
                    root.CFrame = CFrame.new(targetPos) * CFrame.Angles(config.ANGLE_TILT, 0, 0)
                end
            end)

            task.wait(duration)

            if freezeConn then freezeConn:Disconnect() end
            if followConn then followConn:Disconnect() end

            pcall(function()
                if hum then
                    hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp)
                    hum.WalkSpeed = savedState.WalkSpeed or 16
                    hum.JumpPower = savedState.JumpPower or 50
                    hum.PlatformStand = savedState.PlatformStand or false
                    hum.AutoRotate = savedState.AutoRotate
                end
            end)
        end

        local function playToggleSound()
            local sound = Instance.new("Sound")
            sound.SoundId = soundId
            sound.Volume = 1
            sound.Parent = SoundService
            sound:Play()
            Debris:AddItem(sound, 2)
        end

        local function onAnimationPlayed(animTrack)
            if not _G.MiniSupaTechEnabled then return end

            local anim = animTrack and animTrack.Animation
            if anim then
                local animId = tostring(anim.AnimationId or "")
                for _, targetId in ipairs(config.ANIMATION_IDS) do
                    if animId:find(targetId, 1, true) then
                        -- Exactly 0.3s delay before dashing
                        task.delay(0.3, function()
                            if _G.MiniSupaTechEnabled and not isTechActive then
                                isTechActive = true
                                local char = LocalPlayer.Character
                                if char then
                                    task.spawn(function() createCooldownIndicator(char, 4) end)
                                end
                                task.spawn(performMiniSupaTech)
                                task.wait(4)
                                isTechActive = false
                            end
                        end)
                        return
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        local function setupMiniSupa(character)
            if not character then return end
            local hum = character:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 10)
            if not hum then return end

            if _G.MiniSupaAnimConn then _G.MiniSupaAnimConn:Disconnect() end
            if _G.MiniSupaAnimatorConn then _G.MiniSupaAnimatorConn:Disconnect() end

            _G.MiniSupaAnimConn = hum.AnimationPlayed:Connect(onAnimationPlayed)

            local animator = hum:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator")
            if animator then
                _G.MiniSupaAnimatorConn = animator.AnimationPlayed:Connect(onAnimationPlayed)
            end
        end

        if isEnabled then
            playToggleSound()
            if LocalPlayer.Character then setupMiniSupa(LocalPlayer.Character) end
            if _G.MiniSupaCharConn then _G.MiniSupaCharConn:Disconnect() end
            _G.MiniSupaCharConn = LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(setupMiniSupa)
        else
            if _G.MiniSupaAnimConn then _G.MiniSupaAnimConn:Disconnect() _G.MiniSupaAnimConn = nil end
            if _G.MiniSupaAnimatorConn then _G.MiniSupaAnimatorConn:Disconnect() _G.MiniSupaAnimatorConn = nil end
            if _G.MiniSupaCharConn then _G.MiniSupaCharConn:Disconnect() _G.MiniSupaCharConn = nil end
            if tempBillboard then tempBillboard:Destroy() tempBillboard = nil end
            isTechActive = false
        end
    end,
})

AutoTechTab:Toggle({
    Title = "Supa Tech",
    Value = false,
    Callback = function(isEnabled)
        local Players = game:GetService("Players")
        local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
        local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer

        _G.SupaTechEnabled = isEnabled

        local searchRadius = 20
        local cooldownTime = 4
        local heightOffset = 2.2
        local distanceOffset = 0.3
        local lastTriggerTime = 0
        local isTechActive = false

        local targetAnims = {
            ["10503381238"] = true,
            ["13379003796"] = true
        }

        local function findTarget(rootPart)
            if not rootPart then return nil end
            local bestTarget = nil
            local shortestDist = searchRadius

            for _, desc in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
                if desc:IsA("Model") and desc ~= LocalPlayer.Character then
                    local targetHrp = desc:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    if targetHrp then
                        local success, dist = pcall(function()
                            return (rootPart.Position - targetHrp.Position).Magnitude
                        end)
                        if success and dist and dist < shortestDist then
                            bestTarget = desc
                            shortestDist = dist
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
            return bestTarget
        end

        local function performSupaTech()
            local char = LocalPlayer.Character
            if not char then return end

            local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            local root = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if not hum or not root then return end

            local targetModel = findTarget(root)
            if not targetModel then return end

            local targetRoot = targetModel:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if not targetRoot then return end

            local savedState = {
                WalkSpeed = hum.WalkSpeed,
                JumpPower = hum.JumpPower,
                PlatformStand = hum.PlatformStand,
                AutoRotate = hum.AutoRotate
            }

            local function restoreState()
                pcall(function()
                    if hum then
                        hum.WalkSpeed = savedState.WalkSpeed or 16
                        hum.JumpPower = savedState.JumpPower or 50
                        hum.PlatformStand = savedState.PlatformStand or false
                        if savedState.AutoRotate ~= nil then
                            hum.AutoRotate = savedState.AutoRotate
                        end
                    end
                    if root then
                        root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                        root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
                    end
                end)
            end

            pcall(function()
                hum.WalkSpeed = 0
                hum.JumpPower = 0
                hum.PlatformStand = true
                hum.AutoRotate = false
                root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
            end)

            for _, obj in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
                local class = obj.ClassName
                if class == "BodyVelocity" or class == "BodyPosition" or class == "BodyGyro" 
                or class == "VectorForce" or class == "AlignPosition" or class == "AlignOrientation" 
                or class == "LinearVelocity" or class == "AngularVelocity" then
                    pcall(function() obj:Destroy() end)
                end
            end

            local freezeConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
                if root then
                    pcall(function()
                        root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                        root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
                    end)
                end
                if hum then pcall(function() hum.WalkSpeed = 0 end) end
            end)

            -- Perform auto dash input
            PerformAutoDash()
            task.wait(0.15)

            pcall(function()
                hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics)
                root.CFrame = root.CFrame * CFrame.Angles(math.rad(60), 0, 0)
            end)

            local duration = 0.2
            local startTime = tick()
            local moveConn = nil

            moveConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
                local elapsed = tick() - startTime
                if elapsed < duration then
                    local success, targetCFrame = pcall(function()
                        local targetPos = (targetRoot.Position - targetRoot.CFrame.LookVector * distanceOffset) + Vector3.new(0, heightOffset, 0)
                        local baseCFrame = CFrame.new(targetPos)
                        local rem = duration - elapsed
                        local shake = 0
                        if rem > 0.055 then
                            shake = math.sin(tick() * 110) * 0.35
                        end
                        return baseCFrame * CFrame.Angles(math.rad(60), shake, 0)
                    end)

                    if success and targetCFrame and root then
                        pcall(function() root.CFrame = targetCFrame end)
                    end
                else
                    if moveConn then moveConn:Disconnect() end
                end
            end)

            repeat task.wait() until tick() - startTime >= duration

            if freezeConn then freezeConn:Disconnect() end

            pcall(function()
                if root then
                    root.CFrame = root.CFrame * CFrame.Angles(math.rad(5), math.rad(-25), math.rad(-15))
                end
                if hum then hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp) end
            end)

            restoreState()
        end

        local function triggerTech()
            if not _G.SupaTechEnabled or isTechActive then return end
            if tick() - lastTriggerTime >= 0.3 then
                lastTriggerTime = tick()
                isTechActive = true
                
                task.spawn(function()
                    pcall(performSupaTech)
                    task.wait(cooldownTime)
                    isTechActive = false
                end)
            end
        end

        local function onAnimationPlayed(animTrack)
            if not _G.SupaTechEnabled then return end
            
            local anim = animTrack and animTrack.Animation
            if anim then
                local animId = tostring(anim.AnimationId or "")
                for targetId in pairs(targetAnims) do
                    if animId:find(targetId, 1, true) then
                        -- Exactly 0.3s delay before dashing
                        task.delay(0.3, triggerTech)
                        break
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        local function setupSupaTech(character)
            if not character then return end
            local hum = character:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 10)
            if not hum then return end

            if _G.SupaAnimConn then _G.SupaAnimConn:Disconnect() end
            if _G.SupaAnimatorConn then _G.SupaAnimatorConn:Disconnect() end

            _G.SupaAnimConn = hum.AnimationPlayed:Connect(onAnimationPlayed)

            local animator = hum:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator")
            if animator then
                _G.SupaAnimatorConn = animator.AnimationPlayed:Connect(onAnimationPlayed)
            end
        end

        if isEnabled then
            if LocalPlayer.Character then setupSupaTech(LocalPlayer.Character) end
            if _G.SupaCharConn then _G.SupaCharConn:Disconnect() end
            _G.SupaCharConn = LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(setupSupaTech)
        else
            if _G.SupaAnimConn then _G.SupaAnimConn:Disconnect() _G.SupaAnimConn = nil end
            if _G.SupaAnimatorConn then _G.SupaAnimatorConn:Disconnect() _G.SupaAnimatorConn = nil end
            if _G.SupaCharConn then _G.SupaCharConn:Disconnect() _G.SupaCharConn = nil end
            isTechActive = false
        end
    end,
})

loopdashSection:Toggle({
    Title = "Loop Dash Tech",
    Value = false,
    Callback = function(isEnabled)
        local Players = game:GetService("Players")
        local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
        local VirtualInputManager = game:GetService("VirtualInputManager")
        local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer

        _G.LoopDashEnabled = isEnabled

        local searchRadius = 300
        local uppercutDelay = 0.45 -- Adjust based on your ping
        local TARGET_ANIM_ID = "10503381238"

        local function getNearestTarget(rootPart)
            local nearestPart = nil
            local shortestDistance = searchRadius

            for _, obj in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
                if obj:IsA("Model") and obj ~= LocalPlayer.Character then
                    local humanoid = obj:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                    if humanoid and humanoid.Health > 0 then
                        local hrp = obj:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                        if hrp then
                            local distance = (rootPart.Position - hrp.Position).Magnitude
                            if distance < shortestDistance then
                                nearestPart = hrp
                                shortestDistance = distance
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
            return nearestPart
        end

        local function performLoopDashTech()
            if not _G.LoopDashEnabled then return end

            local character = LocalPlayer.Character
            if not character then return end
            
            local rootPart = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            local humanoid = character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
            if not rootPart or not humanoid then return end

            local targetPart = getNearestTarget(rootPart)
            local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera

            -- Store original states to restore later
            local originalAutoRotate = humanoid.AutoRotate
            local originalCamType = camera.CameraType
            local originalTargetSize = Vector3.new(2, 2, 1)
            
            -- 1. Fake Jump & Expand Hitbox
            if targetPart then
                local targetY = targetPart.Position.Y
                if targetY > rootPart.Position.Y then
                    rootPart.CFrame = CFrame.new(rootPart.Position.X, targetY, rootPart.Position.Z) * rootPart.CFrame.Rotation
                    rootPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(rootPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity.X, 15, rootPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Z)
                end

                originalTargetSize = targetPart.Size
                targetPart.Size = Vector3.new(15, 15, 15)
                targetPart.CanCollide = false
            end
            
            -- 2. Lock Camera and AutoRotate
            local cameraOffset = rootPart.CFrame:ToObjectSpace(camera.CFrame) 
            humanoid.AutoRotate = false 
            camera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Scriptable 

            -- 3. Trigger Dash (Q Key Inputs)
            for _ = 1, 3 do
                VirtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(true, Enum.KeyCode.Q, false, game)
                VirtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(false, Enum.KeyCode.Q, false, game)
                task.wait(0.05)
            end

            -- 4. Spin Character & Camera Sync
            local elapsedTime = 0
            local spinDuration = 0.3
            local aimStartTime = 0.26
            local aimDuration = 0.35
            local renderConnection = nil

            renderConnection = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(deltaTime)
                elapsedTime += deltaTime

                if elapsedTime < spinDuration then
                    local rotation = math.rad(360) * (deltaTime / spinDuration)
                    rootPart.CFrame = rootPart.CFrame * CFrame.Angles(0, rotation, 0)
                    camera.CFrame = rootPart.CFrame * cameraOffset
                end

                if targetPart and (elapsedTime >= aimStartTime and elapsedTime <= aimStartTime + aimDuration) then
                    local lookAtPos = Vector3.new(targetPart.Position.X, rootPart.Position.Y, targetPart.Position.Z)
                    rootPart.CFrame = CFrame.new(rootPart.Position, lookAtPos)
                    camera.CFrame = rootPart.CFrame * cameraOffset 
                end

                if elapsedTime >= aimStartTime + aimDuration and elapsedTime >= spinDuration then
                    renderConnection:Disconnect()
                    
                    humanoid.AutoRotate = originalAutoRotate
                    camera.CameraType = originalCamType
                    if targetPart and targetPart.Parent then
                        targetPart.Size = originalTargetSize
                    end
                end
            end)
        end

        local function setupAnimationDetection(character)
            if not character then return end
            local humanoid = character:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 10)
            if not humanoid then return end
            
            local animator = humanoid:WaitForChild("Animator", 10)
            if not animator then return end

            if _G.LoopDashAnimConn then
                _G.LoopDashAnimConn:Disconnect()
                _G.LoopDashAnimConn = nil
            end

            _G.LoopDashAnimConn = animator.AnimationPlayed:Connect(function(animationTrack)
                if not _G.LoopDashEnabled then return end
                
                if string.find(animationTrack.Animation.AnimationId, TARGET_ANIM_ID) then
                    task.delay(uppercutDelay, function()
                        performLoopDashTech()
                    end)
                end
            end)
        end

        if isEnabled then
            if LocalPlayer.Character then
                setupAnimationDetection(LocalPlayer.Character)
            end

            if _G.LoopDashCharConn then
                _G.LoopDashCharConn:Disconnect()
            end
            _G.LoopDashCharConn = LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(setupAnimationDetection)
        else
            if _G.LoopDashAnimConn then
                _G.LoopDashAnimConn:Disconnect()
                _G.LoopDashAnimConn = nil
            end
            if _G.LoopDashCharConn then
                _G.LoopDashCharConn:Disconnect()
                _G.LoopDashCharConn = nil
            end
        end
    end,
})


AutoTechTab:Toggle({
    Title = " Instant Twisted",
    Value = false,
    Callback = function(isEnabled)
        local player = game.Players.LocalPlayer
        local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
        local Stats = game:GetService("Stats")
        local StarterGui = game:GetService("StarterGui")

        _G.TwistedComboEnabled = isEnabled

        local anims = {
            "rbxassetid://13294471966",
            "rbxassetid://134775406437626"
        }

        local dashWait = 0.23
        local afterDash = 0.02
        local turnTime = 0.025
        local stayLeft = 0.07
        local lockTime = 0.15
        local predict = 0.22
        local maxDist = 100
        local turnDeg = -87

        local doingTheThing = false
        local rotateConn = nil
        local gyroLock = nil

        local function getPing()
            local pingStat = Stats.Network.ServerStatsItem["Data Ping"]
            if pingStat then
                return math.floor(pingStat:GetValue())
            end
            return 0
        end

        local function notifyPing(ping)
            StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {
                Title = "Twisted Combo",
                Text = "Ping: " .. ping .. " ms",
                Duration = 3
            })
        end

        local function autoConfigByPing(ping)
            if ping <= 60 then
                dashWait = 0.15
                predict = 0.18
                lockTime = 0.12
            elseif ping <= 120 then
                dashWait = 0.2
                predict = 0.22
                lockTime = 0.15
            elseif ping <= 200 then
                dashWait = 0.25
                predict = 0.28
                lockTime = 0.18
            else
                dashWait = 0.3
                predict = 0.35
                lockTime = 0.22
            end
        end

        local function findClosest(root)
            local closest = nil
            local bestDist = math.huge
            
            local liveFolder = workspace:FindFirstChild("Live")
            if not liveFolder then return nil end
            
            for _, obj in ipairs(liveFolder:GetChildren()) do
                local hrp = obj:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                local hum = obj:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
                if hrp and hum and hum.Health > 0 and obj ~= player.Character then
                    local dist = (root.Position - hrp.Position).Magnitude
                    if dist < bestDist and dist < maxDist then
                        bestDist = dist
                        closest = hrp
                    end
                end
            end
            return closest
        end

        local function makeTurnGyro(part, targetCF)
            local gyro = Instance.new("BodyGyro")
            gyro.MaxTorque = Vector3.new(0, math.huge, 0)
            gyro.P = 400000
            gyro.D = 800
            gyro.CFrame = targetCF
            gyro.Parent = part
            return gyro
        end

        local function twistedCombo()
            local char = player.Character
            if not char then return end
            
            local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            local hum = char:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
            if not hrp or not hum then return end
            
            local remote = char:FindFirstChild("Communicate")
            if not remote then return end
            
            doingTheThing = true
            
            rotateConn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
                if doingTheThing and hum.AutoRotate then
                    hum.AutoRotate = false
                end
            end)
            
            local originalDir = hrp.CFrame.LookVector
            
            task.wait(dashWait)
            
            remote:FireServer({
                Dash = Enum.KeyCode.W,
                Key = Enum.KeyCode.Q,
                Goal = "KeyPress"
            })
            
            task.wait(afterDash)
            
            local leftCF = hrp.CFrame * CFrame.Angles(0, math.rad(turnDeg), 0)
            local tempGyro = makeTurnGyro(hrp, leftCF)
            task.wait(turnTime)
            tempGyro:Destroy()
            
            task.wait(stayLeft)
            
            local target = findClosest(hrp)
            local lockConn = nil
            if target then
                if gyroLock then gyroLock:Destroy() end
                
                gyroLock = Instance.new("BodyGyro")
                gyroLock.MaxTorque = Vector3.new(0, math.huge, 0)
                gyroLock.P = 600000
                gyroLock.D = 0
                gyroLock.Parent = hrp
                
                lockConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
                    if not gyroLock.Parent or not target.Parent or not _G.TwistedComboEnabled then
                        if lockConn then lockConn:Disconnect() end
                        return
                    end
                    
                    local newTarget = findClosest(hrp)
                    if newTarget then target = newTarget end
                    
                    local vel = target.AssemblyLinearVelocity
                    local predicted = target.Position + vel * predict
                    gyroLock.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(hrp.Position, predicted)
                end)
                
                task.delay(lockTime + 0.15, function()
                    if lockConn then lockConn:Disconnect() end
                end)
            end
            
            task.wait(lockTime)
            
            local backCF = CFrame.lookAt(hrp.Position, hrp.Position + originalDir)
            local backGyro = makeTurnGyro(hrp, backCF)
            task.wait(turnTime)
            backGyro:Destroy()
            
            if gyroLock then
                gyroLock:Destroy()
                gyroLock = nil
            end
            
            task.wait(0.12)
            doingTheThing = false
            
            if rotateConn then
                rotateConn:Disconnect()
                rotateConn = nil
            end
        end

        local function setupAnimationListener(character)
            if not character then return end
            local hum = character:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 10)
            if not hum then return end

            if _G.TwistedAnimConn then
                _G.TwistedAnimConn:Disconnect()
                _G.TwistedAnimConn = nil
            end

            _G.TwistedAnimConn = hum.AnimationPlayed:Connect(function(track)
                if not _G.TwistedComboEnabled then return end
                local id = track.Animation.AnimationId
                for _, animId in ipairs(anims) do
                    if id == animId then
                        task.spawn(twistedCombo)
                        break
                    end
                end
            end)
        end

        if isEnabled then
            local ping = getPing()
            autoConfigByPing(ping)
            notifyPing(ping)

            task.spawn(function()
                while _G.TwistedComboEnabled do
                    local currentPing = getPing()
                    autoConfigByPing(currentPing)
                    task.wait(10)
                end
            end)

            if player.Character then
                setupAnimationListener(player.Character)
            end
            
            if _G.TwistedCharAddedConn then
                _G.TwistedCharAddedConn:Disconnect()
            end
            
            _G.TwistedCharAddedConn = player.CharacterAdded:Connect(setupAnimationListener)
        else
            if _G.TwistedAnimConn then
                _G.TwistedAnimConn:Disconnect()
                _G.TwistedAnimConn = nil
            end
            if _G.TwistedCharAddedConn then
                _G.TwistedCharAddedConn:Disconnect()
                _G.TwistedCharAddedConn = nil
            end
            if rotateConn then
                rotateConn:Disconnect()
                rotateConn = nil
            end
            if gyroLock then
                gyroLock:Destroy()
                gyroLock = nil
            end
            doingTheThing = false
        end
    end,
})


AutoTechTab:Toggle({
    Title = "crazy tech",
    Value = false,
    Callback = function(isEnabled)
        if _G.UppercutTechConnection then 
            _G.UppercutTechConnection:Disconnect() 
            _G.UppercutTechConnection = nil 
        end
        if _G.UppercutHeartbeatConnection then
            _G.UppercutHeartbeatConnection:Disconnect()
            _G.UppercutHeartbeatConnection = nil
        end
        
        if isEnabled then
            local Players = game:GetService("Players")
            local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
            local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
            
            local uppercutAnimationId = "rbxassetid://10503381238"
            local lastTriggerTime = 0
            
            local function getClosestTarget(myRoot)
                local live = workspace:FindFirstChild("Live") or workspace
                local closest, shortest = nil, 50
                
                for _, model in ipairs(live:GetChildren()) do
                    if model:IsA("Model") and model ~= LocalPlayer.Character then
                        local hrp = model:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or model:FindFirstChild("Torso")
                        if hrp then
                            if model.Name == "Weakest Dummy" or Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(model) or model:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") then
                                local dist = (hrp.Position - myRoot.Position).Magnitude
                                if dist < shortest then
                                    shortest = dist
                                    closest = model
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
                return closest
            end
            
            local function processAction(character)
                local myRoot = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if not myRoot then return end

                myRoot.CFrame = myRoot.CFrame + myRoot.CFrame.LookVector * -3

                local comm = character:FindFirstChild("Communicate")
                if comm and comm:IsA("RemoteEvent") then
                    comm:FireServer({
                        Dash = Enum.KeyCode.W,
                        Key = Enum.KeyCode.Q,
                        Goal = "KeyPress"
                    })
                end

                local target = getClosestTarget(myRoot)
                if target then
                    local targetHRP = target:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or target:FindFirstChild("Torso")
                    if targetHRP then
                        local frontPos = targetHRP.Position + targetHRP.CFrame.LookVector * 2
                        myRoot.CFrame = CFrame.new(frontPos, targetHRP.Position)

                        local start = tick()
                        if _G.UppercutHeartbeatConnection then _G.UppercutHeartbeatConnection:Disconnect() end
                        
                        _G.UppercutHeartbeatConnection = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
                            if not _G.UppercutTechConnection then
                                if _G.UppercutHeartbeatConnection then
                                    _G.UppercutHeartbeatConnection:Disconnect()
                                    _G.UppercutHeartbeatConnection = nil
                                end
                                return
                            end
                            
                            local elapsed = tick() - start
                            if elapsed > 1 then
                                if _G.UppercutHeartbeatConnection then
                                    _G.UppercutHeartbeatConnection:Disconnect()
                                    _G.UppercutHeartbeatConnection = nil
                                end
                            elseif elapsed > 0.7 then
                                myRoot.CFrame = CFrame.new(frontPos, targetHRP.Position)
                            else
                                local offsetY = math.floor(elapsed * 20) % 2 == 0 and 3 or -3
                                local movePos = targetHRP.Position + targetHRP.CFrame.LookVector * 2 + Vector3.new(0, offsetY, 0)
                                myRoot.CFrame = CFrame.new(movePos, targetHRP.Position)
                            end
                        end)
                    end
                end
            end
            
            local function listenToCharacter(character)
                local humanoid = character:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 10)
                if not humanoid then return end
                
                local animTrackConnection
                animTrackConnection = humanoid.AnimationPlayed:Connect(function(track)
                    if not _G.UppercutTechConnection then 
                        if animTrackConnection then animTrackConnection:Disconnect() end
                        return 
                    end
                    
                    local id = track.Animation and track.Animation.AnimationId
                    local now = tick()
                    
                    if id == uppercutAnimationId then
                        if now - lastTriggerTime < 1.5 then return end 
                        lastTriggerTime = now
                        
                        task.delay(0.31, function()
                            if _G.UppercutTechConnection and character and character.Parent then 
                                processAction(character)
                            end
                        end)
                    end
                end)
            end

            if LocalPlayer.Character then
                listenToCharacter(LocalPlayer.Character)
            end
            
            _G.UppercutTechConnection = LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(listenToCharacter)
        end
    end,
})

-- Global Task Manager to prevent stacked loops on toggle
_G.MiniLixTask = _G.MiniLixTask or nil
_G.MiniLixConnections = _G.MiniLixConnections or {}

AutoTechTab:Toggle({
    Title = "Mini Lix (Auto Side-Dash / Counter)",
    Value = false,
    Callback = function(state)
        local Players = game:GetService("Players")
        local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
        local StarterGui = game:GetService("StarterGui")
        local SoundService = game:GetService("SoundService")

        local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer

        -- Clear existing connections and tasks when toggled
        if _G.MiniLixTask then
            task.cancel(_G.MiniLixTask)
            _G.MiniLixTask = nil
        end

        for _, conn in pairs(_G.MiniLixConnections) do
            if conn then conn:Disconnect() end
        end
        table.clear(_G.MiniLixConnections)

        if not state then
            -- Reset character state & head visibility on disable
            local character = LocalPlayer.Character
            if character then
                local humanoid = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                local head = character:FindFirstChild("Head")
                if humanoid then humanoid.AutoRotate = true end
                if head then head.Transparency = 0 end
            end
            return
        end

        -- Configuration State
        local Config = {
            OnCooldown = false,
            CooldownDuration = 4.6,
            TargetDistance = 10,
            SideDashDelay = 0.29,
            LockDuration = 0.65,
            TurnSpeed = 65,
            ShowCooldownBar = true,
            JumpPower = nil,
            UseJumpPower = nil,
            Target = nil,
            LockConnection = nil,
            IsExecuting = false,
            TriggerAnims = {
                ["10503381238"] = true,
                ["13379003796"] = true
            },
            CooldownAnims = {
                ["10479335397"] = true,
                ["13380255751"] = true
            }
        }

        -- Helpers
        local function PlaySound(id, volume)
            local sound = Instance.new("Sound")
            sound.SoundId = "rbxassetid://" .. tostring(id)
            sound.Volume = volume or 10
            sound.Parent = SoundService
            sound:Play()
            sound.Ended:Connect(function()
                sound:Destroy()
            end)
        end

        local function SetHeadTransparency(transparency)
            local character = LocalPlayer.Character
            local head = character and character:FindFirstChild("Head")
            if head then
                head.Transparency = transparency
            end
        end

        local function ApplyUpwardForce(hrp)
            if Config.OnCooldown or not hrp then return end

            local jumpAtt = hrp:FindFirstChild("JumpAtt") or Instance.new("Attachment")
            jumpAtt.Name = "JumpAtt"
            jumpAtt.Parent = hrp

            local linearVelocity = Instance.new("LinearVelocity")
            linearVelocity.Attachment0 = jumpAtt
            linearVelocity.MaxForce = 1e999
            linearVelocity.VectorVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 40, 0)
            linearVelocity.RelativeTo = Enum.ActuatorRelativeTo.World
            linearVelocity.Parent = hrp

            task.delay(0.2, function()
                if linearVelocity then
                    linearVelocity:Destroy()
                end
            end)
        end

        local function DisableJumping(humanoid)
            if not humanoid then return end
            Config.JumpPower = humanoid.JumpPower
            Config.UseJumpPower = humanoid.UseJumpPower
            humanoid.UseJumpPower = true
            humanoid.JumpPower = 0
            humanoid:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping, false)
        end

        local function EnableJumping(humanoid)
            if not humanoid then return end
            humanoid:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping, true)
            if Config.JumpPower then
                humanoid.JumpPower = Config.JumpPower
            end
            if Config.UseJumpPower ~= nil then
                humanoid.UseJumpPower = Config.UseJumpPower
            end
        end

        -- UI Visuals
        local function CreateCooldownBar(duration)
            if not Config.ShowCooldownBar then return end

            local character = LocalPlayer.Character
            local hrp = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if not hrp then return end

            local oldBar = hrp:FindFirstChild("CooldownBar")
            if oldBar then oldBar:Destroy() end

            local billboard = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
            billboard.Name = "CooldownBar"
            billboard.Adornee = hrp
            billboard.Size = UDim2.fromScale(4.2, 0.48)
            billboard.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 3.3, 0)
            billboard.AlwaysOnTop = true
            billboard.Parent = hrp

            local bgGlow = Instance.new("Frame")
            bgGlow.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1.15, 1.4)
            bgGlow.Position = UDim2.fromScale(-0.075, -0.2)
            bgGlow.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(180, 70, 255)
            bgGlow.BackgroundTransparency = 0.75
            bgGlow.BorderSizePixel = 0
            bgGlow.ZIndex = 0
            bgGlow.Parent = billboard
            Instance.new("UICorner", bgGlow).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)

            local outerFrame = Instance.new("Frame")
            outerFrame.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
            outerFrame.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(110, 40, 180)
            outerFrame.BorderSizePixel = 0
            outerFrame.ZIndex = 1
            outerFrame.Parent = billboard
            Instance.new("UICorner", outerFrame).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 16)

            local innerBg = Instance.new("Frame")
            innerBg.Size = UDim2.fromScale(0.97, 0.72)
            innerBg.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.015, 0.14)
            innerBg.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(12, 8, 20)
            innerBg.BorderSizePixel = 0
            innerBg.ZIndex = 2
            innerBg.Parent = outerFrame
            Instance.new("UICorner", innerBg).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 14)

            local fillBar = Instance.new("Frame")
            fillBar.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
            fillBar.BorderSizePixel = 0
            fillBar.ZIndex = 3
            fillBar.Parent = innerBg
            Instance.new("UICorner", fillBar).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 14)

            local gradient = Instance.new("UIGradient")
            gradient.Color = ColorSequence.new({
                ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, Color3.fromRGB(160, 80, 255)),
                ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, Color3.fromRGB(220, 120, 255)),
                ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, Color3.fromRGB(110, 90, 255))
            })
            gradient.Parent = fillBar

            local shineGradient = Instance.new("UIGradient")
            shineGradient.Color = ColorSequence.new({
                ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)),
                ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255))
            })
            shineGradient.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
                NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 1),
                NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.45, 0.4),
                NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1)
            })
            shineGradient.Rotation = 35
            shineGradient.Offset = Vector2.new(-1, 0)
            shineGradient.Parent = fillBar

            local startTime = tick()
            local counter = 0
            local barConnection
            barConnection = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
                local progress = math.clamp((tick() - startTime) / duration, 0, 1)

                fillBar.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1 - progress, 1)
                shineGradient.Offset = Vector2.new(progress * 2 - 1, 0)

                counter = counter + dt * 3
                bgGlow.BackgroundTransparency = math.sin(counter) * 0.07 + 0.78

                if progress >= 1 then
                    barConnection:Disconnect()
                    billboard:Destroy()
                end
            end)
        end

        -- Target Detection & Combat
        local function GetClosestTarget()
            local character = LocalPlayer.Character
            local hrp = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            local liveFolder = workspace:FindFirstChild("Live")
            if not hrp or not liveFolder then return nil end

            local closestTarget = nil
            local minDistance = Config.TargetDistance

            for _, child in pairs(liveFolder:GetChildren()) do
                if child:IsA("Model") and child ~= character then
                    local targetHRP = child:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    local targetHumanoid = child:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

                    if targetHRP and targetHumanoid then
                        if child.Name == "Weakest Dummy" or Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(child) then
                            local distance = (targetHRP.Position - hrp.Position).Magnitude
                            if distance <= minDistance then
                                minDistance = distance
                                closestTarget = child
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end

            return closestTarget
        end

        local function AimAtTarget(deltaTime)
            if not Config.Target then return end

            local character = LocalPlayer.Character
            local hrp = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            local targetHRP = Config.Target:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

            if not hrp or not humanoid or not targetHRP then return end

            humanoid.AutoRotate = false

            local targetPos = Vector3.new(targetHRP.Position.X, hrp.Position.Y, targetHRP.Position.Z)
            local _, yaw = CFrame.lookAt(hrp.Position, targetPos):ToOrientation()

            hrp.CFrame = hrp.CFrame:Lerp(CFrame.new(hrp.Position) * CFrame.Angles(0, yaw, 0), deltaTime * Config.TurnSpeed)
        end

        local function FireDashRemote()
            if Config.OnCooldown then return end

            local character = LocalPlayer.Character
            local communicate = character and character:FindFirstChild("Communicate")
            if not communicate then return end

            communicate:FireServer({
                Dash = Enum.KeyCode.W,
                Key = Enum.KeyCode.Q,
                Goal = "KeyPress"
            })
        end

        local function TriggerCooldown(duration)
            if Config.OnCooldown then return end
            Config.OnCooldown = true
            CreateCooldownBar(duration)

            task.delay(duration, function()
                Config.OnCooldown = false
                local humanoid = LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                EnableJumping(humanoid)
            end)
        end

        local function ExecuteDashAction()
            if Config.IsExecuting or Config.OnCooldown then return end

            Config.Target = GetClosestTarget()
            if not Config.Target then return end

            Config.IsExecuting = true
            
            -- Remove Head during dash
            SetHeadTransparency(1)
