            FireDashRemote()

            if Config.OnCooldown then return end

            task.delay(0.8, function()
                TriggerCooldown(Config.CooldownDuration)
            end)

            local hrp = LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if hrp then
                ApplyUpwardForce(hrp)
            end

            task.delay(0.12, function()
                if not Config.IsExecuting then return end
                Config.LockConnection = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(AimAtTarget)
            end)

            task.delay(Config.LockDuration, function()
                if Config.LockConnection then
                    Config.LockConnection:Disconnect()
                    Config.LockConnection = nil
                end

                Config.Target = nil
                Config.IsExecuting = false

                -- Restore Head after dash lock ends
                SetHeadTransparency(0)

                local humanoid = LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                if humanoid then
                    humanoid.AutoRotate = true
                end
            end)
        end

        -- Animation Listener Hook
        local function HookCharacter(character)
            local humanoid = character:WaitForChild("Humanoid")

            local animConn = humanoid.AnimationPlayed:Connect(function(animationTrack)
                if Config.OnCooldown then return end

                local animId = animationTrack.Animation.AnimationId:match("%d+")
                if not animId then return end

                if Config.CooldownAnims[animId] then
                    task.delay(0.8, function()
                        TriggerCooldown(Config.CooldownDuration)
                    end)
                    return
                end

                if Config.TriggerAnims[animId] then
                    DisableJumping(humanoid)
                    task.delay(Config.SideDashDelay, ExecuteDashAction)
                end
            end)

            table.insert(_G.MiniLixConnections, animConn)
        end

        -- Initialize hook on active character & future spawns
        if LocalPlayer.Character then
            HookCharacter(LocalPlayer.Character)
        end

        local charAddedConn = LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(HookCharacter)
        table.insert(_G.MiniLixConnections, charAddedConn)

        PlaySound(6646751775, 10)
    end,
})



AutoTechTab:Toggle({
    Title = "Tornado Tech",
    Value = false,
    Callback = function(isEnabled)
        if _G.TornadoConnection then _G.TornadoConnection:Disconnect() _G.TornadoConnection = nil end
        if _G.TornadoHeartbeat then _G.TornadoHeartbeat:Disconnect() _G.TornadoHeartbeat = nil end
        if _G.TornadoDashConnection then _G.TornadoDashConnection:Disconnect() _G.TornadoDashConnection = nil end
        
        _G.TornadoEnabled = isEnabled

        if isEnabled then
            local Players = game:GetService("Players")
            local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
            local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
            
            _G.TornadoEarlyFire = _G.TornadoEarlyFire or 0.6
            _G.TornadoTilt = _G.TornadoTilt or 85
            _G.TornadoSpinSpeed = _G.TornadoSpinSpeed or 65
            _G.TornadoQDelay = _G.TornadoQDelay or 0.55
            _G.TornadoUpHeight = _G.TornadoUpHeight or 38
            _G.TornadoSpinTime = _G.TornadoSpinTime or 0.55

            local TARGET_ANIM_ID = "10503381238"
            local DETECTION_RANGE = 12.7
            local MAX_COOLDOWN = 5
            
            local Character, Humanoid, RootPart, Animator, CommunicateRemote
            local isAttacking, hasFired, isExecuting, lastTarget = false, false, false, nil
            local lastFireTime = 0

            local function ResetState()
                isAttacking = false
                hasFired = false
                isExecuting = false
                lastTarget = nil
            end

            local function StopDash()
                if _G.TornadoDashConnection then
                    _G.TornadoDashConnection:Disconnect()
                    _G.TornadoDashConnection = nil
                end
                if Humanoid and Humanoid.Parent then
                    Humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running)
                    Humanoid.AutoRotate = true
                end
            end

            local function GetNearestTarget()
                if not RootPart or not RootPart.Parent then return nil end
                local closestTarget = nil
                local minDistance = DETECTION_RANGE

                for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                    if player ~= LocalPlayer and player.Character then
                        local pRoot = player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                        local pHum = player.Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
                        if pRoot and pHum and pHum.Health > 0 then
                            local dist = (pRoot.Position - RootPart.Position).Magnitude
                            if dist < minDistance then minDistance = dist; closestTarget = pRoot end
                        end
                    end
                end

                for _, model in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
                    if model:IsA("Model") and model ~= Character then
                        local mRoot = model:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                        local mHum = model:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
                        if mRoot and mHum and mHum.Health > 0 then
                            local isPlayer = false
                            for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                                if player.Character == model then isPlayer = true break end
                            end
                            if not isPlayer then
                                local dist = (mRoot.Position - RootPart.Position).Magnitude
                                if dist < minDistance then minDistance = dist; closestTarget = mRoot end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
                return closestTarget
            end

            local function CheckForAttackAnimation()
                if not Animator or not Animator.Parent then return false, nil end
                for _, track in ipairs(Animator:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()) do
                    local id = tostring(track.Animation.AnimationId):match("%d+$")
                    if id == TARGET_ANIM_ID then return true, track end
                end
                return false, nil
            end

            local function ExecuteAttack(targetPart)
                if not RootPart or not RootPart.Parent or not CommunicateRemote then return end
                
                lastFireTime = tick()

                pcall(function()
                    CommunicateRemote:FireServer(unpack({ {Dash = Enum.KeyCode.W, Key = Enum.KeyCode.Q, Goal = "KeyPress"} }))
                end)

                if targetPart and targetPart.Parent then
                    if Humanoid and Humanoid.Parent then
                        Humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics)
                        Humanoid.AutoRotate = false
                    end
                    RootPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                    RootPart.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero

                    if _G.TornadoDashConnection then _G.TornadoDashConnection:Disconnect() end
                    
                    local elapsed = 0
                    local DASH_TIME = _G.TornadoSpinTime

                    _G.TornadoDashConnection = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
                        if not _G.TornadoEnabled then StopDash() return end
                        if not RootPart or not RootPart.Parent then StopDash(); return end

                        elapsed += dt
                        if elapsed >= DASH_TIME or not targetPart or not targetPart.Parent then
                            StopDash()
                            return
                        end

                        local aimPos = Vector3.new(targetPart.Position.X, targetPart.Position.Y - 2.8, targetPart.Position.Z)
                        local progress = elapsed / DASH_TIME
                        local arcY = math.sin(progress * math.pi) * _G.TornadoUpHeight
                        local diffX = aimPos.X - RootPart.Position.X
                        local diffZ = aimPos.Z - RootPart.Position.Z

                        RootPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(diffX * 28, arcY, diffZ * 28)
                        RootPart.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero

                        local spinRad = math.rad(_G.TornadoSpinSpeed * 40 * elapsed)
                        local tiltRad = math.rad(_G.TornadoTilt)
                        RootPart.CFrame = CFrame.new(RootPart.Position) * CFrame.Angles(tiltRad, spinRad, 0)
                    end)
                end

                task.wait(_G.TornadoQDelay)
                
                StopDash()
                isExecuting = false
                hasFired = false
                lastTarget = nil
            end

            local function listenToCharacter(newChar)
                Character = newChar
                Humanoid = Character:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 10)
                RootPart = Character:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 10)
                Animator = Humanoid and Humanoid:WaitForChild("Animator", 10)
                CommunicateRemote = Character:WaitForChild("Communicate", 5)
                ResetState()
            end

            if LocalPlayer.Character then listenToCharacter(LocalPlayer.Character) end
            _G.TornadoConnection = LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(listenToCharacter)

            _G.TornadoHeartbeat = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
                if not Character or not RootPart or not RootPart.Parent then return end
                if (tick() - lastFireTime) < MAX_COOLDOWN then return end

                local isPlaying, animTrack = CheckForAttackAnimation()
                local earlyFireThreshold = tonumber(_G.TornadoEarlyFire) or 0.6

                if isPlaying and not isAttacking then
                    isAttacking = true
                    hasFired = false
                    isExecuting = false
                    lastTarget = GetNearestTarget()

                elseif isPlaying and isAttacking then
                    if not hasFired and not isExecuting and lastTarget then
                        if animTrack and animTrack.Length > 0.01 then
                            if (animTrack.Length - animTrack.TimePosition) <= earlyFireThreshold then
                                hasFired = true
                                isExecuting = true
                                local target = lastTarget
                                task.spawn(function() ExecuteAttack(target) end)
                            end
                        end
                    end

                elseif not isPlaying and isAttacking then
                    isAttacking = false
                    if not hasFired and not isExecuting and lastTarget then
                        hasFired = true
                        isExecuting = true
                        local target = lastTarget
                        task.spawn(function() ExecuteAttack(target) end)
                    else
                        ResetState()
                    end
                else
                    ResetState()
                end
            end)
        else
            pcall(function()
                local char = LocalPlayer.Character
                local hum = char and char:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
                if hum then
                    hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running)
                    hum.AutoRotate = true
                end
            end)
        end
    end,
})

AutoTechTab:Toggle({
    Title = "Supa Tech",
    Value = false,
    Callback = function(isEnabled)
        if _G.SupaTechTechConnection then 
            _G.SupaTechTechConnection:Disconnect() 
            _G.SupaTechTechConnection = nil 
        end
        if _G.SupaTechFreezeConnection then
            _G.SupaTechFreezeConnection:Disconnect()
            _G.SupaTechFreezeConnection = nil
        end
        if _G.SupaTechFollowConnection then
            _G.SupaTechFollowConnection:Disconnect()
            _G.SupaTechFollowConnection = nil
        end
        if _G.SupaTechAnimConnection then
            _G.SupaTechAnimConnection:Disconnect()
            _G.SupaTechAnimConnection = nil
        end
        
        if isEnabled then
            local Players = game:GetService("Players")
            local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
            local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
            
            local stickRange = 18
            local dashDuration = 0.15
            local followOffset = 2.5
            local angleTilt = math.rad(55)
            local cooldownDuration = 4
            local animationIds = {"10503381238", "13379003796"}
            
            local inCooldown = false
            
            local function getClosestTarget(myRoot)
                local live = workspace:FindFirstChild("Live") or workspace
                local closest, shortest = nil, stickRange
                
                for _, model in ipairs(live:GetChildren()) do
                    if model:IsA("Model") and model ~= LocalPlayer.Character then
                        local hrp = model:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                        local hum = model:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                        if hrp and (not hum or hum.Health > 0) then
                            local dist = (hrp.Position - myRoot.Position).Magnitude
                            if dist < shortest then
                                shortest = dist
                                closest = hrp
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
                
                if not closest and live ~= workspace then
                    for _, model in ipairs(workspace:GetChildren()) do
                        if model:IsA("Model") and model ~= LocalPlayer.Character then
                            local hrp = model:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                            if hrp then
                                local dist = (hrp.Position - myRoot.Position).Magnitude
                                if dist < shortest then
                                    shortest = dist
                                    closest = hrp
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
                return closest
            end
            
            local function performStickDash(character)
                local myRoot = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                local humanoid = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                if not myRoot or not humanoid then return end
                
                local targetHRP = getClosestTarget(myRoot)
                if not targetHRP then return end
                
                local savedWalkSpeed = humanoid.WalkSpeed
                local savedJumpPower = humanoid.JumpPower
                local savedAutoRotate = humanoid.AutoRotate
                
                if _G.SupaTechFreezeConnection then _G.SupaTechFreezeConnection:Disconnect() end
                _G.SupaTechFreezeConnection = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
                    if myRoot and myRoot.Parent then myRoot.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero end
                    if humanoid and humanoid.Parent then humanoid.WalkSpeed = 0 end
                end)
                
                local comm = character:FindFirstChild("Communicate")
                if comm and comm:IsA("RemoteEvent") then
                    pcall(function()
                        comm:FireServer({
                            Dash = Enum.KeyCode.W,
                            Key = Enum.KeyCode.Q,
                            Goal = "KeyPress"
                        })
                    end)
                end
                
                task.wait(0.2)
                
                if not _G.SupaTechTechConnection or not myRoot or not myRoot.Parent or not targetHRP or not targetHRP.Parent then
                    if _G.SupaTechFreezeConnection then _G.SupaTechFreezeConnection:Disconnect() end
                    if humanoid and humanoid.Parent then
                        humanoid.WalkSpeed = savedWalkSpeed
                        humanoid.AutoRotate = savedAutoRotate
                    end
                    return 
                end
                
                pcall(function() humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics) end)
                myRoot.CFrame = myRoot.CFrame * CFrame.Angles(angleTilt, 0, 0)
                
                local start = tick()
                if _G.SupaTechFollowConnection then _G.SupaTechFollowConnection:Disconnect() end
                
                _G.SupaTechFollowConnection = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
                    if not _G.SupaTechTechConnection or not myRoot or not myRoot.Parent or not targetHRP or not targetHRP.Parent then
                        if _G.SupaTechFollowConnection then _G.SupaTechFollowConnection:Disconnect() _G.SupaTechFollowConnection = nil end
                        return
                    end
                    
                    local elapsed = tick() - start
                    if elapsed >= dashDuration then
                        if _G.SupaTechFollowConnection then _G.SupaTechFollowConnection:Disconnect() _G.SupaTechFollowConnection = nil end
                    else
                        local dir = (targetHRP.Position - myRoot.Position).Unit
                        myRoot.CFrame = CFrame.new(targetHRP.Position - dir * followOffset) * CFrame.Angles(angleTilt, 0, 0)
                    end
                end)
                
                task.wait(dashDuration)
                
                if _G.SupaTechFreezeConnection then _G.SupaTechFreezeConnection:Disconnect() _G.SupaTechFreezeConnection = nil end
                if _G.SupaTechFollowConnection then _G.SupaTechFollowConnection:Disconnect() _G.SupaTechFollowConnection = nil end
                
                pcall(function()
                    if humanoid and humanoid.Parent then
                        humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp)
                        humanoid.WalkSpeed = savedWalkSpeed
                        humanoid.JumpPower = savedJumpPower
                        humanoid.AutoRotate = savedAutoRotate
                    end
                end)
            end
            
            local function listenToCharacter(character)
                local humanoid = character:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 10)
                if not humanoid then return end
                
                if _G.SupaTechAnimConnection then _G.SupaTechAnimConnection:Disconnect() end
                _G.SupaTechAnimConnection = humanoid.AnimationPlayed:Connect(function(track)
                    if not _G.SupaTechTechConnection then return end
                    
                    local anim = track.Animation
                    if anim then
                        local id = tostring(anim.AnimationId)
                        for _, targetId in ipairs(animationIds) do
                            if string.find(id, targetId) then
                                task.delay(0.3, function()
                                    if _G.SupaTechTechConnection and not inCooldown then
                                        inCooldown = true
                                        task.spawn(function() performStickDash(character) end)
                                        task.wait(cooldownDuration)
                                        inCooldown = false
                                    end
                                end)
                                break
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end)
            end

            if LocalPlayer.Character then
                listenToCharacter(LocalPlayer.Character)
            end
            
            _G.SupaTechTechConnection = LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(listenToCharacter)
        else
            pcall(function()
                local character = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer.Character
                local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                if humanoid then
                    humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp)
                    humanoid.AutoRotate = true
                end
            end)
        end
    end,
})

AutoTechTab:Toggle({
    Title = "Lix Tech",
    Value = false,
    Callback = function(isEnabled)
        local Players = game:GetService("Players")
        local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer

        _G.LixTechEnabled = isEnabled

        -- Configuration
        local delayAdjust = 0.3
        local isTechActive = false
        local nilBvName = "moveme"
        
        local targetAnims = {
            [13379003796] = true,
            [10503381238] = true
        }

        local function getNilInstance(name, className)
            if not getnilinstances then return nil end
            for _, inst in ipairs(getnilinstances()) do
                if inst and inst.ClassName == className and inst.Name == name then
                    return inst
                end
            end
            return nil
        end

        local function getSavedState(hum)
            if not hum then
                return { WalkSpeed = 16, JumpPower = 50, PlatformStand = false, AutoRotate = true }
            end
            return {
                WalkSpeed = hum.WalkSpeed,
                JumpPower = hum.JumpPower,
                PlatformStand = hum.PlatformStand,
                AutoRotate = hum.AutoRotate
            }
        end

        local function onAnimationPlayed(animTrack)
            if not _G.LixTechEnabled or isTechActive then return end
            
            if animTrack and animTrack.Animation then
                local animIdStr = animTrack.Animation.AnimationId or ""
                local animIdNum = tonumber(animIdStr:match("%d+"))
                
                if animIdNum and targetAnims[animIdNum] then
                    isTechActive = true
                    
                    local char = LocalPlayer.Character
                    if not char then 
                        isTechActive = false 
                        return 
                    end
                    
                    local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                    local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    local savedState = getSavedState(hum)
                    
                    task.wait(delayAdjust)
                    
                    local commRemote = char:FindFirstChild("Communicate")
                    if commRemote then
                        pcall(function()
                            commRemote:FireServer({
                                Dash = Enum.KeyCode.W,
                                Key = Enum.KeyCode.Q,
                                Goal = "KeyPress"
                            })
                        end)
                    end
                    
                    local bodyVel = getNilInstance(nilBvName, "BodyVelocity")
                    local originalParent = nil
                    if bodyVel then
                        originalParent = bodyVel.Parent
                        pcall(function() bodyVel.Parent = nil end)
                    end
                    
                    if commRemote then
                        pcall(function()
                            commRemote:FireServer({
                                Goal = "delete bv",
                                BV = bodyVel
                            })
                        end)
                    end
                    
                    task.wait(0.3)
                    
                    if hrp then
                        pcall(function()
                            hrp.CFrame = hrp.CFrame * CFrame.Angles(0, math.rad(180), 0)
                        end)
                    end
                    
                    if bodyVel and bodyVel.Parent == nil and originalParent then
                        pcall(function() bodyVel.Parent = originalParent end)
                    end
                    
                    if hum then
                        pcall(function()
                            hum.WalkSpeed = savedState.WalkSpeed or 16
                            hum.JumpPower = savedState.JumpPower or 50
                            hum.PlatformStand = savedState.PlatformStand or false
                            hum.AutoRotate = savedState.AutoRotate or true
                        end)
                    end
                    
                    task.wait(0.4)
                    
                    hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or hrp
                    if hrp then
                        pcall(function()
                            hrp.CFrame = hrp.CFrame * CFrame.Angles(0, math.rad(180), 0)
                        end)
                    end
                    
                    task.wait(0.15)
                    isTechActive = false
                end
            end
        end

        local function setupLixTech(character)
            if not character then return end
            local hum = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") or character:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 10)
            if not hum then return end

            if _G.LixAnimConn then
                _G.LixAnimConn:Disconnect()
                _G.LixAnimConn = nil
            end

            _G.LixAnimConn = hum.AnimationPlayed:Connect(onAnimationPlayed)
        end

        if isEnabled then
            if LocalPlayer.Character then
                setupLixTech(LocalPlayer.Character)
            end
            
            if _G.LixCharConn then
                _G.LixCharConn:Disconnect()
            end
            _G.LixCharConn = LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(newChar)
                task.wait(0.1)
                setupLixTech(newChar)
            end)
        else
            if _G.LixAnimConn then
                _G.LixAnimConn:Disconnect()
                _G.LixAnimConn = nil
            end
            if _G.LixCharConn then
                _G.LixCharConn:Disconnect()
                _G.LixCharConn = nil
            end
            isTechActive = false
        end
    end,
})

AutoTechTab:Toggle({
    Title = "Omine Tech",
    Value = false,
    Callback = function(isEnabled)
        local Players = game:GetService("Players")
        local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
        local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
        local Workspace = game:GetService("Workspace")

        _G.OmineTechEnabled = isEnabled

        local OmineTech = {
            DetectAnimationId = "10503381238",
            BlockAnimationId = "10471478869"
        }

        local OmineConfig = {
            ["b"] = false,
            ["d"] = 2.9,
            ["e"] = 0,
            ["f"] = 0.5,
            ["g"] = 10,
            ["h"] = 150,
            ["i"] = 40,
            ["j"] = 836,
            ["k"] = 46
        }

        local OmineLockFunction = nil

        local function GetCharacterParts()
            local char = LocalPlayer.Character
            if char then
                local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                local root = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if hum and root then return char, hum, root end
            end
            return nil
        end

        local function OmineFireDash()
            local char = LocalPlayer.Character
            if char then
                local comm = char:FindFirstChild("Communicate")
                if comm then
                    local data = {{Dash = Enum.KeyCode.W, Key = Enum.KeyCode.Q, Goal = "KeyPress"}}
                    pcall(function() comm:FireServer(unpack(data)) end)
                end
            end
        end

        local function OmineFindTarget(radius)
            local rad = radius or OmineConfig["h"]
            local live = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Live")
            if not live then return nil end
            local _, _, root = GetCharacterParts()
            if not root then return nil end
            local best = nil
            for _, child in ipairs(live:GetChildren()) do
                if child and child:IsA("Model") and child ~= LocalPlayer.Character then
                    local targetRoot = child:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    local targetHum = child:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                    if targetRoot and targetHum and targetHum.Health > 0 then
                        local dist = (targetRoot.Position - root.Position).Magnitude
                        if dist <= rad then best = targetRoot; rad = dist end
                    end
                end
            end
            return best
        end

        local function OmineLockToTarget(target, duration, responsiveness)
            if not (target and target.Parent) then return nil end
            local _, hum, root = GetCharacterParts()
            if not (root and hum) then return nil end
            local resp = math.clamp(responsiveness or OmineConfig["j"], 1, 10000)
            local startTime = tick()
            local connection
            
            connection = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(delta)
                if not _G.OmineTechEnabled then
                    if connection then connection:Disconnect() end
                    return
                end

                if target and target.Parent and root and root.Parent then
                    local pos = root.Position
                    local targetPos = Vector3.new(target.Position.X, pos.Y, target.Position.Z)
                    
                    if (targetPos - pos).Magnitude >= 0.001 then
                        root.CFrame = CFrame.new(pos, Vector3.new(target.Position.X, pos.Y, target.Position.Z))
                        root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(root.AssemblyLinearVelocity.X, root.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Y, root.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Z)
                    end
                    if tick() - startTime >= duration then connection:Disconnect() end
                else
                    connection:Disconnect()
                end
            end)
            return function() if connection then pcall(function() connection:Disconnect() end) end end
        end

        local function OmineRunSequence()
            if OmineConfig["b"] or not _G.OmineTechEnabled then return end
            OmineConfig["b"] = true
            
            task.wait(OmineConfig["d"] / 10)
            local _, hum, root = GetCharacterParts()
            if hum and root and _G.OmineTechEnabled then
                hum.AutoRotate = false
                root.Velocity = Vector3.new(root.Velocity.X, OmineConfig["k"], root.Velocity.Z)
                task.wait(OmineConfig["e"] / 10)
                
                if _G.OmineTechEnabled then OmineFireDash() end
                
                task.wait(OmineConfig["f"] / 10)
                
                if _G.OmineTechEnabled then
                    local target = OmineFindTarget()
                    if target then
                        OmineLockFunction = OmineLockToTarget(target, OmineConfig["g"] / 10, OmineConfig["j"])
                    end
                end
                
                local endTime = tick() + (OmineConfig["g"] / 10)
                task.spawn(function()
                    while tick() < endTime and _G.OmineTechEnabled do
                        hum.AutoRotate = false
                        RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
                    end
                    hum.AutoRotate = true
                end)
            end
            
            local cdTime = OmineConfig["i"] / 10
            task.wait(cdTime)
            OmineConfig["b"] = false
        end

        local function OmineOnAnimationPlayed(animTrack)
            if _G.OmineTechEnabled and not OmineConfig["b"] then
                local animId = tostring(animTrack.Animation.AnimationId)
                if animId:find(OmineTech.DetectAnimationId) then
                    task.spawn(OmineRunSequence)
                end
            end
        end

        local function ConnectOmineCharacter(char)
            if not char then return end
            local hum = char:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 10)
            if not hum then return end

            if _G.OmineAnimConn then _G.OmineAnimConn:Disconnect() end
            _G.OmineAnimConn = hum.AnimationPlayed:Connect(OmineOnAnimationPlayed)
        end

        if isEnabled then
            if LocalPlayer.Character then
                ConnectOmineCharacter(LocalPlayer.Character)
            end

            if _G.OmineCharConn then _G.OmineCharConn:Disconnect() end
            _G.OmineCharConn = LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(ConnectOmineCharacter)
        else
            if _G.OmineAnimConn then
                _G.OmineAnimConn:Disconnect()
                _G.OmineAnimConn = nil
            end
            if _G.OmineCharConn then
                _G.OmineCharConn:Disconnect()
                _G.OmineCharConn = nil
            end
            
            local _, hum = GetCharacterParts()
            if hum then hum.AutoRotate = true end
            if OmineLockFunction then OmineLockFunction() end
            OmineConfig["b"] = false
        end
    end,
})

AutoTechTab:Toggle({
    Title = "Crystal Oreo",
    Value = false,
    Callback = function(isEnabled)
        local Players = game:GetService("Players")
        local UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")
        local VirtualInputManager = game:GetService("VirtualInputManager")
        local rs = game:GetService("RunService")
        local Debris = game:GetService("Debris")

        _G.CrystalOreoEnabled = isEnabled

        -- Cleanup active connections from previous state runs
        if _G.CrystalOreoConns then
            for _, conn in ipairs(_G.CrystalOreoConns) do
                if typeof(conn) == "RBXScriptConnection" then
                    conn:Disconnect()
                end
            end
        end
        _G.CrystalOreoConns = {}

        if not isEnabled then return end

        local player = Players.LocalPlayer
        if not player then return end

        -- Configuration / Settings
        local autojump_oreo = true
        local autodash_oreo = true
        local startdelay_oreo = 0.3

        local spinDuration = 0.3       -- Spin time
        local lockOnDuration = 0.2     -- Track and chase target
        local forwardSpeed = 85        -- Forward velocity during the spin
        local constantSpin = 1600      -- High, unchanging speed to force a sharp loop instantly
        local endDashSpeed = 100       -- Velocity when launching/tracking the nearest player
        local maxDetectionRadius = 150 -- Maximum distance to look for a player

        local isLooping = false

        -- Uppercut Animation IDs
        local uppercutAnimationIds = {
            ["rbxassetid://13379003796"] = true,
            ["rbxassetid://10503381238"] = true,
        }

        -- =====================================
        -- CORE LOGIC
        -- =====================================
        local function getCharacterData()
            local char = player.Character
            if not char then return nil, nil, nil end
            local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            local hum = char:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
            if hrp and hum and hum.Health > 0 then
                return char, hrp, hum
            end
            return nil, nil, nil
        end

        local function getNearestPlayer(myRootPart)
            local closestPlayer = nil
            local shortestDistance = maxDetectionRadius

            for _, otherPlayer in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                if otherPlayer ~= player and otherPlayer.Character then
                    local otherRoot = otherPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    local otherHumanoid = otherPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
                    
                    if otherRoot and otherHumanoid and otherHumanoid.Health > 0 then
                        local distance = (otherRoot.Position - myRootPart.Position).Magnitude
                        if distance < shortestDistance then
                            shortestDistance = distance
                            closestPlayer = otherPlayer
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
            return closestPlayer
        end

        local function executeCrystalSupa()
            local _, hrp = getCharacterData()
            if not hrp then return end

            if autojump_oreo then
                local bv = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
                bv.MaxForce = Vector3.new(0, 50000, 0) 
                bv.Velocity = Vector3.new(0, 30, 0) 
                bv.Parent = hrp
                Debris:AddItem(bv, 0.15)
            end

            task.wait(startdelay_oreo)
            local character, rootPart, humanoid = getCharacterData()
            if not character or isLooping then return end
            
            isLooping = true

            local originalState = humanoid:GetState()
            humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics)
            
            local timeElapsed = 0
            local connection
            
            if autodash_oreo then
                VirtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(true, Enum.KeyCode.Q, false, game)
                VirtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(false, Enum.KeyCode.Q, false, game)
            end
            
            connection = rs.Heartbeat:Connect(function(delta)
                timeElapsed = timeElapsed + delta
                
                local modernChar, modernRoot, modernHum = getCharacterData()
                if not modernChar or modernHum.Health <= 0 or not _G.CrystalOreoEnabled then
                    connection:Disconnect()
                    isLooping = false
                    return
                end
                
                modernHum.AutoRotate = false

                -- Phase 1: Spinning Loop
                if timeElapsed <= spinDuration then
                    local rotationAngle = math.rad(constantSpin * delta)
                    modernRoot.CFrame = modernRoot.CFrame * CFrame.Angles(0, rotationAngle, 0)
                    modernRoot.AssemblyLinearVelocity = modernRoot.CFrame.LookVector * forwardSpeed

                -- Phase 2: Lock-on Tracking
                elseif timeElapsed > spinDuration and timeElapsed <= (spinDuration + lockOnDuration) then
                    local targetPlayer = getNearestPlayer(modernRoot)
                    
                    if targetPlayer and targetPlayer.Character then
                        local targetRoot = targetPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                        if targetRoot then
                            local flatTargetPos = Vector3.new(targetRoot.Position.X, modernRoot.Position.Y, targetRoot.Position.Z)
                            local targetDirection = (flatTargetPos - modernRoot.Position).Unit
                            
                            modernRoot.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(modernRoot.Position, flatTargetPos)
                            modernRoot.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(
                                targetDirection.X * endDashSpeed,
                                0, 
                                targetDirection.Z * endDashSpeed
                            )
                        end
                    else
                        local flatLook = Vector3.new(modernRoot.CFrame.LookVector.X, 0, modernRoot.CFrame.LookVector.Z).Unit
                        modernRoot.AssemblyLinearVelocity = flatLook * endDashSpeed
                    end

                -- End Ability
                else
                    connection:Disconnect()
                    modernRoot.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
                    modernHum:ChangeState(originalState)
                    modernHum.AutoRotate = true
                    isLooping = false
                end
            end)
            table.insert(_G.CrystalOreoConns, connection)
        end

        -- Monitor Animations
        local function onCharacterAdded(character)
            local humanoid = character:WaitForChild("Humanoid")
            local animationConnection
            
            animationConnection = humanoid.AnimationPlayed:Connect(function(animationTrack)
                if not humanoid or humanoid.Health <= 0 then 
                    animationConnection:Disconnect()
                    return 
                end
                
                if not _G.CrystalOreoEnabled or isLooping then return end
                
                local animId = animationTrack.Animation.AnimationId
                if uppercutAnimationIds[animId] then
                    task.delay(0.1, function()
                        executeCrystalSupa()
                    end)
                end
            end)
            table.insert(_G.CrystalOreoConns, animationConnection)
        end

        -- Initialize Character Listeners
        local charAddedConn = player.CharacterAdded:Connect(onCharacterAdded)
        table.insert(_G.CrystalOreoConns, charAddedConn)

        if player.Character then
            onCharacterAdded(player.Character)
        end
    end,
})


loopdashSection:Toggle({
    Title = "LoopDash",
    Value = false,
    Callback = function(isEnabled)
        if _G.LoopDashTechConnection then 
            _G.LoopDashTechConnection:Disconnect() 
            _G.LoopDashTechConnection = nil 
        end
        if _G.LoopDashLerpConnection then
            _G.LoopDashLerpConnection:Disconnect()
            _G.LoopDashLerpConnection = nil
        end
        
        if isEnabled then
            local Players = game:GetService("Players")
            local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
            local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
            
            local dashAnimationId = "10503381238"
            local lastTriggerTime = 0
            
            local targetRadius = 50
            local waitDetect = 0.3
            local waitJump = 0
            local waitRemote = 0.1
            local lockDuration = 1.5
            local cooldownDuration = 1.0
            local responsiveness = 483
            local jumpVelocity = 62
            
            local debounce = false
            
            local function getClosestTarget(myRoot)
                local live = workspace:FindFirstChild("Live") or workspace
                local closest, shortest = nil, targetRadius
                
                for _, model in ipairs(live:GetChildren()) do
                    if model:IsA("Model") and model ~= LocalPlayer.Character then
                        local hrp = model:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or model:FindFirstChild("Torso")
                        local hum = model:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                        if hrp and hum and hum.Health > 0 then
                            if model.Name == "Weakest Dummy" or Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(model) or model:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") then
                                local dist = (hrp.Position - myRoot.Position).Magnitude
                                if dist < shortest then
                                    shortest = dist
                                    closest = hrp
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
                return closest
            end
            
            local function processAction(character)
                if debounce then return end
                debounce = true
                
                task.wait(waitDetect)
                if not _G.LoopDashTechConnection then debounce = false return end
                
                local myRoot = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                local humanoid = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                if not myRoot or not humanoid then debounce = false return end
                
                humanoid.AutoRotate = false
                myRoot.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(myRoot.AssemblyLinearVelocity.X, jumpVelocity, myRoot.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Z)
                
                if waitJump > 0 then task.wait(waitJump) end
                if not _G.LoopDashTechConnection then humanoid.AutoRotate = true debounce = false return end
                
                local comm = character:FindFirstChild("Communicate")
                if comm and comm:IsA("RemoteEvent") then
                    pcall(function()
                        comm:FireServer({
                            Dash = Enum.KeyCode.W,
                            Key = Enum.KeyCode.Q,
                            Goal = "KeyPress"
                        })
                    end)
                end
                
                task.wait(waitRemote)
                if not _G.LoopDashTechConnection then humanoid.AutoRotate = true debounce = false return end
                
                local targetHRP = getClosestTarget(myRoot)
                if targetHRP then
                    local start = tick()
                    if _G.LoopDashLerpConnection then _G.LoopDashLerpConnection:Disconnect() end
                    
                    _G.LoopDashLerpConnection = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
                        if not _G.LoopDashTechConnection or not myRoot or not myRoot.Parent or not targetHRP or not targetHRP.Parent then
                            if _G.LoopDashLerpConnection then
                                _G.LoopDashLerpConnection:Disconnect()
                                _G.LoopDashLerpConnection = nil
                            end
                            if humanoid and humanoid.Parent then humanoid.AutoRotate = true end
                            return
                        end
                        
                        local elapsed = tick() - start
                        if elapsed >= lockDuration then
                            if _G.LoopDashLerpConnection then
                                _G.LoopDashLerpConnection:Disconnect()
                                _G.LoopDashLerpConnection = nil
                            end
                            humanoid.AutoRotate = true
                        else
                            humanoid.AutoRotate = false
                            local currentPos = myRoot.Position
                            local targetLookPos = Vector3.new(targetHRP.Position.X, currentPos.Y, targetHRP.Position.Z)
                            
                            if (targetLookPos - currentPos).Magnitude >= 0.001 then
                                local targetCFrame = CFrame.new(currentPos, targetLookPos)
                                local alpha = math.clamp(1 - math.exp(-0.02 * responsiveness * dt), 0, 1)
                                local lerpedCFrame = myRoot.CFrame:Lerp(targetCFrame, alpha)
                                
                                myRoot.CFrame = CFrame.new(currentPos) * CFrame.fromMatrix(Vector3.new(), lerpedCFrame.RightVector, lerpedCFrame.UpVector)
                            end
                        end
                    end)
                else
                    humanoid.AutoRotate = true
                end
                
                task.wait(cooldownDuration)
                debounce = false
            end
            
            local function listenToCharacter(character)
                local humanoid = character:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 10)
                if not humanoid then return end
                
                local animTrackConnection
                animTrackConnection = humanoid.AnimationPlayed:Connect(function(track)
                    if not _G.LoopDashTechConnection then 
                        if animTrackConnection then animTrackConnection:Disconnect() end
                        return 
                    end
                    
                    local id = track.Animation and track.Animation.AnimationId
                    local now = tick()
                    
                    if id and tostring(id):find(dashAnimationId) then
                        if now - lastTriggerTime < (cooldownDuration + waitDetect + waitRemote) then return end 
                        lastTriggerTime = now
                        
                        task.spawn(function()
                            processAction(character)
                        end)
                    end
                end)
            end

            if LocalPlayer.Character then
                listenToCharacter(LocalPlayer.Character)
            end
            
            _G.LoopDashTechConnection = LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(listenToCharacter)
        else
            pcall(function()
                local character = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer.Character
                local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                if humanoid then
                    humanoid.AutoRotate = true
                end
            end)
        end
    end,
})

DripzSection:Toggle({
    Title = "Dripz Tech v2",
    Value = false,
    Callback = function(isEnabled)
        local Players = game:GetService("Players")
        local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
        local StarterGui = game:GetService("StarterGui")
        local Stats = game:GetService("Stats")
        local SoundService = game:GetService("SoundService")

        _G.DripzTechEnabled = isEnabled

        -- Cleanup active connections from previous state runs
        if _G.DripzTechConns then
            for _, conn in ipairs(_G.DripzTechConns) do
                if typeof(conn) == "RBXScriptConnection" then
                    conn:Disconnect()
                end
            end
        end
        _G.DripzTechConns = {}

        if _G.DripzCurrentBodyGyro then
            _G.DripzCurrentBodyGyro:Destroy()
            _G.DripzCurrentBodyGyro = nil
        end

        if not isEnabled then return end

        local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
        if not LocalPlayer then return end

        -- Animation IDs
        local DASH_ANIMATIONS = {
            "rbxassetid://10503381238",
            "rbxassetid://13379003796"
        }

        local TECH_ANIMATIONS = {
            "rbxassetid://10479335397",
            "rbxassetid://13380255751"
        }

        -- States
        local renderConnection = nil
        local isTechActive = false
        local isCooldownActive = false

        local originalJumpPower = nil
        local originalUseJumpPower = nil

        -- Config Timings
        local maxTargetDistance = 100
        local dashWait = 0.01
        local turnTime = 0.03
        local stayLeft = 0.07
        local lockTime = 0.85
        local predictTime = 0.22

        --------------------------------------------------------------------------------
        -- Helper Functions
        --------------------------------------------------------------------------------

        local function notify(title, text)
            pcall(function()
                StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {
                    Title = title,
                    Text = text,
                    Duration = 4
                })
            end)
        end

        local function playSound(soundId, volume)
            local sound = Instance.new("Sound")
            sound.SoundId = "rbxassetid://" .. tostring(soundId)
            sound.Volume = volume or 1
            sound.Parent = SoundService
            sound:Play()
            sound.Ended:Connect(function()
                sound:Destroy()
            end)
        end

        local function getPing()
            local pingItem = Stats.Network.ServerStatsItem:FindFirstChild("Data Ping")
            if pingItem then
                return pingItem:GetValue()
            end
            return 60
        end

        local function highlightCharacter(char)
            if not char then return end
            local highlight = Instance.new("Highlight")
            highlight.FillColor = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
            highlight.OutlineColor = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 255)
            highlight.FillTransparency = 1
            highlight.OutlineTransparency = 0
            highlight.Parent = char

            task.delay(0.2, function()
                if highlight then
                    highlight:Destroy()
                end
            end)
        end

        local function restoreJumping(humanoid)
            if not humanoid then return end
            humanoid:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping, true)
            if originalJumpPower then
                humanoid.JumpPower = originalJumpPower
            end
            if originalUseJumpPower ~= nil then
                humanoid.UseJumpPower = originalUseJumpPower
            end
        end

        local function applyJumpBoost(rootPart, targetRoot)
            if not rootPart then return end

            local jumpAtt = rootPart:FindFirstChild("JumpAtt")
            if not jumpAtt then
                jumpAtt = Instance.new("Attachment")
                jumpAtt.Name = "JumpAtt"
                jumpAtt.Parent = rootPart
            end

            local lookDir = if targetRoot then (targetRoot.Position - rootPart.Position).Unit else rootPart.CFrame.LookVector
            local linearVelocity = Instance.new("LinearVelocity")

            linearVelocity.Attachment0 = jumpAtt
            linearVelocity.MaxForce = 1e999
            linearVelocity.VectorVelocity = lookDir * 15 + Vector3.new(0, 45, 0)
            linearVelocity.RelativeTo = Enum.ActuatorRelativeTo.World
            linearVelocity.Parent = rootPart

            task.delay(0.18, function()
                if linearVelocity then
                    linearVelocity:Destroy()
                end
            end)
        end

        local function getClosestTarget(rootPart)
            local closestTarget = nil
            local closestDist = maxTargetDistance
            local liveFolder = workspace:FindFirstChild("Live")

            if not liveFolder then return nil end

            for _, child in ipairs(liveFolder:GetChildren()) do
                if child ~= LocalPlayer.Character then
                    local targetRoot = child:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    local targetHum = child:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")

                    if targetRoot and targetHum and targetHum.Health > 0 then
                        local dist = (rootPart.Position - targetRoot.Position).Magnitude
                        if dist < closestDist then
                            closestDist = dist
                            closestTarget = targetRoot
                        end
                    end
                end
            end

            return closestTarget
        end

        --------------------------------------------------------------------------------
        -- Main Tech Logic
        --------------------------------------------------------------------------------

        local function performTech()
            local character = LocalPlayer.Character
            if not character or not _G.DripzTechEnabled then return end

            local rootPart = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            local humanoid = character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
            local communicate = character:FindFirstChild("Communicate")

            if not rootPart or not humanoid or not communicate then return end

            isTechActive = true

            renderConnection = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
                if isTechActive and humanoid.AutoRotate then
                    humanoid.AutoRotate = false
                end
            end)
            table.insert(_G.DripzTechConns, renderConnection)

            local initialLook = rootPart.CFrame.LookVector
            task.wait(0.3)

            communicate:FireServer({
                Dash = Enum.KeyCode.W,
                Key = Enum.KeyCode.Q,
                Goal = "KeyPress"
            })
            task.wait(dashWait)

            -- Turn character
            local turnCFrame = rootPart.CFrame * CFrame.Angles(0, -2.443460952792061, 0)
            local initialGyro = Instance.new("BodyGyro")
            initialGyro.MaxTorque = Vector3.new(0, 1e999, 0)
            initialGyro.P = 400000
            initialGyro.D = 1000
            initialGyro.CFrame = turnCFrame
            initialGyro.Parent = rootPart

            task.wait(turnTime)
            initialGyro:Destroy()
            task.wait(stayLeft)

            -- Target tracking
            local targetRoot = getClosestTarget(rootPart)
            local heartbeatConn = nil

            if targetRoot then
                if _G.DripzCurrentBodyGyro then
                    _G.DripzCurrentBodyGyro:Destroy()
                end

                _G.DripzCurrentBodyGyro = Instance.new("BodyGyro")
                _G.DripzCurrentBodyGyro.MaxTorque = Vector3.new(0, 1e999, 0)
                _G.DripzCurrentBodyGyro.P = 600000
                _G.DripzCurrentBodyGyro.D = 500
                _G.DripzCurrentBodyGyro.Parent = rootPart

                heartbeatConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
                    if not _G.DripzCurrentBodyGyro or not _G.DripzCurrentBodyGyro.Parent or not targetRoot.Parent then
                        if heartbeatConn then heartbeatConn:Disconnect() end
                        return
                    end

                    local predictedPos = targetRoot.Position + (targetRoot.AssemblyLinearVelocity * predictTime)
                    _G.DripzCurrentBodyGyro.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(rootPart.Position, predictedPos)
                end)
                table.insert(_G.DripzTechConns, heartbeatConn)

                task.delay(1, function()
                    if heartbeatConn then heartbeatConn:Disconnect() end
                end)
            end

            task.wait(lockTime)

            -- Reset view forward
            local endCFrame = CFrame.lookAt(rootPart.Position, rootPart.Position + initialLook)
            local endGyro = Instance.new("BodyGyro")
            endGyro.MaxTorque = Vector3.new(0, 1e999, 0)
            endGyro.P = 400000
            endGyro.D = 1000
            endGyro.CFrame = endCFrame
            endGyro.Parent = rootPart

            task.wait(0.03)
            endGyro:Destroy()

            if _G.DripzCurrentBodyGyro then
                _G.DripzCurrentBodyGyro:Destroy()
                _G.DripzCurrentBodyGyro = nil
            end

            task.wait(0.12)
            isTechActive = false
            isCooldownActive = true

            task.delay(4.6, function()
                isCooldownActive = false
                local char = LocalPlayer.Character
                if char then
                    restoreJumping(char:FindFirstChild("Humanoid"))
                    highlightCharacter(char)
                end
            end)

            if renderConnection then
                renderConnection:Disconnect()
                renderConnection = nil
            end
        end

        local function onAnimationPlayed(animTrack, humanoid, character)
            if not _G.DripzTechEnabled then return end
            local animId = animTrack.Animation.AnimationId

            -- Tech Jump Disable Animations
            for _, id in ipairs(TECH_ANIMATIONS) do
                if animId == id then
                    if isCooldownActive or isTechActive then return end

                    if humanoid then
                        originalJumpPower = humanoid.JumpPower
                        originalUseJumpPower = humanoid.UseJumpPower
                        humanoid.UseJumpPower = true
                        humanoid.JumpPower = 0
                        humanoid:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping, false)
                    end

                    isCooldownActive = true
                    task.delay(0.85, function()
                        task.delay(4.6, function()
                            isCooldownActive = false
                            if LocalPlayer.Character then
                                highlightCharacter(LocalPlayer.Character)
                            end
                        end)
                    end)
                    return
                end
            end

            -- Tech Dash Animations
            for _, id in ipairs(DASH_ANIMATIONS) do
                if animId == id then
                    if isCooldownActive or isTechActive then return end

                    local rootPart = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    if rootPart then
                        local target = getClosestTarget(rootPart)
                        applyJumpBoost(rootPart, target)
                    end

                    task.spawn(performTech)
                    return
                end
            end
        end

        --------------------------------------------------------------------------------
        -- Character Event Handling
        --------------------------------------------------------------------------------

        local function connectCharacter(character)
            if not _G.DripzTechEnabled then return end

            local humanoid = character:WaitForChild("Humanoid")
            local animConn = humanoid.AnimationPlayed:Connect(function(animTrack)
                onAnimationPlayed(animTrack, humanoid, character)
            end)
            table.insert(_G.DripzTechConns, animConn)
        end

        local ping = getPing()
        local scale = math.clamp(ping / 60, 0.7, 2)
        notify("Script Ready", string.format("Ping: %dms | Scale: %.2fx", ping, scale))

        if LocalPlayer.Character then
            connectCharacter(LocalPlayer.Character)
        end

        local charConn = LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(character)
            if renderConnection then
                renderConnection:Disconnect()
                renderConnection = nil
            end
            if _G.DripzCurrentBodyGyro then
                _G.DripzCurrentBodyGyro:Destroy()
                _G.DripzCurrentBodyGyro = nil
            end

            if _G.DripzTechEnabled then
                task.wait(0.2)
                connectCharacter(character)
            end
        end)
        table.insert(_G.DripzTechConns, charConn)
    end,
})


DripzSection:Toggle({
    Title = "Dripz Tech",
    Value = false,
    Callback = function(isEnabled)
        -- Clear prior character connections/cleanup state
        if _G.DripzCharConn then
            _G.DripzCharConn:Disconnect()
            _G.DripzCharConn = nil
        end

        -- Global control reference flag
        _G.DripzEnabled = isEnabled

        if isEnabled then
            local Players = game:GetService("Players")
            local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
            local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
            local VirtualInputManager = game:GetService("VirtualInputManager")

            local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
            local Camera = workspace.CurrentCamera

            local Config = {
                Delay = 0.26,
                Jump = 58,
                CamDur = 0.46,
                CamSpeed = 1.65,
            }

            local UPPERCUT_ANIMS = {
                ["rbxassetid://10503381238"] = true,
                ["rbxassetid://13379003796"] = true,
                ["10503381238"] = true,
                ["13379003796"] = true
            }

            local function autoPressQ()
                pcall(function()
                    local remote = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Resources") 
                        and ReplicatedStorage.Resources:FindFirstChild("Brother") 
                        and ReplicatedStorage.Resources.Brother:FindFirstChild("#Friend") 
                        and ReplicatedStorage.Resources.Brother["#Friend"]:FindFirstChild("Communicate")
                    
                    if remote then
                        remote:FireServer({[1] = {Dash = Enum.KeyCode.W, Key = Enum.KeyCode.Q, Goal = "KeyPress"}})
                    end
                end)

                pcall(function()
                    VirtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(true, Enum.KeyCode.Q, false, game)
                    task.wait(0.05)
                    VirtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(false, Enum.KeyCode.Q, false, game)
                end)
            end

            local function forceJump(height)
                local char = LocalPlayer.Character
                if not char then return end
                local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if hrp then
                    pcall(function()
                        local vel = hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity
                        hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(vel.X, height, vel.Z)
                    end)
                end
            end

            local function getClosestEnemy(maxDist)
                local char = LocalPlayer.Character
                if not char then return end
                local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if not hrp then return end

                local best, bestDist = nil, maxDist
                local live = workspace:FindFirstChild("Live") or workspace

                for _, model in ipairs(live:GetChildren()) do
                    if model:IsA("Model") and model ~= char then
                        local hum = model:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                        local targetHrp = model:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                        if hum and hum.Health > 0 and targetHrp then
                            local dist = (targetHrp.Position - hrp.Position).Magnitude
                            if dist < bestDist then
                                best = targetHrp
                                bestDist = dist
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
                return best
            end

            local function runDripzTech()
                if not _G.DripzEnabled then return end
                
                local targetHrp = getClosestEnemy(60)
                local char = LocalPlayer.Character
                if not char or not targetHrp then return end
                local myHrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if not myHrp then return end

                task.delay(Config.Delay, function()
                    if not _G.DripzEnabled then return end
                    autoPressQ()
                    forceJump(Config.Jump)

                    local locked = true
                    task.delay(Config.CamDur, function() locked = false end)

                    local connection
                    connection = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
                        if not _G.DripzEnabled or not locked or not targetHrp or not targetHrp.Parent or not myHrp or not myHrp.Parent then
                            if connection then connection:Disconnect() end
                            return
                        end
                        local camPos = Camera.CFrame.Position
                        local offset = camPos - myHrp.Position
                        local targetCFrame = CFrame.new(myHrp.Position + offset, targetHrp.Position)
                        Camera.CFrame = Camera.CFrame:Lerp(targetCFrame, Config.CamSpeed * 0.1)
                    end)
                end)
            end

            local function setupAnimationHook(character)
                local hum = character:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 5)
                if not hum then return end
                local animator = hum:WaitForChild("Animator", 5)
                if not animator then return end

                animator.AnimationPlayed:Connect(function(track)
                    if not _G.DripzEnabled then return end
                    if track.Animation and (UPPERCUT_ANIMS[track.Animation.AnimationId] or UPPERCUT_ANIMS[track.Animation.AnimationId:match("%d+")]) then
                        runDripzTech()
                    end
                end)
            end

            if LocalPlayer.Character then 
                setupAnimationHook(LocalPlayer.Character) 
            end
            
            _G.DripzCharConn = LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(setupAnimationHook)
        end
    end,
})

AutoTechTab:Toggle({
    Title = "Auto Uppercut",
    Value = false,
    Callback = function(isEnabled)
        local Players = game:GetService("Players")
        local VirtualInputManager = game:GetService("VirtualInputManager")

        _G.AutoUpperPunchEnabled = isEnabled

        -- Cleanup active connections from previous runs
        if _G.AutoUpperPunchConns then
            for _, conn in ipairs(_G.AutoUpperPunchConns) do
                if typeof(conn) == "RBXScriptConnection" then
                    conn:Disconnect()
                end
            end
        end
        _G.AutoUpperPunchConns = {}

        if not isEnabled then return end

        local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
        if not LocalPlayer then return end

        --------------------------------------------------------------------------------
        -- Helper Functions & Punch Logic
        --------------------------------------------------------------------------------

        local function fireCommunicate(goal)
            local char = LocalPlayer.Character
            local communicate = char and char:FindFirstChild("Communicate")

            if communicate then
                communicate:FireServer(unpack({
                    {
                        Goal = goal,
                        Mobile = true,
                    }
                }))
            end
        end

        local function performUpperPunch()
            if not _G.AutoUpperPunchEnabled then return end

            VirtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(true, Enum.KeyCode.Space, false, game)
            
            task.delay(0.1, function()
                if _G.AutoUpperPunchEnabled then
                    fireCommunicate("LeftClick")
                end
            end)

            task.delay(0.3, function()
                if _G.AutoUpperPunchEnabled then
                    fireCommunicate("LeftClickRelease")
                end
            end)

            task.delay(0.5, function()
                VirtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(false, Enum.KeyCode.Space, false, game)
            end)
        end

        --------------------------------------------------------------------------------
        -- Character Event Connection
        --------------------------------------------------------------------------------

        local function setupCharacter(char)
            if not _G.AutoUpperPunchEnabled or not char then return end

            local humanoid = char:WaitForChild("Humanoid")
            local stateConn = humanoid.StateChanged:Connect(function(_, newState)
                if not _G.AutoUpperPunchEnabled then return end

                if newState == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping then
                    performUpperPunch()
                end
            end)
            table.insert(_G.AutoUpperPunchConns, stateConn)
        end

        if LocalPlayer.Character then
            setupCharacter(LocalPlayer.Character)
        end

        local charAddedConn = LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
            task.wait(0.1)
            setupCharacter(char)
        end)
        table.insert(_G.AutoUpperPunchConns, charAddedConn)
    end,
})


AutoTechTab:Toggle({
    Title = "macro",
    Value = false,
    Callback = function(isEnabled)
        local Players = game:GetService("Players")
        local Workspace = game:GetService("Workspace")
        local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
        local UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")
        local Stats = game:GetService("Stats")

        _G.CreditXMacroEnabled = isEnabled

        -- Global Connection & Cleanup Tracking
        if _G.CreditXConns then
            for _, conn in ipairs(_G.CreditXConns) do
                if typeof(conn) == "RBXScriptConnection" then
                    conn:Disconnect()
                end
            end
        end
        _G.CreditXConns = {}

        if not isEnabled then return end

        local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
        if not LocalPlayer then return end

        -- =========================================================================
        -- STATE VARIABLES (Credit X & Macro)
        -- =========================================================================
        local punchAnimActive = true
        local flingActive = true
        local flingPower = 10

        -- Macro Global States
        local u15 = true
        local up57 = 2
        local u18 = false
        local u19 = true
        local u13 = false
        local t4 = {}

        -- Key & Animation Tables for Macro
        local t2 = {
            [10470104242] = true,
            [10503381238] = true,
            [13379003796] = true
        }

        local t3 = {
            [10479335397] = true,
            [13380255751] = true
        }

        local u7

        -- =========================================================================
        -- CHARACTER & ANIMATION SETUP
        -- =========================================================================
        local character = LocalPlayer.Character or LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Wait()
        local humanoid = character:WaitForChild("Humanoid")

        local punchAnim = Instance.new("Animation")
        punchAnim.AnimationId = "rbxassetid://204062532"
        local punchTrack = humanoid:LoadAnimation(punchAnim)

        -- =========================================================================
        -- HELPER FUNCTIONS
        -- =========================================================================
        local function v10(p1)
            local t1 = {}
            for i = 1, #p1 do
                table.insert(t1, p1:sub(i, i))
            end
            for i = #t1, 2, -1 do
                local v31 = math.random(i)
                t1[i], t1[v31] = t1[v31], t1[i]
            end
            return table.concat(t1)
        end

        local function getSafePing()
            local ping = 0.1
            pcall(function()
                local network = Stats:FindFirstChild("Network")
                if network then
                    local dataPing = network:FindFirstChild("Data Ping")
                    if dataPing then
                        ping = math.max(dataPing:GetValue() / 1000, 0)
                    end
                end
            end)
            return ping
        end

        local function extractPlayerName(attrValue)
            if not attrValue or type(attrValue) ~= "string" then return "" end
            local matched = attrValue:match("^(.-);;")
            if matched then return matched end
            return attrValue:split(";;")[1] or ""
        end

        local function v16(p2)
            if not p2 then return nil end
            local Live = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Live")
            if not Live then return nil end

            for _, child in ipairs(Live:GetChildren()) do
                if child.Name == p2 and child:IsA("Model") then
                    return child
                end
            end
            return nil
        end

        local function v17(p3)
            if not p3 then return nil end
            if u15 then return p3.PrimaryPart end

            local v44 = p3:FindFirstChild("Torso") or p3:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso")
            if v44 and v44:IsA("BasePart") then
                return v44
            end
            return p3.PrimaryPart
        end

        local function v20(p4)
            if not p4 then return nil end
            local v49 = p4.Position + Vector3.new(0, -6, 0)
            local cFrame = CFrame.Angles(math.pi / 2, 0, 0)
            return CFrame.new(v49) * cFrame
        end

        local function v22(p5)
            if not p5 then return false end
            local Communicate = p5:FindFirstChild("Communicate")
            
            if Communicate and Communicate:IsA("RemoteEvent") then
                local t5 = {{
                    Dash = Enum.KeyCode.W,
                    Key = Enum.KeyCode.Q,
                    Goal = "KeyPress"
                }}
                pcall(function()
                    Communicate:FireServer(unpack(t5))
                end)
                return true
            end
            return false
        end

        -- =========================================================================
        -- MACRO LOGIC
        -- =========================================================================
        local function v23(p6, p7)
            if not u19 or u18 then return end
            if not p6 or not p6.Parent or p6 ~= LocalPlayer.Character then return end

            u18 = true

            local LastM1Hitted = p6:GetAttribute("LastM1Hitted")
            local targetName = extractPlayerName(LastM1Hitted)
            local v60 = v16(targetName)

            if not v60 or not v22(p6) then
                u18 = false
                return
            end

            local Humanoid = p6:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            local Animator = Humanoid and Humanoid:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator")

            if not Animator then
                u18 = false
                return
            end

            local timestamp = tick()
            local pingVal = getSafePing()
            local timeoutLimit = pingVal + 0.5
            local animDetected = false
            local animConnection

            animConnection = Animator.AnimationPlayed:Connect(function(animationTrack)
                if animationTrack and animationTrack.Animation then
                    local ok, animId = pcall(function()
                        return tonumber(animationTrack.Animation.AnimationId:match("%d+"))
                    end)

                    if ok and animId and t3[animId] then
                        animDetected = true
                        if animConnection then
                            animConnection:Disconnect()
                            animConnection = nil
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)
