            while not animDetected and tick() < (timestamp + timeoutLimit) do
                if not p6 or not p6.Parent then
                    if animConnection then animConnection:Disconnect() end
                    u18 = false
                    return
                end
                RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
            end

            if animConnection then animConnection:Disconnect() end

            if animDetected then
                local positionMode = (p7 ~= 10470104242) and "above" or "below"
                u7(p6, v60, positionMode)
            else
                u18 = false
            end
        end

        local function v25(p8)
            local ok, err = pcall(function()
                if not u19 or u18 or u13 then return end
                if not p8 or not p8.Parent or p8 ~= LocalPlayer.Character then return end
                if p8:GetAttribute("Character") ~= "Hunter" then return end

                u18 = true
                local Live = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Live")
                if not Live then 
                    u18 = false
                    return 
                end

                local connections = {}
                local hitTarget = nil
                local timeout = tick() + 0.7

                for _, model in ipairs(Live:GetChildren()) do
                    if model:IsA("Model") then
                        if extractPlayerName(model:GetAttribute("LastHit")) == LocalPlayer.Name then
                            table.insert(connections, model:GetAttributeChangedSignal("LastDamage"):Connect(function()
                                hitTarget = model
                            end))
                        else
                            table.insert(connections, model:GetAttributeChangedSignal("LastHit"):Connect(function()
                                if extractPlayerName(model:GetAttribute("LastHit")) == LocalPlayer.Name then
                                    hitTarget = model
                                end
                            end))
                        end
                    end
                end

                while not hitTarget and tick() < timeout do
                    task.wait()
                end

                for _, conn in ipairs(connections) do
                    conn:Disconnect()
                end

                if not hitTarget then 
                    u18 = false
                    return 
                end

                local grabTimeout = tick() + 0.7
                local beingGrabbed = nil

                while not beingGrabbed and tick() < grabTimeout do
                    beingGrabbed = hitTarget:FindFirstChild("BeingGrabbed")
                    task.wait()
                end

                if not beingGrabbed then 
                    u18 = false
                    return 
                end

                while beingGrabbed and beingGrabbed.Parent do
                    task.wait(0.05)
                end

                if not v22(p8) then 
                    u18 = false
                    return 
                end

                local Humanoid = p8:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                local Animator = Humanoid and Humanoid:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator")
                if not Animator then 
                    u18 = false
                    return 
                end

                local timestamp = tick()
                local pingVal = getSafePing()
                local timeoutLimit = pingVal + 0.5
                local animDetected = false
                local animConnection

                animConnection = Animator.AnimationPlayed:Connect(function(animationTrack)
                    if animationTrack and animationTrack.Animation then
                        local isOk, animId = pcall(function()
                            return tonumber(animationTrack.Animation.AnimationId:match("%d+"))
                        end)

                        if isOk and animId and t3[animId] then
                            animDetected = true
                            if animConnection then animConnection:Disconnect() end
                        end
                    end
                end)

                while not animDetected and tick() < (timestamp + timeoutLimit) do
                    if not p8 or not p8.Parent then
                        if animConnection then animConnection:Disconnect() end
                        u18 = false
                        return
                    end
                    RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
                end

                if animConnection then animConnection:Disconnect() end

                if animDetected then
                    u7(p8, hitTarget, "above")
                else
                    u18 = false
                end
            end)

            if not ok then
                u18 = false
            end
        end

        function u7(p9, p10, p11)
            local Humanoid = p9:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            local HumanoidRootPart = p9:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

            if not Humanoid or not HumanoidRootPart or not p10 then
                u18 = false
                return
            end

            local p10Name = p10.Name
            local origPos = HumanoidRootPart.Position
            local canCollideTable = {}

            for _, descendant in ipairs(p9:GetDescendants()) do
                if descendant:IsA("BasePart") then
                    canCollideTable[descendant] = descendant.CanCollide
                end
            end

            local origAutoRotate = Humanoid.AutoRotate
            local isActive = true

            local bodyVel, bodyGyro
            local preSimConn, dashConn, hitConn, lastHitConn, timeoutThread

            local function cleanup(success)
                if not isActive then return end
                isActive = false

                if bodyVel then bodyVel:Destroy() end
                if bodyGyro then bodyGyro:Destroy() end

                if preSimConn then preSimConn:Disconnect() end
                if dashConn then dashConn:Disconnect() end
                if hitConn then hitConn:Disconnect() end
                if lastHitConn then lastHitConn:Disconnect() end

                if timeoutThread then pcall(task.cancel, timeoutThread) end

                pcall(function()
                    if Humanoid and Humanoid.Parent then
                        Humanoid.AutoRotate = origAutoRotate
                    end

                    for part, canCollide in pairs(canCollideTable) do
                        if part and part.Parent then
                            part.CanCollide = canCollide
                        end
                    end

                    if p9 and p9.Parent and HumanoidRootPart and HumanoidRootPart.Parent then
                        if success then
                            local targetModel = v16(p10Name)
                            local targetPart = v17(targetModel)

                            if targetPart then
                                local pos = targetPart.Position
                                local yOffset = (p11 ~= "below") and 3 or -2
                                local finalPos = Vector3.new(pos.X, pos.Y + yOffset, pos.Z)
                                HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = CFrame.new(finalPos, pos)
                            end
                        else
                            local lookVector = HumanoidRootPart.CFrame.LookVector
                            HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = CFrame.new(origPos, origPos + lookVector)
                        end
                    end
                end)

                u18 = false
            end

            lastHitConn = p10:GetAttributeChangedSignal("LastHit"):Connect(function()
                if extractPlayerName(p10:GetAttribute("LastHit")) ~= LocalPlayer.Name then
                    cleanup(false)
                end
            end)

            task.delay(1, function()
                if lastHitConn then lastHitConn:Disconnect() end
            end)

            bodyVel = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
            bodyVel.Name = v10("sigeonpeks67boi_vel")
            bodyVel.MaxForce = Vector3.new(1e8, 1e8, 1e8)
            bodyVel.Velocity = Vector3.zero
            bodyVel.Parent = HumanoidRootPart

            bodyGyro = Instance.new("BodyGyro")
            bodyGyro.Name = v10("sigeonpeks67boi_gyro")
            bodyGyro.MaxTorque = Vector3.new(1e8, 1e8, 1e8)
            bodyGyro.P = 500000
            bodyGyro.CFrame = CFrame.Angles(math.pi / 2, 0, 0)
            bodyGyro.Parent = HumanoidRootPart

            dashConn = p9:GetAttributeChangedSignal("LastDashSwing"):Connect(function()
                cleanup(true)
            end)

            hitConn = p9:GetAttributeChangedSignal("LastHit"):Connect(function()
                cleanup(false)
            end)

            task.spawn(function()
                while isActive do
                    if not p9 or not p9.Parent or p9:FindFirstChild("Ragdoll") then
                        cleanup(false)
                        return
                    end
                    task.wait(0.05)
                end
            end)

            task.spawn(function()
                while isActive do
                    if not p10 or not p10.Parent or p10:FindFirstChild("JustUnrag") then
                        cleanup(false)
                        return
                    end
                    task.wait(0.05)
                end
            end)

            preSimConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
                if not isActive or not Humanoid or not Humanoid.Parent or not HumanoidRootPart or not HumanoidRootPart.Parent then
                    cleanup(false)
                    return
                end

                for part in pairs(canCollideTable) do
                    if part and part.Parent then
                        part.CanCollide = false
                    end
                end

                local targetModel = v16(p10Name)
                local targetPart = v17(targetModel)

                if targetPart then
                    HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = v20(targetPart)
                    return
                end

                cleanup(false)
            end)

            timeoutThread = task.delay(up57, function()
                if isActive then
                    cleanup(false)
                end
            end)
        end

        -- Touch Tap Animation Connection
        local touchConn = UserInputService.TouchTap:Connect(function(_, processed)
            if not processed and punchAnimActive and punchTrack then
                punchTrack:Play()
            end
        end)
        table.insert(_G.CreditXConns, touchConn)

        -- Fling Physics Loop
        task.spawn(function()
            local flipToggle = 0.1

            while _G.CreditXMacroEnabled do
                RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()

                if flingActive and character and character.Parent then
                    local rootPart = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") 
                        or character:FindFirstChild("Torso") 
                        or character:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso")

                    if rootPart then
                        local originalVelocity = rootPart.Velocity

                        rootPart.Velocity = originalVelocity * flingPower + Vector3.new(0, flingPower, 0)
                        RunService.RenderStepped:Wait()

                        if rootPart and rootPart.Parent then
                            rootPart.Velocity = originalVelocity
                        end

                        RunService.Stepped:Wait()

                        if rootPart and rootPart.Parent then
                            rootPart.Velocity = originalVelocity + Vector3.new(0, flipToggle, 0)
                            flipToggle = -flipToggle
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end)

        -- =========================================================================
        -- LISTENER & CHARACTER INITIALIZATION
        -- =========================================================================
        local function v26(newCharacter)
            if not newCharacter then return end

            character = newCharacter
            humanoid = character:WaitForChild("Humanoid")
            punchTrack = humanoid:LoadAnimation(punchAnim)

            local animator = humanoid and humanoid:WaitForChild("Animator", 10)

            if animator then
                local animConn = animator.AnimationPlayed:Connect(function(animationTrack)
                    if not animationTrack or not animationTrack.Animation then return end

                    local ok, animId = pcall(function()
                        return tonumber(animationTrack.Animation.AnimationId:match("%d+"))
                    end)

                    if not ok or not animId or not t2[animId] then return end

                    local m1Conn
                    m1Conn = character:GetAttributeChangedSignal("LastM1Hitted"):Connect(function()
                        if m1Conn then
                            m1Conn:Disconnect()
                            m1Conn = nil
                        end
                        task.spawn(v23, character, animId)
                    end)

                    task.delay(0.7, function()
                        if m1Conn then m1Conn:Disconnect() end
                    end)
                end)
                table.insert(t4, animConn)
                table.insert(_G.CreditXConns, animConn)
            end

            task.spawn(function()
                local hotbarPath
                pcall(function()
                    local current = LocalPlayer
                    for _, v in ipairs(("PlayerGui.Hotbar.Backpack.Hotbar.2.Base"):split(".")) do
                        current = current:WaitForChild(v, 3)
                    end
                    hotbarPath = current
                end)

                if hotbarPath then
                    local cooldownConn = hotbarPath.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
                        if child.Name == "Cooldown" and LocalPlayer.Character then
                            task.spawn(v25, LocalPlayer.Character)
                        end
                    end)
                    table.insert(t4, cooldownConn)
                    table.insert(_G.CreditXConns, cooldownConn)
                end
            end)

            local ultConn = character:GetAttributeChangedSignal("Ulted"):Connect(function()
                u13 = (character:GetAttribute("Ulted") == true)
            end)
            table.insert(t4, ultConn)
            table.insert(_G.CreditXConns, ultConn)

            u13 = (character:GetAttribute("Ulted") == true)
        end

        local charConn = LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(newCharacter)
            for _, conn in ipairs(t4) do
                conn:Disconnect()
            end
            table.clear(t4)
            u13 = false
            task.wait(0.2)
            v26(newCharacter)
        end)
        table.insert(_G.CreditXConns, charConn)

        if LocalPlayer.Character then
            task.spawn(v26, LocalPlayer.Character)
        end
    end,
})

AutoTechTab:Toggle({
    Title = "Nigger Tech",
    Value = false,
    Callback = function(isEnabled)
        local Players = game:GetService("Players")
        local TweenService = game:GetService("TweenService")
        local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")

        _G.AutoFollowEnabled = isEnabled

        -- Cleanup active connections and instances from previous runs
        if _G.AutoFollowConns then
            for _, conn in ipairs(_G.AutoFollowConns) do
                if typeof(conn) == "RBXScriptConnection" then
                    conn:Disconnect()
                end
            end
        end
        _G.AutoFollowConns = {}

        local function cleanupFollowParts()
            if _G.AutoFollowAlignPos then
                _G.AutoFollowAlignPos:Destroy()
                _G.AutoFollowAlignPos = nil
            end
            if _G.AutoFollowAlignOrient then
                _G.AutoFollowAlignOrient:Destroy()
                _G.AutoFollowAlignOrient = nil
            end
            if _G.AutoFollowRenderStep then
                _G.AutoFollowRenderStep:Disconnect()
                _G.AutoFollowRenderStep = nil
            end
            if _G.AutoFollowPart then
                _G.AutoFollowPart:Destroy()
                _G.AutoFollowPart = nil
            end

            local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
            local char = LocalPlayer and LocalPlayer.Character
            local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if hrp then
                for _, child in ipairs(hrp:GetChildren()) do
                    if child:IsA("Attachment") or child.Name == "HasSnapped" then
                        child:Destroy()
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        cleanupFollowParts()

        if not isEnabled then return end

        local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
        if not LocalPlayer then return end

        -- Config / Parameters
        local DASH_ANIMATIONS = {
            ["10503381238"] = true,
            ["13379003796"] = true
        }
        local TECH_ANIMATIONS = {
            ["10479335397"] = true,
            ["13380255751"] = true
        }

        local isBusy = false
        local maxTargetDistance = 10
        local dashDelay = 0.35
        local attachDuration = 0.50
        local smoothness = 50
        local prediction = 10
        local minResp = 20
        local maxResp = 400
        local predictFactor = 0.2

        --------------------------------------------------------------------------------
        -- Tracking & Attachment Logic
        --------------------------------------------------------------------------------

        local function executeAttachment(targetRoot)
            if not targetRoot or not targetRoot.Parent or targetRoot.Parent == LocalPlayer.Character then
                return
            end

            local char = LocalPlayer.Character
            local myRoot = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if not myRoot or not myRoot.Parent then return end

            local smoothnessClamped = math.floor(maxResp * (1 - smoothness / 100) + minResp * (smoothness / 100))
            local responsiveness = math.clamp(smoothnessClamped, minResp, maxResp)
            local leadTime = (prediction / 100) * predictFactor

            cleanupFollowParts()

            local followPart = Instance.new("Part")
            followPart.Size = Vector3.new(0.5, 0.5, 0.5)
            followPart.Transparency = 1
            followPart.Anchored = true
            followPart.CanCollide = false
            followPart.Name = "FollowPart"
            followPart.Parent = workspace
            _G.AutoFollowPart = followPart

            local att0_pos = Instance.new("Attachment", myRoot)
            local att1_pos = Instance.new("Attachment", followPart)
            local att0_rot = Instance.new("Attachment", myRoot)
            local att1_rot = Instance.new("Attachment", followPart)

            local alignPos = Instance.new("AlignPosition")
            alignPos.Attachment0 = att0_pos
            alignPos.Attachment1 = att1_pos
            alignPos.RigidityEnabled = true
            alignPos.Responsiveness = responsiveness
            alignPos.MaxForce = math.huge
            alignPos.Parent = myRoot
            _G.AutoFollowAlignPos = alignPos

            local alignOrient = Instance.new("AlignOrientation")
            alignOrient.Attachment0 = att0_rot
            alignOrient.Attachment1 = att1_rot
            alignOrient.RigidityEnabled = true
            alignOrient.Responsiveness = responsiveness
            alignOrient.MaxTorque = math.huge
            alignOrient.Parent = myRoot
            _G.AutoFollowAlignOrient = alignOrient

            local renderConn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
                if targetRoot and targetRoot.Parent and myRoot and myRoot.Parent then
                    local targetPos = targetRoot.Position
                    local targetVel = targetRoot.Velocity or Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
                    local predictedY = targetPos.Y + (targetVel.Y * leadTime)
                    local targetCFrame = CFrame.new(targetPos.X, predictedY, targetPos.Z) * CFrame.Angles(math.rad(85), 0, 0)
                    
                    followPart.CFrame = targetCFrame

                    if not myRoot:FindFirstChild("HasSnapped") then
                        myRoot.CFrame = targetCFrame
                        local snapVal = Instance.new("BoolValue")
                        snapVal.Name = "HasSnapped"
                        snapVal.Parent = myRoot
                    end
                end
            end)
            _G.AutoFollowRenderStep = renderConn
            table.insert(_G.AutoFollowConns, renderConn)
        end

        local function findClosestTarget()
            local closest = nil
            local shortestDist = maxTargetDistance
            local liveFolder = workspace:FindFirstChild("Live")

            if liveFolder then
                for _, child in ipairs(liveFolder:GetChildren()) do
                    if child:IsA("Model") and child ~= LocalPlayer.Character then
                        local targetRoot = child:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                        local targetHum = child:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                        local myRoot = LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

                        if targetRoot and targetHum and targetHum.Health > 0 and myRoot then
                            local dist = (targetRoot.Position - myRoot.Position).Magnitude
                            if dist <= shortestDist then
                                if child.Name == "Weakest Dummy" or Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(child) then
                                    shortestDist = dist
                                    closest = targetRoot
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
            return closest
        end

        local function onAnimationPlayed(track)
            if not _G.AutoFollowEnabled or isBusy then return end

            local animId = string.match(track.Animation.AnimationId or "", "%d+")
            if not animId then return end

            if TECH_ANIMATIONS[animId] then
                isBusy = true
                task.delay(0.85, function()
                    task.delay(4.8, function()
                        isBusy = false
                    end)
                end)
            elseif DASH_ANIMATIONS[animId] then
                local targetRoot = findClosestTarget()
                isBusy = true

                task.spawn(function()
                    task.wait(dashDelay)

                    local communicate = LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("Communicate")
                    if communicate then
                        pcall(function()
                            communicate:FireServer({
                                Dash = Enum.KeyCode.W,
                                Key = Enum.KeyCode.Q,
                                Goal = "KeyPress"
                            })
                        end)
                    end

                    if targetRoot and targetRoot.Parent then
                        executeAttachment(targetRoot)
                        task.delay(attachDuration, cleanupFollowParts)
                    end
                end)

                task.delay(0.85, function()
                    task.delay(4.8, function()
                        isBusy = false
                    end)
                end)
            end
        end

        --------------------------------------------------------------------------------
        -- Character Event Connection
        --------------------------------------------------------------------------------

        local function setupCharacter(char)
            if not _G.AutoFollowEnabled or not char then return end

            local humanoid = char:WaitForChild("Humanoid")
            local animConn = humanoid.AnimationPlayed:Connect(onAnimationPlayed)
            table.insert(_G.AutoFollowConns, animConn)
        end

        if LocalPlayer.Character then
            setupCharacter(LocalPlayer.Character)
        end

        local charAddedConn = LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
            task.wait(0.1)
            setupCharacter(char)
        end)
        table.insert(_G.AutoFollowConns, charAddedConn)
    end,
})


AutoTechTab:Toggle({
    Title = "K1ng Tech",
    Value = false,
    Callback = function(isEnabled)
        local Players = game:GetService("Players")
        local UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")
        local VirtualInputManager = game:GetService("VirtualInputManager")
        local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
        local Debris = game:GetService("Debris")

        _G.K1ngTechEnabled = isEnabled

        -- Cleanup active connections from previous state runs
        if _G.K1ngTechConns then
            for _, conn in ipairs(_G.K1ngTechConns) do
                if typeof(conn) == "RBXScriptConnection" then
                    conn:Disconnect()
                end
            end
        end
        _G.K1ngTechConns = {}

        if not isEnabled then return end

        local player = Players.LocalPlayer
        if not player then return end

        -- Configuration / Settings
        local targetAnimationIds = { 
            ["rbxassetid://13379003796"] = true, 
            ["rbxassetid://10503381238"] = true 
        }

        local startdelay_k1ng = 0.3
        local jumpdelay_k1ng = 0.2
        local rotatedelay_k1ng = 0.25

        -- =====================================
        -- CORE LOGIC
        -- =====================================
        local function onK1ngCharAdded(char)
            local hum = char:WaitForChild("Humanoid")
            local hrp = char:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            local conn

            conn = hum.AnimationPlayed:Connect(function(track)
                if not hum or hum.Health <= 0 then 
                    conn:Disconnect() 
                    return 
                end
                
                if not targetAnimationIds[track.Animation.AnimationId] then return end
                if not _G.K1ngTechEnabled then return end

                -- Auto Jump
                task.delay(jumpdelay_k1ng, function()
                    if not _G.K1ngTechEnabled or not hrp or not hrp.Parent then return end
                    local bv = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
                    bv.MaxForce = Vector3.new(0, 50000, 0)
                    bv.Velocity = Vector3.new(0, 30, 0)
                    bv.Parent = hrp
                    Debris:AddItem(bv, 0.15)
                end)

                -- Dash & 180-degree Rotation
                task.delay(startdelay_k1ng, function()
                    if not _G.K1ngTechEnabled or not hrp or not hrp.Parent then return end
                    
                    -- Press Q to Dash
                    VirtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(true, Enum.KeyCode.Q, false, game)
                    VirtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(false, Enum.KeyCode.Q, false, game)

                    local tcf = hrp.CFrame * CFrame.Angles(0, math.rad(180), 0)
                    local et = 0
                    task.wait(0.25)

                    local kc
                    kc = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
                        if not _G.K1ngTechEnabled or not hum or hum.Health <= 0 or not hrp or not hrp.Parent then 
                            if kc then kc:Disconnect() end
                            if hum then hum.AutoRotate = true end 
                            return 
                        end

                        et += dt
                        hum.AutoRotate = false
                        hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(hrp.Position) * tcf.Rotation

                        if et >= rotatedelay_k1ng then 
                            kc:Disconnect()
                            hum.AutoRotate = true 
                        end
                    end)
                    table.insert(_G.K1ngTechConns, kc)
                end)
            end)
            table.insert(_G.K1ngTechConns, conn)
        end

        -- Initialize Character Listeners
        local charConn = player.CharacterAdded:Connect(onK1ngCharAdded)
        table.insert(_G.K1ngTechConns, charConn)

        if player.Character then 
            onK1ngCharAdded(player.Character) 
        end
    end,
})


AutoTechTab:Toggle({
    Title = "Oreo v2 Utility",
    Value = false,
    Callback = function(isEnabled)
        -- Clear prior character connections/cleanup state
        if _G.OreoCharConn then
            _G.OreoCharConn:Disconnect()
            _G.OreoCharConn = nil
        end

        -- Global control reference flag
        _G.OreoEnabled = isEnabled

        if isEnabled then
            local Players = game:GetService("Players")
            local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
            local VirtualInputManager = game:GetService("VirtualInputManager")
            local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")

            local player = Players.LocalPlayer
            local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera

            local DashRemote
            local function ensureRemote()
                if not DashRemote then
                    pcall(function()
                        DashRemote = ReplicatedStorage.Resources.Brother["#Friend"].Communicate
                    end)
                end
                return DashRemote ~= nil
            end

            local UPPERCUT = {
                ["rbxassetid://10503381238"] = true,
                ["rbxassetid://13379003796"] = true,
            }

            local settings = {
                kittyStartJump = 0.07,
                kittyStart     = 0.26,
                kittyWait      = 0.08,
                kittyCam       = 1,
                kittyDur       = 0.17,
                onCooldown     = false,
            }

            local function getRoot(char)
                return char and (
                    char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or
                    char:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso") or
                    char:FindFirstChild("LowerTorso") or
                    char:FindFirstChild("Torso")
                )
            end

            local function getClosestEnemy(maxDist)
                local hrp = getRoot(player.Character)
                if not hrp then return nil end
                local live = workspace:FindFirstChild("Live")
                if not live then return nil end
                local best, bestD = nil, maxDist
                for _, m in ipairs(live:GetChildren()) do
                    if m:IsA("Model") and m ~= player.Character then
                        local hum = m:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                        if hum and hum.Health > 0 then
                            local r = getRoot(m)
                            if r then
                                local d = (r.Position - hrp.Position).Magnitude
                                if d < bestD then best = r; bestD = d end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
                return best
            end

            local function fireQ()
                if not ensureRemote() then return end
                DashRemote:FireServer({[1]={Dash=Enum.KeyCode.W, Key=Enum.KeyCode.Q, Goal="KeyPress"}})
                VirtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(true, Enum.KeyCode.Q, false, game)
                VirtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(false, Enum.KeyCode.Q, false, game)
            end

            local function forceJump(height)
                local char = player.Character
                if not char then return end
                local hrp = getRoot(char)
                if not hrp then return end
                pcall(function()
                    local cv = hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity
                    hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(cv.X, height or 50, cv.Z)
                end)
            end

            local function flip()
                local cf = cam.CFrame
                local lv = cf.LookVector
                cam.CFrame = CFrame.new(cf.Position, cf.Position + Vector3.new(-lv.X, lv.Y, -lv.Z))
            end

            local function executeOreoV2()
                if not _G.OreoEnabled or settings.onCooldown then return end
                local e = getClosestEnemy(10)
                if not e then return end

                task.delay(settings.kittyStartJump, function()
                    if not _G.OreoEnabled then return end
                    forceJump(60)
                end)

                task.delay(settings.kittyStart, function()
                    if not _G.OreoEnabled then return end
                    ensureRemote()
                    fireQ()
                    flip()
                    task.wait(settings.kittyWait)

                    local targetPart = e.Parent:FindFirstChild("Left Arm") or getRoot(e.Parent)
                    if not targetPart then return end

                    local t0 = tick()
                    local conn
                    conn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
                        if not _G.OreoEnabled or not targetPart.Parent then conn:Disconnect(); return end
                        if tick() - t0 >= settings.kittyDur then conn:Disconnect(); return end

                        local cf = cam.CFrame
                        local cp = cf.Position
                        local dir = Vector3.new(
                            targetPart.Position.X - cp.X, 0,
                            targetPart.Position.Z - cp.Z
                        )
                        if dir.Magnitude > 0 then
                            dir = dir.Unit
                            local targetYaw = math.atan2(dir.Z, dir.X)
                            local curYaw = math.atan2(cf.LookVector.Z, cf.LookVector.X)
                            local newYaw = curYaw + (targetYaw - curYaw) * settings.kittyCam
                            local pitch = math.asin(math.clamp(cf.LookVector.Y, -1, 1))
                            cam.CFrame = CFrame.new(cp, cp + Vector3.new(
                                math.cos(newYaw) * math.cos(pitch),
                                math.sin(pitch),
                                math.sin(newYaw) * math.cos(pitch)
                            ))
                        end
                    end)
                end)
            end

            local function hookCharacter(char)
                local hum = char:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 5)
                local anim = hum:WaitForChild("Animator", 5)
                anim.AnimationPlayed:Connect(function(track)
                    if not _G.OreoEnabled then return end
                    if not track.Animation then return end
                    local id = track.Animation.AnimationId
                    if UPPERCUT[id] then
                        task.spawn(executeOreoV2)
                    end
                end)
            end

            if player.Character then
                task.wait(0.1)
                hookCharacter(player.Character)
            end

            _G.OreoCharConn = player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
                char:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 5)
                task.wait(0.1)
                hookCharacter(char)
            end)
        end
    end,
})

AutoTechTab:Toggle({
    Title = "Twinnie v2 Utility",
    Value = false,
    Callback = function(isEnabled)
        -- Clear prior character connections/cleanup state
        if _G.Twin2CharConn then
            _G.Twin2CharConn:Disconnect()
            _G.Twin2CharConn = nil
        end

        -- Global control reference flag
        _G.Twin2Enabled = isEnabled

        if isEnabled then
            local Players = game:GetService("Players")
            local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
            local VirtualInputManager = game:GetService("VirtualInputManager")

            local player = Players.LocalPlayer

            local DashRemote
            local function ensureRemote()
                if not DashRemote then
                    pcall(function()
                        DashRemote = ReplicatedStorage.Resources.Brother["#Friend"].Communicate
                    end)
                end
                return DashRemote ~= nil
            end

            local UPPERCUT = {
                ["rbxassetid://10503381238"] = true,
                ["rbxassetid://13379003796"] = true,
            }

            local S = {
                twin2Jump   = 60,
                twin2Delay  = 0.24,
                twin2QDelay = 0,
            }

            local F = {
                twin2PressQ = true,
            }

            local function autoPressQ()
                if ensureRemote() then
                    pcall(function()
                        DashRemote:FireServer({[1] = {Dash = Enum.KeyCode.W, Key = Enum.KeyCode.Q, Goal = "KeyPress"}})
                    end)
                end
                
                pcall(function()
                    VirtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(true, Enum.KeyCode.Q, false, game)
                    task.wait(0.05)
                    VirtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(false, Enum.KeyCode.Q, false, game)
                end)
            end

            local function forceJump(h)
                local c = player.Character; if not c then return end
                local r = c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or c:FindFirstChild("Torso")
                if not r then return end
                pcall(function()
                    local cv = r.AssemblyLinearVelocity
                    r.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(cv.X, h or 50, cv.Z)
                end)
            end

            local function getClosestEnemy(maxDist)
                local char = player.Character; if not char then return nil end
                local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or char:FindFirstChild("Torso")
                if not hrp then return nil end

                local live = workspace:FindFirstChild("Live") or workspace
                local best, bestD = nil, maxDist

                for _, m in ipairs(live:GetChildren()) do
                    if m:IsA("Model") and m ~= char then
                        local hum = m:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                        local r   = m:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or m:FindFirstChild("Torso")
                        if hum and hum.Health > 0 and r then
                            local d = (r.Position - hrp.Position).Magnitude
                            if d < bestD then best = r; bestD = d end
                        end
                    end
                end
                return best
            end

            local animatorVersion = 0

            local function setupCharacter(char)
                animatorVersion = animatorVersion + 1
                local currentVersion = animatorVersion
                
                local hum = char:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 5)
                if not hum then return end
                local animator = hum:WaitForChild("Animator", 5)
                if not animator then return end

                animator.AnimationPlayed:Connect(function(track)
                    if not _G.Twin2Enabled then return end
                    if currentVersion ~= animatorVersion then return end
                    local anim = track.Animation
                    if not anim then return end

                    if UPPERCUT[anim.AnimationId] then
                        local enemy = getClosestEnemy(12)
                        if enemy then
                            task.delay(S.twin2Delay, function()
                                if not _G.Twin2Enabled then return end
                                if not player.Character or not player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then return end
                                forceJump(S.twin2Jump)
                                if F.twin2PressQ then
                                    task.delay(S.twin2QDelay, autoPressQ)
                                end
                            end)
                        end
                    end
                end)
            end

            if player.Character then 
                setupCharacter(player.Character) 
            end
            
            _G.Twin2CharConn = player.CharacterAdded:Connect(setupCharacter)
        end
    end,
})


AutoTechTab:Toggle({
    Title = "hunter slam",
    Desc = "make your hunter grasp finisher slam the player ar the last",
    Value = false,
    Callback = function(isEnabled)
        -- Clear prior character connections/cleanup state
        if _G.EmoteDashCharConn then
            _G.EmoteDashCharConn:Disconnect()
            _G.EmoteDashCharConn = nil
        end

        -- Global control reference flag
        _G.EmoteDashEnabled = isEnabled

        if isEnabled then
            local Players = game:GetService("Players")
            local Workspace = game:GetService("Workspace")

            local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
            local Camera = Workspace.CurrentCamera

            local TARGET_ANIMATION_ID = "12447247483"

            local isActionActive = false
            local loadedAnims = {}

            local function getCharacterAndHumanoid()
                local Character = LocalPlayer.Character
                local Humanoid = Character and Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                return Character, Humanoid
            end

            local function stopAllAnimations()
                local _, humanoid = getCharacterAndHumanoid()
                local animator = humanoid and humanoid:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator")
                if animator then
                    pcall(function()
                        for _, track in ipairs(animator:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()) do
                            track:Stop()
                        end
                    end)
                end
            end

            local function playAnimation(animationId)
                local _, humanoid = getCharacterAndHumanoid()
                if humanoid then
                    local animator = humanoid:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator") or Instance.new("Animator", humanoid)
                    local animation = loadedAnims[animationId] or Instance.new("Animation")
                    if not loadedAnims[animationId] then
                        animation.AnimationId = "rbxassetid://" .. tostring(animationId)
                        loadedAnims[animationId] = animation
                    end
                    local success, track = pcall(function() return animator:LoadAnimation(animation) end)
                    if success and track then
                        track.Priority = Enum.AnimationPriority.Action
                        track:Play()
                        pcall(function() track:AdjustSpeed(1.2) end)
                    end
                end
            end

            local function turnCamera(degrees)
                if not isActionActive then
                    isActionActive = true
                    local character, _ = getCharacterAndHumanoid()
                    local hrp = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    if hrp then
                        pcall(function()
                            hrp.CFrame = hrp.CFrame * CFrame.Angles(0, math.rad(degrees), 0)
                            local cameraPosition = Camera and Camera.CFrame.Position or hrp.Position + Vector3.new(0, 5, 0)
                            local cameraY = cameraPosition.Y
                            local offsetVector = cameraPosition - hrp.Position
                            local horizontalOffset = Vector3.new(offsetVector.X, 0, offsetVector.Z)
                            local rotatedOffset = CFrame.fromAxisAngle(Vector3.yAxis, math.rad(degrees)) * horizontalOffset
                            local newCameraLookAt = hrp.Position + rotatedOffset
                            if Camera and Camera:IsA("Camera") then
                                Camera.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(Vector3.new(newCameraLookAt.X, cameraY, newCameraLookAt.Z), hrp.Position)
                            end
                        end)
                    end
                    isActionActive = false
                end
            end

            local function applyBodyVelocity(directionX, distanceMultiplier, verticalForce, maxSpeed, duration)
                if not isActionActive then
                    isActionActive = true
                    local character, _ = getCharacterAndHumanoid()
                    local hrp = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    if hrp then
                        local bodyVelocity = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
                        local velocityVector = hrp.CFrame.RightVector * directionX * distanceMultiplier + Vector3.new(0, verticalForce, 0)
                        if velocityVector.Magnitude == 0 then velocityVector = Vector3.new(0, 1, 0) end
                        bodyVelocity.Velocity = velocityVector.Unit * maxSpeed
                        bodyVelocity.MaxForce = Vector3.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge)
                        bodyVelocity.Parent = hrp
                        task.delay(duration, function() pcall(function() bodyVelocity:Destroy() end) end)
                    end
                    isActionActive = false
                end
            end

            local function EMOTE_DASH_FUNCTION()
                if not _G.EmoteDashEnabled then return end
                stopAllAnimations()
                pcall(function() playAnimation(10480793962) end)
                pcall(function() turnCamera(90) end)
                pcall(function() applyBodyVelocity(1, 38, 8, 95, 0.27) end)
            end

            local function setupAnimationListener(character)
                if not character then return end
                local humanoid = character:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 5)
                local animator = humanoid and humanoid:WaitForChild("Animator", 5)
                
                if animator then
                    animator.AnimationPlayed:Connect(function(animationTrack)
                        if not _G.EmoteDashEnabled then return end
                        local animId = animationTrack.Animation and animationTrack.Animation.AnimationId
                        if animId and (animId:find(TARGET_ANIMATION_ID) or animId == "rbxassetid://" .. TARGET_ANIMATION_ID) then
                            EMOTE_DASH_FUNCTION()
                        end
                    end)
                end
            end

            if LocalPlayer.Character then
                setupAnimationListener(LocalPlayer.Character)
            end
            
            _G.EmoteDashCharConn = LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(setupAnimationListener)
        end
    end,
})


AutoTechTab:Toggle({
    Title = "FreezeTech",
    Value = false,
    Callback = function(isEnabled)
        -- Clear previous connections
        if _G.FreezeTechAnimationConn then 
            _G.FreezeTechAnimationConn:Disconnect() 
            _G.FreezeTechAnimationConn = nil 
        end
        if _G.FreezeTechCharAddedConn then 
            _G.FreezeTechCharAddedConn:Disconnect() 
            _G.FreezeTechCharAddedConn = nil 
        end
        if _G.FreezeTechLoopConn then
            _G.FreezeTechLoopConn:Disconnect()
            _G.FreezeTechLoopConn = nil
        end
        
        if isEnabled then
            local Players = game:GetService("Players")
            local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
            local VirtualInputManager = game:GetService("VirtualInputManager")
            local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer

            -- Configuration constants
            local TARGET_ANIMATION = "10503381238"
            local DISABLE_ANIMATION = "10479335397"
            local DASH_DELAY = 0.236
            local FREEZE_DELAY = 0.2
            local FREEZE_DURATION = 0.67
            local COOLDOWN_TIME = 5
            local DISABLE_DURATION = 5
            local TILT_ANGLE = 0

            -- Runtime states
            local cooldownActive = false
            local disabledTimerActive = false
            local nextAvailableTime = 0
            local disableUntilTime = 0

            -- Freeze Action Mechanics
            local function doFreeze(char, duration)
                local humanoid = char:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
                local root = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if not humanoid or not root then return end

                local freezePos = root.Position
                local freezeLook = root.CFrame.LookVector
                local start = tick()

                humanoid.AutoRotate = false
                humanoid.PlatformStand = true

                local bodyGyro = Instance.new("BodyGyro")
                bodyGyro.Parent = root
                bodyGyro.MaxTorque = Vector3.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge)
                bodyGyro.P = 50000
                bodyGyro.D = 1000

                local bodyPos = Instance.new("BodyPosition")
                bodyPos.Parent = root
                bodyPos.MaxForce = Vector3.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge)
                bodyPos.P = 50000
                bodyPos.D = 1000
                bodyPos.Position = freezePos

                if _G.FreezeTechLoopConn then _G.FreezeTechLoopConn:Disconnect() end

                _G.FreezeTechLoopConn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
                    if not _G.FreezeTechLoopConn or not char.Parent then
                        if _G.FreezeTechLoopConn then _G.FreezeTechLoopConn:Disconnect() _G.FreezeTechLoopConn = nil end
                        pcall(function() bodyGyro:Destroy() bodyPos:Destroy() end)
                        return
                    end

                    if tick() - start >= duration then
                        if _G.FreezeTechLoopConn then _G.FreezeTechLoopConn:Disconnect() _G.FreezeTechLoopConn = nil end
                        pcall(function() bodyGyro:Destroy() bodyPos:Destroy() end)
                        if humanoid and humanoid.Parent then
                            humanoid.PlatformStand = false
                            humanoid.AutoRotate = true
                        end
                        return
                    end

                    bodyPos.Position = freezePos
                    local targetCFrame = CFrame.lookAt(freezePos, freezePos + freezeLook) * CFrame.Angles(math.rad(35), 0, math.rad(TILT_ANGLE))
                    bodyGyro.CFrame = targetCFrame

                    root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                    root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
                    humanoid.PlatformStand = true
                end)
            end

            -- Process input trigger sequencing
            local function processTechSequence(char)
                cooldownActive = true
                nextAvailableTime = tick() + DASH_DELAY + FREEZE_DELAY + FREEZE_DURATION + COOLDOWN_TIME

                task.wait(DASH_DELAY)
                if not _G.FreezeTechAnimationConn or not char.Parent then cooldownActive = false return end

                -- Virtual key stroke for Dash Q
                VirtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(true, Enum.KeyCode.Q, false, game)
                task.wait(0.03)
                VirtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(false, Enum.KeyCode.Q, false, game)

                task.wait(FREEZE_DELAY)
                if char.Parent and _G.FreezeTechAnimationConn then
                    doFreeze(char, FREEZE_DURATION)
                end

                task.wait(FREEZE_DURATION + COOLDOWN_TIME)
                cooldownActive = false
            end

            -- Character Listener Configuration
            local function listenToCharacter(character)
                local humanoid = character:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 10)
                local animator = humanoid and humanoid:WaitForChild("Animator", 10)
                if not animator then return end

                if _G.FreezeTechAnimationConn then _G.FreezeTechAnimationConn:Disconnect() end

                _G.FreezeTechAnimationConn = animator.AnimationPlayed:Connect(function(track)
                    if not _G.FreezeTechAnimationConn then return end
                    local anim = track.Animation
                    if not anim then return end

                    local animId = tostring(anim.AnimationId)
                    local currentTime = tick()

                    -- Check Disable Condition Animation
                    if animId:find(DISABLE_ANIMATION) then
                        if currentTime > disableUntilTime then
                            disableUntilTime = currentTime + DISABLE_DURATION
                        end
                        return
                    end

                    -- Check Action Target Animation
                    if animId:find(TARGET_ANIMATION) then
                        if cooldownActive or currentTime < nextAvailableTime or currentTime < disableUntilTime then return end

                        task.spawn(function()
                            processTechSequence(character)
                        end)
                    end
                end)
            end

            if LocalPlayer.Character then
                listenToCharacter(LocalPlayer.Character)
            end
            
            _G.FreezeTechCharAddedConn = LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(listenToCharacter)
        else
            -- Cleanup character mechanics state upon disabling toggle
            pcall(function()
                local character = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer.Character
                local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                if humanoid then
                    humanoid.PlatformStand = false
                    humanoid.AutoRotate = true
                end
            end)
        end
    end,
})

AutoTechTab:Toggle({
    Title = "Auto Void Kill",
    Value = false,
    Callback = function(isEnabled)
        local Players = game:GetService("Players")
        local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer

        _G.AutoVoidKillEnabled = isEnabled

        local animations = {
            ["rbxassetid://12273188754"] = 1.311,
            ["rbxassetid://12296113986"] = 1.2,
        }

        local function ifind(t, a)
            for i, v in pairs(t) do
                if i == a then
                    return i
                end
            end
            return false
        end

        local function startVoidLoop()
            if _G.AutoVoidThread then
                pcall(function()
                    coroutine.close(_G.AutoVoidThread)
                end)
                _G.AutoVoidThread = nil
            end

            workspace.FallenPartsDestroyHeight = 0/0

            _G.AutoVoidThread = coroutine.create(function()
                local dothetech = false
                local lastcf

                while task.wait() do
                    if not _G.AutoVoidKillEnabled then break end

                    local character = LocalPlayer.Character
                    if character then
                        local hum = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                        local root = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                        local animate = hum and hum:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator")

                        if animate and root then
                            for _, v in pairs(animate:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()) do
                                if v.Animation and ifind(animations, v.Animation.AnimationId) then
                                    task.wait(animations[v.Animation.AnimationId])
                                    dothetech = true
                                    lastcf = root.CFrame

                                    local stopConn
                                    stopConn = v.Stopped:Connect(function()
                                        dothetech = false
                                        if stopConn then stopConn:Disconnect() end
                                    end)

                                    repeat
                                        task.wait()
                                        if workspace.CurrentCamera then
                                            workspace.CurrentCamera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Scriptable
                                        end
                                        if root then
                                            root.CFrame = CFrame.new(-1200, -460, 67)
                                            root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                                            root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
                                        end
                                    until not dothetech or not _G.AutoVoidKillEnabled

                                    task.wait(0.1)

                                    if root and lastcf then
                                        root.CFrame = lastcf
                                    end
                                    
                                    if workspace.CurrentCamera then
                                        workspace.CurrentCamera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Custom
                                        if hum then
                                            workspace.CurrentCamera.CameraSubject = hum
                                        end
                                    end

                                    task.wait(1)
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)

            coroutine.resume(_G.AutoVoidThread)
        end

        if isEnabled then
            startVoidLoop()
        else
            if _G.AutoVoidThread then
                pcall(function()
                    coroutine.close(_G.AutoVoidThread)
                end)
                _G.AutoVoidThread = nil
            end
            if workspace.CurrentCamera and LocalPlayer.Character then
                local hum = LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                workspace.CurrentCamera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Custom
                if hum then
                    workspace.CurrentCamera.CameraSubject = hum
                end
            end
        end
    end,
})


AutoTechTab:Toggle({
    Title = "Auto Down Slam",
    Value = false,
    Callback = function(isEnabled)
        -- Clear previous active lifecycle connections
        if _G.SlamAnimConn then
            _G.SlamAnimConn:Disconnect()
            _G.SlamAnimConn = nil
        end
        if _G.SlamCharConn then
            _G.SlamCharConn:Disconnect()
            _G.SlamCharConn = nil
        end

        if isEnabled then
            local Players = game:GetService("Players")
            local TweenService = game:GetService("TweenService")
            local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer

            -- Targeted down slam animation hash list
            local slamAnimations = {
                ["rbxassetid://10469639222"] = true,
                ["rbxassetid://13532604085"] = true,
                ["rbxassetid://13295919399"] = true,
                ["rbxassetid://13378751717"] = true,
                ["rbxassetid://14001963401"] = true,
                ["rbxassetid://15240176873"] = true,
                ["rbxassetid://16515448089"] = true,
                ["rbxassetid://17889471098"] = true,
                ["rbxassetid://104895379416342"] = true
            }

            -- Spatial evaluation utility to look for adjacent targets
            local function isNearEnemy(character, rootPart)
                local liveFolder = workspace:FindFirstChild("Live")
                if not liveFolder then return false end
                
                for _, v in ipairs(liveFolder:GetChildren()) do
                    if v:IsA("Model") and v ~= character then
                        local enemyRoot = v:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                        if enemyRoot and (enemyRoot.Position - rootPart.Position).Magnitude <= 15 then
                            if Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(v) or v.Name == "Weakest Dummy" then
                                return true
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
                return false
            end

            -- Execute jump-evasion dynamic override sequence
            local function performSlamJump(character, rootPart, humanoid)
                if not (rootPart and humanoid) then return end
                
                if isNearEnemy(character, rootPart) then
                    local tween = TweenService:Create(
                        rootPart,
                        TweenInfo.new(0.35, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
                        {CFrame = rootPart.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, 6, 0)}
                    )
                    tween:Play()
                    
                    local forceStates = {
                        Enum.HumanoidStateType.PlatformStanding,
                        Enum.HumanoidStateType.Freefall,
                        Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp
                    }
                    for _, state in ipairs(forceStates) do
                        if humanoid:GetState() == state then
                            humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics)
                            task.wait()
                        end
                    end
                    
                    humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping)
                end
            end

            -- Initialize tracking hooks onto the local humanoid
            local function setupCharacter()
                local char = LocalPlayer.Character or LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Wait()
                task.wait(0.5)
                
                local humanoid = char:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 5) or char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                local rootPart = char:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 5) or char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                
                if _G.SlamAnimConn then
                    _G.SlamAnimConn:Disconnect()
                end
                
                if humanoid and rootPart then
                    _G.SlamAnimConn = humanoid.AnimationPlayed:Connect(function(animTrack)
                        if animTrack.Animation and slamAnimations[animTrack.Animation.AnimationId] then
                            performSlamJump(char, rootPart, humanoid)
                        end
                    end)
                end
            end

            -- Active connection setup
            setupCharacter()
            _G.SlamCharConn = LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(setupCharacter)
        end
    end,
})


kibaSection:Toggle({
    Title = "Kiba v1",
    Value = _G.KibaEnabled or false,
    Callback = function(isEnabled)
        _G.KibaEnabled = isEnabled
        
        -- Cleanup connections when disabled
        if not isEnabled then
            if _G.KibaConnection then _G.KibaConnection:Disconnect() end
            return
        end

        -- Variables
        local Players = game:GetService("Players")
        local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
        local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
        local Character = LocalPlayer.Character or LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Wait()
        local HRP = Character:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local Humanoid = Character:WaitForChild("Humanoid")
        local Communicate = Character:WaitForChild("Communicate")
        
        local ANIM_ID = "10503381238" -- Replace if needed
        local COOLDOWN = 5.0
        local lastDash = 0
        local isDashing = false

        -- Core Logic
        local function getTarget()
            local target, shortest = nil, 20 -- Range
            for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                if p ~= LocalPlayer and p.Character then
                    local hrp = p.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    if hrp and (hrp.Position - HRP.Position).Magnitude < shortest then
                        shortest = (hrp.Position - HRP.Position).Magnitude
                        target = hrp
                    end
                end
            end
            return target
        end

        local function performDash(target)
            if isDashing then return end
            isDashing = true
            
            -- Fire Remote
            Communicate:FireServer({Dash = Enum.KeyCode.W, Key = Enum.KeyCode.Q, Goal = "KeyPress"})
            
            -- Dash logic
            local oldCFrame = HRP.CFrame
            HRP.CFrame = CFrame.new(HRP.Position, Vector3.new(target.Position.X, HRP.Position.Y, target.Position.Z))
            
            task.wait(0.55)
            
            HRP.CFrame = oldCFrame
            isDashing = false
        end

        -- Monitor Animations
        _G.KibaConnection = Humanoid.AnimationPlayed:Connect(function(track)
            if not _G.KibaEnabled then return end
            
            local id = tostring(track.Animation.AnimationId):match("%d+$")
            if id == ANIM_ID and (tick() - lastDash) > COOLDOWN then
                local target = getTarget()
                if target then
                    lastDash = tick()
                    performDash(target)
                end
            end
        end)
    end,
})

kibaSection:Toggle({
    Title = "Kiba Tech V2",
    Value = _G.KibaV3Enabled or false,
    Callback = function(isEnabled)
        _G.KibaV3Enabled = isEnabled
        
        -- Master disconnect/cleanup wrapper routine
        local function clearActiveTech()
            if _G.KibaAnimConnection then _G.KibaAnimConnection:Disconnect() _G.KibaAnimConnection = nil end
            if _G.KibaCharConnection then _G.KibaCharConnection:Disconnect() _G.KibaCharConnection = nil end
            if _G.KibaRenderConnection then _G.KibaRenderConnection:Disconnect() _G.KibaRenderConnection = nil end
            
            -- Clear physical instances and tags
            local player = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer
            local char = player.Character
            local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if hrp then
                for _, child in ipairs(hrp:GetChildren()) do
                    if child:IsA("Attachment") or child:IsA("AlignPosition") or child:IsA("AlignOrientation") or child.Name == "HasSnapped" then
                        child:Destroy()
                    end
                end
            end
            local oldFollowPart = workspace:FindFirstChild("FollowPart")
            if oldFollowPart then oldFollowPart:Destroy() end
        end

        -- Erase remnants from previous toggle sessions
        clearActiveTech()
        if not isEnabled then return end

        -- Local Environment Declarations
        local Players = game:GetService('Players')
        local RunService = game:GetService('RunService')
        local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer

        -- Configuration Tables
        local DASH_ANIMS = { ['10503381238'] = true, ['13379003796'] = true }
        local BURST_ANIMS = { ['10479335397'] = true, ['13380255751'] = true }
        local MAX_RANGE = 10
        local isOnCooldown = false

        -- Instance Trackers
        local alignPos, alignOrient, followPart

        -- Specific structural cleanup
        local function clearAlignment()
            if alignPos then alignPos:Destroy() alignPos = nil end
            if alignOrient then alignOrient:Destroy() alignOrient = nil end
            if _G.KibaRenderConnection then _G.KibaRenderConnection:Disconnect() _G.KibaRenderConnection = nil end
            if followPart then followPart:Destroy() followPart = nil end

            local char = LocalPlayer.Character
            local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if hrp then
                for _, child in ipairs(hrp:GetChildren()) do
                    if child:IsA('Attachment') or child.Name == 'HasSnapped' then
                        child:Destroy()
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        -- Creates physical alignment logic towards target
        local function startFollow(targetRoot)
            clearAlignment()
            local char = LocalPlayer.Character
            local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if not hrp then return end

            followPart = Instance.new('Part')
            followPart.Size = Vector3.new(0.5, 0.5, 0.5)
            followPart.Transparency = 1
            followPart.Anchored = true
            followPart.CanCollide = false
            followPart.Name = 'FollowPart'
            followPart.Parent = workspace

            local att0_pos = Instance.new('Attachment', hrp)
            local att1_pos = Instance.new('Attachment', followPart)
            local att0_rot = Instance.new('Attachment', hrp)
            local att1_rot = Instance.new('Attachment', followPart)

            alignPos = Instance.new('AlignPosition')
            alignPos.Attachment0 = att0_pos
            alignPos.Attachment1 = att1_pos
            alignPos.RigidityEnabled = true
            alignPos.Responsiveness = 200
            alignPos.MaxForce = math.huge
            alignPos.Parent = hrp

            alignOrient = Instance.new('AlignOrientation')
            alignOrient.Attachment0 = att0_rot
            alignOrient.Attachment1 = att1_rot
            alignOrient.RigidityEnabled = true
            alignOrient.Responsiveness = 100
            alignOrient.MaxTorque = math.huge
            alignOrient.Parent = hrp

            _G.KibaRenderConnection = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
                if targetRoot and targetRoot.Parent and hrp and hrp.Parent then
                    local offsetCFrame = targetRoot.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0, 0) * CFrame.Angles(math.rad(55), 0, 0)
                    followPart.CFrame = offsetCFrame

                    if not hrp:FindFirstChild('HasSnapped') then
                        hrp.CFrame = offsetCFrame
                        local snapTag = Instance.new('BoolValue')
                        snapTag.Name = 'HasSnapped'
                        snapTag.Parent = hrp
                    end
                else
                    clearAlignment()
                end
            end)
        end

        -- Scans workspace environment targets
        local function getClosestTarget()
            local char = LocalPlayer.Character
            local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if not hrp then return nil end

            local closestTarget = nil
            local shortestDistance = MAX_RANGE
            local liveFolder = workspace:FindFirstChild("Live")
            if not liveFolder then return nil end

            for _, child in ipairs(liveFolder:GetChildren()) do
                if child:IsA('Model') and child ~= char then
                    local targetHrp = child:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart')
                    local targetHum = child:FindFirstChildOfClass('Humanoid')

                    if targetHrp and targetHum and targetHum.Health > 0 then
                        local distance = (targetHrp.Position - hrp.Position).Magnitude
                        if distance <= shortestDistance then
                            if child.Name == 'Weakest Dummy' or Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(child) then
                                closestTarget = targetHrp
                                shortestDistance = distance
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
            return closestTarget
        end

        -- Internal execution timing loop
        local function runCooldownLogic(duration)
            isOnCooldown = true
            task.delay(duration, function()
                isOnCooldown = false
            end)
        end

        -- Tracks ongoing player animations
        local function onAnimationPlayed(track)
            if not _G.KibaV3Enabled or isOnCooldown then return end
            local animId = string.match(track.Animation.AnimationId, '%d+')
            if not animId then return end

            if BURST_ANIMS[animId] then
                isOnCooldown = true
                task.delay(0.8, function()
                    runCooldownLogic(4.6)
                end)
            elseif DASH_ANIMS[animId] then
                isOnCooldown = true
                task.delay(0.32, function()
                    local char = LocalPlayer.Character
                    local communicate = char and char:FindFirstChild('Communicate')
                    if communicate then
                        pcall(function()
                            communicate:FireServer({
                                Dash = Enum.KeyCode.W,
                                Key = Enum.KeyCode.Q,
                                Goal = 'KeyPress',
                            })
                        end)
                    end

                    local target = getClosestTarget()
                    if target then
                        startFollow(target)
                        task.delay(0.5, clearAlignment)
                    end
                end)
                task.delay(0.8, function()
                    runCooldownLogic(4.6)
                end)
            end
        end

        -- Safe initialization for character state changes
        local function setupCharacter(character)
            if not character then return end
            local humanoid = character:WaitForChild('Humanoid', 5)
            if not humanoid then return end
            
            if _G.KibaAnimConnection then _G.KibaAnimConnection:Disconnect() end
            _G.KibaAnimConnection = humanoid.AnimationPlayed:Connect(onAnimationPlayed)
        end

        if LocalPlayer.Character then setupCharacter(LocalPlayer.Character) end
        _G.KibaCharConnection = LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
            task.wait(0.1)
            setupCharacter(char)
        end)
    end,
})

kibaSection:Toggle({
    Title = "Kiba Tech V3",
    Value = false,
    Callback = function(isEnabled)
        _G.KibaTechV4Enabled = isEnabled

        -- Safely clean up background tasks and listeners if turned off
        if not isEnabled then
            if _G.CleanupKibaTech then
                _G.CleanupKibaTech()
            end
            return
        end

        -- Services
        local Players = game:GetService('Players')
        local RunService = game:GetService('RunService')
        local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer

        -- Configuration Maps (Animation IDs)
        local DASH_ANIMATIONS = {
            ['10503381238'] = true,
            ['13379003796'] = true,
        }
        local SPECIAL_ANIMATIONS = {
            ['10479335397'] = true,
            ['13380255751'] = true,
        }

        -- Script State Variables
        local character = LocalPlayer.Character or LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Wait()
        local humanoid = character:WaitForChild('Humanoid', 5)
        local rootPart = character:WaitForChild('HumanoidRootPart', 5)

        local maxTargetDistance = 35 
        local isExecuting = false

        -- Connection Trackers
        local animationConnection = nil
        local lockOnRenderConnection = nil
        local characterAddedConnection = nil
        local characterRemovingConnection = nil

        -- Clean up tracking positions and alignment physics objects
        local function cleanupLockOn()
            if lockOnRenderConnection then
                lockOnRenderConnection:Disconnect()
                lockOnRenderConnection = nil
            end

            if rootPart then
                for _, child in ipairs(rootPart:GetChildren()) do
                    if child:IsA('AlignPosition') or child:IsA('AlignOrientation') or child:IsA('Attachment') or child.Name == 'HasSnapped' then
                        child:Destroy()
                    end
                end
            end

            local followPart = workspace:FindFirstChild('KibaFollowPart')
            if followPart then
                followPart:Destroy()
            end
        end

        -- Find the closest valid player character or Weakest Dummy target
        local function getClosestTarget()
            local closestPart = nil
            local currentMaxDist = maxTargetDistance
            local liveFolder = workspace:FindFirstChild('Live')

            if liveFolder then
                for _, target in ipairs(liveFolder:GetChildren()) do
                    if target:IsA('Model') and target ~= character then
                        local targetRoot = target:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart')
                        local targetHumanoid = target:FindFirstChildOfClass('Humanoid')

                        if targetRoot and targetHumanoid and targetHumanoid.Health > 0 then
                            local originPos = rootPart and rootPart.Position or Vector3.zero
                            local distance = (targetRoot.Position - originPos).Magnitude

                            if distance <= currentMaxDist then
                                if target.Name == 'Weakest Dummy' or Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(target) then
                                    currentMaxDist = distance
                                    closestPart = targetRoot
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
            return closestPart
        end

        -- Evaluator for tracked animation sequences
        local function onAnimationPlayed(animationTrack)
            if not _G.KibaTechV4Enabled or isExecuting then return end
            if not animationTrack or not animationTrack.Animation then return end

            local animId = tostring(string.match(animationTrack.Animation.AnimationId or '', '%d+'))
            if animId == '' then return end

            if SPECIAL_ANIMATIONS[animId] then
                isExecuting = true
                task.delay(0.8, function()
                    isExecuting = false
                end)
            elseif DASH_ANIMATIONS[animId] then
                isExecuting = true
                
                task.delay(0.32, function()
                    if not _G.KibaTechV4Enabled then return end

                    -- Remote Parameter formatting
                    pcall(function()
