                        local communicate = character and character:FindFirstChild('Communicate')
                        if communicate then
                            communicate:FireServer({
                                Dash = Enum.KeyCode.W,
                                Key = Enum.KeyCode.Q,
                                Goal = 'KeyPress',
                            })
                        end
                    end)

                    local target = getClosestTarget()
                    if target then
                        cleanupLockOn()

                        local followPart = Instance.new('Part')
                        followPart.Size = Vector3.new(0.5, 0.5, 0.5)
                        followPart.Transparency = 1
                        followPart.Anchored = true
                        followPart.CanCollide = false
                        followPart.Name = 'KibaFollowPart'
                        followPart.Parent = workspace

                        local att0_Pos = Instance.new('Attachment', rootPart)
                        local att1_Pos = Instance.new('Attachment', followPart)
                        local att0_Ori = Instance.new('Attachment', rootPart)
                        local att1_Ori = Instance.new('Attachment', followPart)

                        local alignPosition = Instance.new('AlignPosition')
                        alignPosition.Attachment0 = att0_Pos
                        alignPosition.Attachment1 = att1_Pos
                        alignPosition.RigidityEnabled = true
                        alignPosition.Responsiveness = 100
                        alignPosition.MaxForce = math.huge
                        alignPosition.Parent = rootPart

                        local alignOrientation = Instance.new('AlignOrientation')
                        alignOrientation.Attachment0 = att0_Ori
                        alignOrientation.Attachment1 = att1_Ori
                        alignOrientation.RigidityEnabled = true
                        alignOrientation.Responsiveness = 200
                        alignOrientation.MaxTorque = math.huge
                        alignOrientation.Parent = rootPart

                        lockOnRenderConnection = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
                            if not _G.KibaTechV4Enabled then cleanupLockOn() return end
                            
                            if target and target.Parent then
                                local calculatedCFrame = target.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 1, 0) * CFrame.Angles(math.rad(55), 0, 0)
                                followPart.CFrame = calculatedCFrame

                                if rootPart and not rootPart:FindFirstChild('HasSnapped') and rootPart:IsDescendantOf(workspace) then
                                    rootPart.CFrame = calculatedCFrame

                                    local snapMarker = Instance.new('BoolValue')
                                    snapMarker.Name = 'HasSnapped'
                                    snapMarker.Parent = rootPart
                                end
                            else
                                cleanupLockOn()
                            end
                        end)
                        
                        task.delay(0.5, cleanupLockOn)
                    end
                end)

                task.delay(1.2, function()
                    isExecuting = false
                end)
            end
        end

        -- Character Environment Initialization Routine
        local function initializeCharacterState()
            cleanupLockOn()
            if animationConnection then
                animationConnection:Disconnect()
                animationConnection = nil
            end

            character = LocalPlayer.Character or LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Wait()
            humanoid = character:WaitForChild('Humanoid', 5)
            rootPart = character:WaitForChild('HumanoidRootPart', 5)

            if humanoid then
                animationConnection = humanoid.AnimationPlayed:Connect(onAnimationPlayed)
            end
        end

        -- Run initialization
        initializeCharacterState()

        characterAddedConnection = LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
            task.wait(0.1)
            if not _G.KibaTechV4Enabled then return end
            initializeCharacterState()
        end)

        characterRemovingConnection = LocalPlayer.CharacterRemoving:Connect(function()
            cleanupLockOn()
            if animationConnection then
                animationConnection:Disconnect()
                animationConnection = nil
            end
        end)

        -- Dynamic cleanup registry
        _G.CleanupKibaTech = function()
            cleanupLockOn()
            if animationConnection then animationConnection:Disconnect() animationConnection = nil end
            if characterAddedConnection then characterAddedConnection:Disconnect() characterAddedConnection = nil end
            if characterRemovingConnection then characterRemovingConnection:Disconnect() characterRemovingConnection = nil end
            _G.CleanupKibaTech = nil
        end
    end,
})

kibaSection:Toggle({
    Title = "kibaz",
    Value = false,
    Callback = function(isEnabled)
        -- Clear previous connections
        if _G.StickDashMainConnections then
            for _, conn in ipairs(_G.StickDashMainConnections) do
                if conn and conn.Disconnect then
                    pcall(function() conn:Disconnect() end)
                end
            end
        end
        _G.StickDashMainConnections = {}

        if _G.StickDashRenderConn then
            _G.StickDashRenderConn:Disconnect()
            _G.StickDashRenderConn = nil
        end
        if _G.StickDashMovementConn then
            _G.StickDashMovementConn:Disconnect()
            _G.StickDashMovementConn = nil
        end

        if isEnabled then
            local Players = game:GetService("Players")
            local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
            local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer

            -- Configuration Constants
            local TARGET_ANIMATION = "10503381238"
            local DETECT_RADIUS = math.huge
            local ATTACK_COOLDOWN = 7
            local THROTTLE_INTERVAL = 0.35

            -- Runtime State References
            local localCharacter = nil
            local localHumanoid = nil
            local localRootPart = nil
            local lastTriggerTimestamp = 0
            local actionDebounceFlag = false

            -- Internal Utility: Safely retrieve the closest target Model
            local function getClosestEnemyTarget()
                if not localRootPart then return nil end
                local closestModel, shortestDistance = nil, DETECT_RADIUS

                for _, descendant in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
                    if descendant:IsA("Model") and descendant ~= localCharacter and descendant:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
                        local targetRoot = descendant.HumanoidRootPart
                        local success, distance = pcall(function()
                            return (localRootPart.Position - targetRoot.Position).Magnitude
                        end)
                        if success and distance and distance < shortestDistance then
                            shortestDistance = distance
                            closestModel = descendant
                        end
                    end
                end
                return closestModel
            end

            -- Internal Utility: Fire remote packets for actions & cleanup
            local function executeNetworkDashPackets()
                task.delay(0.1, function()
                    pcall(function()
                        if localCharacter and localCharacter:FindFirstChild("Communicate") then
                            localCharacter.Communicate:FireServer({
                                Dash = Enum.KeyCode.W,
                                Key = Enum.KeyCode.Q,
                                Goal = "KeyPress"
                            })
                        end
                    end)
                end)

                -- Dynamic fallback detection for explicit movement values
                local function findNilInstance(name, className)
                    if type(getnilinstances) ~= "function" then return nil end
                    for _, instance in ipairs(getnilinstances()) do
                        if instance.ClassName == className and instance.Name == name then
                            return instance
                        end
                    end
                    return nil
                end

                pcall(function()
                    if localCharacter and localCharacter:FindFirstChild("Communicate") then
                        localCharacter.Communicate:FireServer({
                            Goal = "delete bv",
                            BV = findNilInstance("moveme", "BodyVelocity")
                        })
                    end
                end)
            end

            -- Main Action Processing Loop
            local function runStickDashSequence()
                if not (localCharacter and localHumanoid and localRootPart) then return end
                local targetModel = getClosestEnemyTarget()
                if not targetModel then return end

                local targetRoot = targetModel:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if not targetRoot then return end

                -- Backup original values
                local originalSpecs = {
                    WalkSpeed = localHumanoid.WalkSpeed,
                    JumpPower = localHumanoid.JumpPower,
                    PlatformStand = localHumanoid.PlatformStand,
                    AutoRotate = localHumanoid.AutoRotate
                }

                -- Anchor & Stop velocities instantly
                local function stopMovement()
                    pcall(function()
                        localHumanoid.WalkSpeed = 0
                        localHumanoid.JumpPower = 0
                        localHumanoid.PlatformStand = true
                        localHumanoid.AutoRotate = false
                        if localRootPart then
                            localRootPart.Velocity = Vector3.zero
                            localRootPart.RotVelocity = Vector3.zero
                        end
                    end)

                    -- Wipe background velocity modifiers
                    for _, object in ipairs(localCharacter:GetDescendants()) do
                        local cName = object.ClassName
                        if cName == "BodyVelocity" or cName == "BodyPosition" or cName == "BodyGyro" or 
                           cName == "VectorForce" or cName == "AlignPosition" or cName == "AlignOrientation" or 
                           cName == "LinearVelocity" or cName == "AngularVelocity" then
                            pcall(function() object:Destroy() end)
                        end
                    end

                    _G.StickDashMovementConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
                        if localRootPart then
                            pcall(function()
                                localRootPart.Velocity = Vector3.zero
                                localRootPart.RotVelocity = Vector3.zero
                            end)
                        end
                        if localHumanoid then
                            pcall(function() localHumanoid.WalkSpeed = 0 end)
                        end
                    end)
                end

                -- Restore physics specs to character
                local function restoreMovement()
                    if _G.StickDashMovementConn then
                        pcall(function() _G.StickDashMovementConn:Disconnect() end)
                        _G.StickDashMovementConn = nil
                    end
                    pcall(function()
                        if localHumanoid then
                            localHumanoid.WalkSpeed = originalSpecs.WalkSpeed or 16
                            localHumanoid.JumpPower = originalSpecs.JumpPower or 50
                            localHumanoid.PlatformStand = originalSpecs.PlatformStand or false
                            localHumanoid.AutoRotate = originalSpecs.AutoRotate or true
                        end
                        if localRootPart then
                            localRootPart.Velocity = Vector3.zero
                            localRootPart.RotVelocity = Vector3.zero
                        end
                    end)
                end

                stopMovement()
                pcall(function() localHumanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics) end)
                localRootPart.CFrame = localRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.Angles(math.rad(85), 0, 0)

                local sequenceStart = tick()
                if _G.StickDashRenderConn then _G.StickDashRenderConn:Disconnect() end

                _G.StickDashRenderConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
                    if tick() - sequenceStart < 0.7 then
                        local pass, nextCFrame = pcall(function()
                            return CFrame.new(targetRoot.Position - targetRoot.CFrame.LookVector * 0.3) * CFrame.Angles(math.rad(85), 0, 0)
                        end)
                        if pass and nextCFrame and localRootPart then
                            localRootPart.CFrame = nextCFrame
                        end
                    else
                        if _G.StickDashRenderConn then _G.StickDashRenderConn:Disconnect() _G.StickDashRenderConn = nil end
                    end
                end)

                task.delay(0.18, function() pcall(executeNetworkDashPackets) end)
                task.delay(0.3, function()
                    pcall(function() localHumanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp) end)
                end)
                task.delay(0.7 + 0.3, function() pcall(restoreMovement) end)
            end

            -- Universal Trigger Evaluator
            local function evaluateTriggerCondition()
                if actionDebounceFlag then return end
                if tick() - lastTriggerTimestamp >= THROTTLE_INTERVAL then
                    lastTriggerTimestamp = tick()
                    
                    actionDebounceFlag = true
                    task.spawn(function()
                        pcall(runStickDashSequence)
                    end)
                    task.delay(ATTACK_COOLDOWN, function()
                        actionDebounceFlag = false
                    end)
                end
            end

            -- Hook Event Observers
            local function handleTrackActivation(track)
                local animationObj = track.Animation
                if animationObj then
                    local idStr = tostring(animationObj.AnimationId or "")
                    if idStr:find(TARGET_ANIMATION, 1, true) then
                        evaluateTriggerCondition()
                    end
                end
            end

            local function listenToCharacter(character)
                -- Flush sub-level configurations
                localCharacter = character
                localHumanoid = character:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 5) or character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                localRootPart = character:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 5) or character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

                if localHumanoid and localRootPart then
                    local s1, c1 = pcall(function() return localHumanoid.AnimationPlayed:Connect(handleTrackActivation) end)
                    if s1 and c1 then table.insert(_G.StickDashMainConnections, c1) end

                    local animator = localHumanoid:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator")
                    if animator then
                        local s2, c2 = pcall(function() return animator.AnimationPlayed:Connect(handleTrackActivation) end)
                        if s2 and c2 then table.insert(_G.StickDashMainConnections, c2) end
                    end

                    local c3 = character.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(descendant)
                        if descendant:IsA("Animation") then
                            local idStr = tostring(descendant.AnimationId or "")
                            if idStr:find(TARGET_ANIMATION, 1, true) then
                                evaluateTriggerCondition()
                            end
                        end
                    end)
                    table.insert(_G.StickDashMainConnections, c3)
                end
            end

            -- Initialize character binding connections
            if LocalPlayer.Character then
                pcall(listenToCharacter, LocalPlayer.Character)
            end

            local spawnConnection = LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
                task.wait(0.1)
                pcall(listenToCharacter, char)
            end)
            table.insert(_G.StickDashMainConnections, spawnConnection)
        else
            -- Cleanup character physical states upon turning toggle off
            pcall(function()
                local character = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer.Character
                local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                if humanoid then
                    humanoid.PlatformStand = false
                    humanoid.AutoRotate = true
                end
            end)
        end
    end,
})

-- Shared State & Configuration
local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
local TweenService = game:GetService("TweenService")
local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer

local KNOWN_WALL_COMBO_IDS = {
    ["15955393872"] = "Saitama",
    ["16310343179"] = "Garou",
    ["15943915877"] = "Genos",
    ["15997042291"] = "Blade Master",
    ["15944317351"] = "Metal Bat",
    ["16311141574"] = "Tatsumaki",
    ["18181589384"] = "Martial Artist",
    ["16023456135"] = "Deadly Ninja",
    ["18903642853"] = "Sorcerer / Gojo"
}

local currentPreset = "None"
local animationConnection = nil

local function isWallComboAnim(animId)
    local cleanId = tostring(animId):match("%d+")
    if not cleanId then return false end
    return KNOWN_WALL_COMBO_IDS[cleanId] ~= nil
end

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- PRESET 1: SHINJI LOGIC
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
local SHINJI_AUDIO_URL = "https://github.com/Kenjihin69/Kenjihin69/blob/main/Screen_Recording_20250121_202931_20250121_203018_20250121_204216_20250121_204307.mp3?raw=true"
local SHINJI_AUDIO_FILE_NAME = "shinji_spin_wall_combo"

local function playShinjiSound()
    if not isfile(SHINJI_AUDIO_FILE_NAME .. ".mp3") then
        writefile(SHINJI_AUDIO_FILE_NAME .. ".mp3", game:HttpGet(SHINJI_AUDIO_URL))
    end

    local sound = Instance.new("Sound")
    sound.SoundId = (getcustomasset or getsynasset)(SHINJI_AUDIO_FILE_NAME .. ".mp3")
    sound.Parent = workspace
    sound.Volume = 5
    sound.TimePosition = 0
    sound:Play()

    sound.Ended:Connect(function()
        sound:Destroy()
    end)
end

local function executeShinjiPreset(track)
    local animId = track.Animation.AnimationId:match("%d+")
    if not animId or not KNOWN_WALL_COMBO_IDS[animId] then return end

    local character = LocalPlayer.Character
    if not character then return end
    local humanoid = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not humanoid then return end

    for _, playingTrack in ipairs(humanoid:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()) do
        playingTrack:Stop()
    end

    local anim1 = Instance.new("Animation")
    anim1.AnimationId = "rbxassetid://18897648446"
    local track1 = humanoid:LoadAnimation(anim1)
    track1:Play()
    track1:AdjustSpeed(0)
    track1.TimePosition = 3.8
    track1:AdjustSpeed(1)

    task.spawn(playShinjiSound)
    task.wait(0.4)

    local anim2 = Instance.new("Animation")
    anim2.AnimationId = "rbxassetid://76530443909428"
    local track2 = humanoid:LoadAnimation(anim2)
    track1:Stop()
    track2:Play()
    track2:AdjustSpeed(0)
    track2.TimePosition = 0
    track2:AdjustSpeed(3)

    local constantRes = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Resources")
        and ReplicatedStorage.Resources:FindFirstChild("Sunrise")
        and ReplicatedStorage.Resources.Sunrise:FindFirstChild("Constant2")
        and ReplicatedStorage.Resources.Sunrise.Constant2:FindFirstChild("Part")
        and ReplicatedStorage.Resources.Sunrise.Constant2.Part:FindFirstChild("Constant")

    if constantRes and character:FindFirstChild("Torso") then
        local partClone = constantRes:Clone()
        partClone.Parent = character.Torso

        for _, child in ipairs(partClone:GetChildren()) do
            if child:IsA("ParticleEmitter") then
                child.Color = ColorSequence.new(Color3.fromRGB(0, 125, 255))
                child:Emit(20)
                child.Enabled = true
            end
        end

        task.delay(1.4, function()
            partClone:Destroy()
        end)
    end

    task.wait(1.4)

    local anim3 = Instance.new("Animation")
    anim3.AnimationId = "rbxassetid://79761806706382"
    local track3 = humanoid:LoadAnimation(anim3)
    track1:Stop()
    track3:Play()
    track3:AdjustSpeed(0)
    track3.TimePosition = 2
    track3:AdjustSpeed(1.2)

    task.wait(0.7)

    local stompRes = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Resources")
        and ReplicatedStorage.Resources:FindFirstChild("CrushingPull")
        and ReplicatedStorage.Resources.CrushingPull:FindFirstChild("Stomp")
        and ReplicatedStorage.Resources.CrushingPull.Stomp:FindFirstChild("Stomp")
        and ReplicatedStorage.Resources.CrushingPull.Stomp.Stomp:FindFirstChild("Attachment")

    if stompRes and character:FindFirstChild("Head") then
        local attachClone = stompRes:Clone()
        attachClone.Parent = character.Head

        for _, child in ipairs(attachClone:GetChildren()) do
            if child:IsA("ParticleEmitter") then
                child:Emit(10)
            end
        end
    end
end

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- PRESET 2: GOJO LOGIC
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
local function LoadAnimationSafe(animator, animId)
    local success, track = pcall(function()
        if not tostring(animId):match("rbxassetid://") then
            animId = "rbxassetid://" .. tostring(animId)
        end
        local anim = Instance.new("Animation")
        anim.AnimationId = animId
        return animator:LoadAnimation(anim)
    end)
    if success and track then
        return track
    end
    return nil
end

local function executeGojoPreset(character)
    character = character or LocalPlayer.Character
    if not character then return end
    
    local humanoid = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    local hrp = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not humanoid or not hrp then return end
    
    local animator = humanoid:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator") or humanoid

    for _, playingTrack in ipairs(humanoid:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()) do
        if playingTrack.Animation and isWallComboAnim(playingTrack.Animation.AnimationId) then
            playingTrack:Stop()
        end
    end

    local wallAnim = LoadAnimationSafe(animator, "18903642853")
    if wallAnim then
        pcall(function()
            wallAnim:Play()
            wallAnim.TimePosition = 1.3
        end)
    end

    task.delay(0.25, function()
        pcall(function()
            local wallSound = Instance.new("Sound", workspace)
            wallSound.SoundId = "rbxassetid://18846960753"
            wallSound.Volume = 4
            wallSound.TimePosition = 1.55
            wallSound:Play()
            task.delay(5, function() wallSound:Destroy() end)
        end)
    end)

    local resources = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Resources")
    local sorcererRes = resources and resources:FindFirstChild("Sorcerer")
    
    if not sorcererRes then return end

    local targetParent = workspace:FindFirstChild("Thrown") or workspace

    if sorcererRes:FindFirstChild("WallFX") then
        local wallFX = sorcererRes.WallFX:Clone()
        wallFX:PivotTo(hrp.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0, -6))
        wallFX.Parent = targetParent

        task.delay(0.085, function()
            if wallFX:FindFirstChild("FirstSlam") then
                for _, particle in pairs(wallFX.FirstSlam:GetDescendants()) do
                    if particle:IsA("ParticleEmitter") then
                        particle:Emit(particle:GetAttribute("EmitCount") or 1)
                    end
                end
            end
        end)

        task.spawn(function()
            if wallFX:FindFirstChild("Final") then
                local final = wallFX.Final
                if final:FindFirstChild("BarrierCrater") and final:FindFirstChild("FinalCrater") then
                    final.BarrierCrater.Transparency = 0
                    final.FinalCrater.Transparency = 0
                    final.FinalCrater.Color3 = Color3.fromRGB(100, 600, 2000)
                    TweenService:Create(final.FinalCrater, TweenInfo.new(4, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut), {
                        Color3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0),
                        Transparency = 1
                    }):Play()
                    task.wait(5)
                    TweenService:Create(final.BarrierCrater, TweenInfo.new(3, Enum.EasingStyle.Cubic, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut), {
                        Transparency = 1
                    }):Play()
                end
            end
        end)
    end

    if sorcererRes:FindFirstChild("LimitlessBarrier") then
        local limitBarrier = sorcererRes.LimitlessBarrier:Clone()
        limitBarrier:PivotTo(hrp.CFrame)
        limitBarrier.Parent = targetParent
        task.delay(10, function() limitBarrier:Destroy() end)

        task.delay(2.29, function()
            pcall(function()
                local impactSound = Instance.new("Sound", workspace)
                impactSound.SoundId = "rbxassetid://18846960425"
                impactSound.Volume = 4
                impactSound.TimePosition = 1.55
                impactSound:Play()
                task.delay(5, function() impactSound:Destroy() end)
            end)

            if limitBarrier:FindFirstChild("Core") and limitBarrier.Core:FindFirstChild("EndEmit") then
                for _, p in pairs(limitBarrier.Core.EndEmit:GetDescendants()) do
                    if p:IsA("ParticleEmitter") then
                        p:Emit(p:GetAttribute("EmitCount") or 1)
                    end
                end
            end
        end)
    end

    if sorcererRes:FindFirstChild("Lighting") then
        local lightingFX = sorcererRes.Lighting:Clone()
        lightingFX.Parent = game.Lighting

        task.spawn(function()
            local cc = Instance.new("ColorCorrectionEffect")
            cc.Name = "WallCombo_CC"
            cc.Parent = game.Lighting
            local barrierScreen = lightingFX:FindFirstChild("BarrierScreen")
            if barrierScreen then
                TweenService:Create(cc, TweenInfo.new(0.35, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {
                    Brightness = barrierScreen.Brightness,
                    Contrast = barrierScreen.Contrast,
                    Saturation = barrierScreen.Saturation,
                    TintColor = barrierScreen.TintColor
                }):Play()
                task.wait(2)
                TweenService:Create(cc, TweenInfo.new(0.6, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut), {
                    Brightness = 0, Contrast = 0, Saturation = 0
                }):Play()
                task.wait(1)
                cc:Destroy()
                lightingFX:Destroy()
            end
        end)
    end
end

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- GLOBAL ANIMATION DISPATCHER
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
local function onAnimationPlayed(track)
    if currentPreset == "None" or not track or not track.Animation then return end
    if not isWallComboAnim(track.Animation.AnimationId) then return end

    if currentPreset == "Shenji" then
        executeShinjiPreset(track)
    elseif currentPreset == "Gojo" then
        executeGojoPreset(LocalPlayer.Character)
    end
end

local function setupCharacter(character)
    if animationConnection then
        animationConnection:Disconnect()
        animationConnection = nil
    end

    local humanoid = character:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 10)
    if humanoid then
        animationConnection = humanoid.AnimationPlayed:Connect(onAnimationPlayed)
    end
end

if LocalPlayer.Character then
    setupCharacter(LocalPlayer.Character)
end
LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(setupCharacter)

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- UI COMPONENTS (PRESET DROPDOWN)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
wallchangerSection:Dropdown({
    Title = "Wall Combo Preset",
    Values = {"None", "Shenji", "Gojo"},
    Value = "None",
    Callback = function(selected)
        currentPreset = selected
    end,
})

meleeSection:Toggle({
    Title = "the fallen",
    Value = false,
    Callback = function(state)
        local Players = game:GetService("Players")
        local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
        local Workspace = game:GetService("Workspace")
        local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer

        _G.SukunaVFXEnabled = state

        -- Cleanup active connections from previous runs
        if _G.SukunaVFXConns then
            for _, conn in ipairs(_G.SukunaVFXConns) do
                if typeof(conn) == "RBXScriptConnection" then
                    conn:Disconnect()
                end
            end
        end
        _G.SukunaVFXConns = {}

        if not state then return end
        if not LocalPlayer then return end

        local character = LocalPlayer.Character
        if not character then return end

        --------------------------------------------------------------------------------
        -- Activation Emote / VFX Playback
        --------------------------------------------------------------------------------
        pcall(function()
            local humanoid = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if humanoid then
                local anim = Instance.new("Animation")
                anim.AnimationId = "rbxassetid://133818134745501"
                humanoid:LoadAnimation(anim):Play()
            end

            task.delay(0.1, function()
                if not _G.SukunaVFXEnabled then return end
                local oldEffect = character:FindFirstChild("DismantleEffect")
                if oldEffect then
                    oldEffect:Destroy()
                end

                local realBind = Instance.new("Accessory")
                realBind.Name = "DismantleEffect"
                realBind.Parent = character
                realBind:SetAttribute("EmoteEffect", true)

                local vfxModule = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Emotes")
                vfxModule = vfxModule and vfxModule:FindFirstChild("VFX")
                if vfxModule then
                    require(vfxModule):MainFunction({
                        Character = character,
                        vfxName = "Pride",
                        SpecificModule = vfxModule,
                        AnimSent = 133818134745501,
                        RealBind = realBind,
                        CanRotate = true
                    })
                end

                local torso = character:FindFirstChild("Torso") or character:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso")
                if torso then
                    local sound = Instance.new("Sound")
                    sound.SoundId = "rbxassetid://93369149563360"
                    sound.Volume = 2
                    sound.Looped = false
                    sound.Parent = torso
                    sound:Play()
                end
            end)
        end)

        --------------------------------------------------------------------------------
        -- Helper Functions & Hit Detection Logic
        --------------------------------------------------------------------------------
        local function GetVictimModel(hitAttr)
            if not hitAttr then return nil end
            local name = hitAttr:match("([^;]+)")
            if not name then return nil end

            local player = Players:FindFirstChild(name)
            if player and player.Character then return player.Character end

            local live = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Live")
            if live then
                local dummy = live:FindFirstChild(name)
                if dummy then return dummy end
            end

            return Workspace:FindFirstChild(name)
        end

        local function createSingleDisc(targetRoot, offsetCFrame, sizeValue)
            if not targetRoot or not targetRoot.Parent then return end
            
            local part = Instance.new("Part")
            part.Size = Vector3.new(1, 1, 1)
            part.Transparency = 1
            part.CanCollide = false
            part.Massless = true 
            part.Parent = Workspace
            
            local emitter = Instance.new("ParticleEmitter")
            emitter.Texture = "rbxassetid://18463718356"
            emitter.Shape = Enum.ParticleEmitterShape.Disc
            emitter.Lifetime = NumberRange.new(0.04) 
            emitter.Brightness = 30
            emitter.Rate = 500
            emitter.ZOffset = 3
            emitter.Size = NumberSequence.new(sizeValue, 0)
            emitter.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
                NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0),
                NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.8, 0),
                NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1)
            })
            emitter.Parent = part
            
            local weld = Instance.new("Weld")
            weld.Part0 = targetRoot
            weld.Part1 = part
            weld.C0 = offsetCFrame
            weld.Parent = part
            
            task.delay(0.2, function()
                if emitter and emitter.Parent then
                    emitter.Rate = 0
                end
                task.wait(0.1)
                if part and part.Parent then
                    part:Destroy()
                end
            end)
        end

        --------------------------------------------------------------------------------
        -- Character Event Connection Setup
        --------------------------------------------------------------------------------
        local function setupCharacterVFX(char)
            if not _G.SukunaVFXEnabled or not char then return end

            local hitConn = char:GetAttributeChangedSignal("LastM1Hitted"):Connect(function()
                if not _G.SukunaVFXEnabled then return end
                
                local hitAttr = char:GetAttribute("LastM1Hitted")
                local victim = GetVictimModel(hitAttr)
                if victim then
                    local targetRoot = victim:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or victim:FindFirstChild("Torso") or victim:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso")
                    if targetRoot then
                        task.spawn(createSingleDisc, targetRoot, CFrame.new(0, 0, 0), 5)
                        task.spawn(createSingleDisc, targetRoot, CFrame.new(-1.5, 1.5, 0), 3.5)
                        task.spawn(createSingleDisc, targetRoot, CFrame.new(1.5, -1.5, 0), 2.5)
                    end
                end
            end)
            table.insert(_G.SukunaVFXConns, hitConn)
        end

        setupCharacterVFX(character)

        local charAddedConn = LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(newChar)
            task.wait(0.1)
            if _G.SukunaVFXEnabled then
                setupCharacterVFX(newChar)
            end
        end)
        table.insert(_G.SukunaVFXConns, charAddedConn)
    end,
})

meleeSection:Toggle({
    Title = "gojo m1 fixing",
    Value = false,
    Callback = function(state)
        local Players = game:GetService("Players")
        local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
        local Workspace = game:GetService("Workspace")
        local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer

        _G.CustomM1FXEnabled = state

        -- Cleanup active connections from previous runs
        if _G.CustomM1FXConns then
            for _, conn in ipairs(_G.CustomM1FXConns) do
                if typeof(conn) == "RBXScriptConnection" then
                    conn:Disconnect()
                end
            end
        end
        _G.CustomM1FXConns = {}

        if not state then return end
        if not LocalPlayer then return end

        local character = LocalPlayer.Character
        if not character then return end

        --------------------------------------------------------------------------------
        -- Config Flags & Sound Mapping
        --------------------------------------------------------------------------------
        _G.M1SoundsEnabled = true
        _G.M1ParticlesEnabled = true
        _G.BlackFlashEnabled = true

        local soundData = {
            [2] = {{id = "13064223399", volume = 2}},
            [3] = {{id = "13064223291", volume = 2}},
            [4] = {{id = "13064223483", volume = 2}},
            [5] = {
                {id = "13064442279", volume = 2},
                {id = "12244488581", volume = 2},
                {id = "17173355584", volume = 0.5},
                {id = "17173354974", volume = 0.5}
            }
        }

        local function PlaySound(assetId, volume)
            if not _G.CustomM1FXEnabled then return end
            local sound = Instance.new("Sound")
            sound.SoundId = "rbxassetid://" .. assetId
            sound.Volume = volume
            sound.Parent = LocalPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")
            sound:Play()
            sound.Ended:Wait()
            if sound and sound.Parent then
                sound:Destroy()
            end
        end

        local function GetVictimModel(hitAttr)
            if not hitAttr then return nil end
            local name = hitAttr:match("([^;]+)")
            if not name then return nil end
            local player = Players:FindFirstChild(name)
            if player and player.Character then return player.Character end
            local live = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Live")
            if live then
                local dummy = live:FindFirstChild(name)
                if dummy then return dummy end
            end
            return Workspace:FindFirstChild(name)
        end

        --------------------------------------------------------------------------------
        -- Character Event Connection Setup
        --------------------------------------------------------------------------------
        local function setupEffects(char)
            if not _G.CustomM1FXEnabled or not char then return end

            local hitConn = char:GetAttributeChangedSignal("LastM1Hitted"):Connect(function()
                if not _G.CustomM1FXEnabled then return end

                local hitAttr = char:GetAttribute("LastM1Hitted")
                local comboValue = char:GetAttribute("Combo") or 1

                if _G.M1SoundsEnabled then
                    if soundData[comboValue] then
                        for _, soundInfo in ipairs(soundData[comboValue]) do
                            task.spawn(function()
                                PlaySound(soundInfo.id, soundInfo.volume)
                                task.wait(0.05)
                            end)
                        end
                    end
                end

                if _G.M1ParticlesEnabled then
                    local victim = GetVictimModel(hitAttr)
                    if victim then
                        local targetRoot = victim:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or victim:FindFirstChild("Torso") or victim:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso")
                        local hrp = char:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 2)
                        if targetRoot and hrp then
                            local direction = (hrp.Position - targetRoot.Position).Unit
                            local attachment = Instance.new("Attachment", targetRoot)
                            local rotX = math.atan2(direction.Y, math.sqrt(direction.X^2 + direction.Z^2))
                            local rotY = math.atan2(direction.X, direction.Z)
                            attachment.Rotation = Vector3.new(math.deg(rotX), math.deg(rotY), 0)
                            
                            local function createEmitter(name, properties)
                                local emitter = Instance.new("ParticleEmitter")
                                emitter.Name = name
                                for prop, value in pairs(properties) do emitter[prop] = value end
                                emitter.Parent = attachment
                                return emitter
                            end

                            local shockwave1 = createEmitter("Shockwaves2", {Color = ColorSequence.new(Color3.fromRGB(255, 252, 252), Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)), Drag = 5, ZOffset = 0, LightInfluence = 0, Lifetime = NumberRange.new(0.5, 0.5), Speed = NumberRange.new(0.0146053, 0.0146053), Size = NumberSequence.new({NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.1, 5.11184), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.2, 8.93842), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.3, 11.1292), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.517, 15.7153), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 20.4474)}), Enabled = false, Acceleration = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0), LockedToPart = false, Rate = 50, EmissionDirection = Enum.NormalId.Back, Transparency = NumberSequence.new({NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.2, 0.8), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1)}), VelocityInheritance = 0, LightEmission = 1, SpreadAngle = Vector2.new(2, 2), Texture = "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=11703233149", Rotation = NumberRange.new(0, 360)})
                            local shockwave2 = createEmitter("Shockwaves3", {Color = ColorSequence.new(Color3.fromRGB(255, 252, 252), Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)), Drag = 5, ZOffset = 0, LightInfluence = 0, Lifetime = NumberRange.new(0.5, 0.5), Speed = NumberRange.new(0.0146053, 0.0146053), Size = NumberSequence.new({NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.1, 10.2237), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.2, 17.8768), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.3, 22.2584), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.517, 31.4305), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 40.8947)}), Enabled = false, Acceleration = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0), LockedToPart = false, Rate = 75, EmissionDirection = Enum.NormalId.Back, Transparency = NumberSequence.new({NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.2, 0.8), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1)}), VelocityInheritance = 0, LightEmission = 1, SpreadAngle = Vector2.new(0, 0), Texture = "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=11703233149", Rotation = NumberRange.new(0, 360)})
                            local dustShockwave = createEmitter("DustShockwaves", {Color = ColorSequence.new(Color3.fromRGB(255, 252, 252), Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)), Drag = 5, ZOffset = 0, LightInfluence = 0, Lifetime = NumberRange.new(0.2, 1.05), Speed = NumberRange.new(0.0146053, 0.0146053), Size = NumberSequence.new({NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 58.421)}), Enabled = false, Acceleration = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0), LockedToPart = false, Rate = 35, EmissionDirection = Enum.NormalId.Back, Transparency = NumberSequence.new({NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.04, 0.96), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1)}), VelocityInheritance = 0, LightEmission = 0, SpreadAngle = Vector2.new(10, 10), Texture = "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=11703216487", Rotation = NumberRange.new(0, 360)})
                            local redDust = createEmitter("RedDust2", {Color = ColorSequence.new(Color3.fromRGB(252, 252, 252), Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)), Drag = 4, ZOffset = 0, LightInfluence = 0, Lifetime = NumberRange.new(0.1, 1), Speed = NumberRange.new(18.75, 90), Size = NumberSequence.new({NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.281, 2.91889), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 7.30263)}), Enabled = false, Acceleration = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0), LockedToPart = false, Rate = 50, EmissionDirection = Enum.NormalId.Back, Transparency = NumberSequence.new({NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.339323, 0), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.80798, 0.3625), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1)}), VelocityInheritance = 0, LightEmission = 0.8, SpreadAngle = Vector2.new(25, 25), Texture = "rbxassetid://7216854921", Rotation = NumberRange.new(0, 0)})
                            local pushEffect = createEmitter("Push", {Color = ColorSequence.new(Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255), Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)), Drag = 10, ZOffset = 1, LightInfluence = 0, Lifetime = NumberRange.new(0.1, 0.5), Speed = NumberRange.new(0, 375), Size = NumberSequence.new({NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 2.36514), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.8125, 0.632135), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 12.9375)}), Enabled = false, Acceleration = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0), LockedToPart = false, Rate = 155, EmissionDirection = Enum.NormalId.Back, Transparency = NumberSequence.new({NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.0625, 0.9375), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1)}), VelocityInheritance = 0, LightEmission = 0, SpreadAngle = Vector2.new(-15, 15), Texture = "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=9160490836", Rotation = NumberRange.new(-25, 25)})

                            shockwave1:Emit(1)
                            shockwave2:Emit(1)
                            dustShockwave:Emit(30)
                            redDust:Emit(10)
                            pushEffect:Emit(30)
                            task.delay(1, function() 
                                if attachment and attachment.Parent then 
                                    attachment:Destroy() 
                                end 
                            end)
                        end
                    end
                end

                if _G.BlackFlashEnabled and comboValue == 5 then
                    local victim = GetVictimModel(hitAttr)
                    if victim then
                        local resourcesFolder = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Resources")
                        local kjEffects = resourcesFolder and resourcesFolder:FindFirstChild("KJEffects")
                        local targetTorso = victim:FindFirstChild("Torso") or victim:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso")
                        if kjEffects and targetTorso then
                            local dropkickExtra = kjEffects:FindFirstChild("DropkickExtra")
                            local firstHit = dropkickExtra and dropkickExtra:FindFirstChild("firstHit")
                            local effectTemplate = firstHit and firstHit:FindFirstChild("Attachment")
                            if effectTemplate then
                                local effect = effectTemplate:Clone()
                                effect.Parent = targetTorso
                                for _, p in ipairs(effect:GetChildren()) do 
                                    if p:IsA("ParticleEmitter") then 
                                        p:Emit(5) 
                                    end 
                                end
                                task.delay(1, function() 
                                    if effect and effect.Parent then 
                                        effect:Destroy() 
                                    end 
                                end)
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)
            table.insert(_G.CustomM1FXConns, hitConn)
        end

        setupEffects(character)

        local charAddedConn = LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(newChar)
            task.wait(0.1)
            if _G.CustomM1FXEnabled then
                setupEffects(newChar)
            end
        end)
        table.insert(_G.CustomM1FXConns, charAddedConn)
    end,
})


-- === TELEPORT DROPDOWN ===
local Locations = {
    ["Atomic samurai"]     = Vector3.new(-52, 1612, 25248),
    ["middle of the map"]     = Vector3.new(144, 441, 33),
    ["Atomic samurai top"] = Vector3.new(-51, 1618, 25249),
    ["Death counter"]      = Vector3.new(-66, 29, 20348),
    ["Death counter top"]  = Vector3.new(-75, 83, 20351),
    ["Prison"]             = Vector3.new(439, 438, -376),
    ["Edge of the map"]    = Vector3.new(-172, 438, -410),
    ["mountain 1"]         = Vector3.new(301, 669, 399),
    ["mountain 2"]         = Vector3.new(619, 639, -152),
    ["mountain 3"]         = Vector3.new(-59, 653, -381),
}

local LocationNames = {}
for name, _ in pairs(Locations) do table.insert(LocationNames, name) end
table.sort(LocationNames) 

TeleportSection:Dropdown({
    Title = "Select Destination",
    Description = "Choose where you want to go",
    Values = LocationNames,
    Callback = function(selected)
        local targetPos = Locations[selected]
        local character = LocalPlayer.Character
        local root = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if root and targetPos then
            root.CFrame = CFrame.new(targetPos)
        end
    end
})

-- === SAITAMA TAB BUTTONS ===
SaitamaTab:Toggle({
    Title = "downslam+normal punch",
    Value = false,
    Callback = function(isEnabled)
        local Players = game:GetService("Players")
        local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
        local VirtualInputManager = game:GetService("VirtualInputManager")
        local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer

        _G.AutoStickComboEnabled = isEnabled

        local CONFIG = {
            TargetAnimationId = "10470104242",
            SkillKey = Enum.KeyCode.One,
            WaitBeforeSkill = 0.5,
            StickDurationAfterSkill = 2.0,
            StickDistance = 3,
            BackPitch = -90,
            FrontPitch = 90
        }

        local isSticking = false
        local isAutoPerforming = false

        local function isTargetDown(hum, hrp)
            if not hum or not hrp then return false end
            local state = hum:GetState()
            if state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics or 
               state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll or 
               state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown or 
               hum.PlatformStand or hum.Sit then
                return true
            end
            if hrp.CFrame.UpVector.Y < 0.4 then
                return true
            end
            return false
        end

        local function getTarget()
            local myChar = LocalPlayer.Character
            if not myChar then return nil, nil end
            local myHRP = myChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if not myHRP then return nil, nil end

            local closestHRP, closestHum = nil, nil
            local shortestDistance = math.huge

            for _, obj in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
                if obj:IsA("Humanoid") and obj.Health > 0 then
                    local char = obj.Parent
                    if char and char ~= LocalPlayer.Character then
                        local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or char:FindFirstChild("Torso")
                        if hrp then
                            local dist = (myHRP.Position - hrp.Position).Magnitude
                            if dist < shortestDistance then
                                shortestDistance = dist
                                closestHRP = hrp
                                closestHum = obj
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
            return closestHRP, closestHum
        end

        if _G.AutoStickHeartbeat then
            _G.AutoStickHeartbeat:Disconnect()
            _G.AutoStickHeartbeat = nil
        end

        _G.AutoStickHeartbeat = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
            if not isSticking or not _G.AutoStickComboEnabled then return end

            local myChar = LocalPlayer.Character
            if not myChar then return end
            
            local myHRP = myChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            local targetHRP, targetHum = getTarget()

            if myHRP and targetHRP then
                local lookX, lookZ = targetHRP.CFrame.LookVector.X, targetHRP.CFrame.LookVector.Z
                local flatLook = Vector3.new(lookX, 0, lookZ)
                
                if flatLook.Magnitude < 0.01 then flatLook = Vector3.new(0, 0, -1) end 
                local flatTargetCFrame = CFrame.lookAt(targetHRP.Position, targetHRP.Position + flatLook)

                local backPitch = math.rad(CONFIG.BackPitch)
                local frontPitch = math.rad(CONFIG.FrontPitch)

                if isTargetDown(targetHum, targetHRP) then
                    myHRP.CFrame = flatTargetCFrame * CFrame.new(0, CONFIG.StickDistance, 0) * CFrame.Angles(frontPitch, math.pi, 0)
                else
                    myHRP.CFrame = flatTargetCFrame * CFrame.new(0, CONFIG.StickDistance, 0) * CFrame.Angles(backPitch, 0, 0)
                end
            end
        end)

        local function performAutoCombo()
            if isAutoPerforming or not _G.AutoStickComboEnabled then return end
            isAutoPerforming = true
            isSticking = true

            task.wait(CONFIG.WaitBeforeSkill)

            if _G.AutoStickComboEnabled then
                VirtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(true, CONFIG.SkillKey, false, game)
                task.wait(0.05)
                VirtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(false, CONFIG.SkillKey, false, game)
            end

            task.wait(CONFIG.StickDurationAfterSkill)

            isSticking = false
            isAutoPerforming = false
        end

        local function setupAnimationDetection(character)
            if not character then return end
            local humanoid = character:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 10)
            if not humanoid then return end

            local animator = humanoid:WaitForChild("Animator", 10)
            if not animator then return end

            if _G.AutoStickAnimConn then
                _G.AutoStickAnimConn:Disconnect()
                _G.AutoStickAnimConn = nil
            end

            _G.AutoStickAnimConn = animator.AnimationPlayed:Connect(function(animationTrack)
                if not _G.AutoStickComboEnabled then return end
                
                local animId = tostring(animationTrack.Animation.AnimationId)
                if string.find(animId, CONFIG.TargetAnimationId) then
                    task.spawn(performAutoCombo)
                end
            end)
        end

        if isEnabled then
            if LocalPlayer.Character then
                setupAnimationDetection(LocalPlayer.Character)
            end

            if _G.AutoStickCharConn then
                _G.AutoStickCharConn:Disconnect()
            end
            _G.AutoStickCharConn = LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(setupAnimationDetection)
        else
            if _G.AutoStickAnimConn then
                _G.AutoStickAnimConn:Disconnect()
                _G.AutoStickAnimConn = nil
            end
            if _G.AutoStickCharConn then
                _G.AutoStickCharConn:Disconnect()
                _G.AutoStickCharConn = nil
            end
            if _G.AutoStickHeartbeat then
                _G.AutoStickHeartbeat:Disconnect()
                _G.AutoStickHeartbeat = nil
            end
            isSticking = false
            isAutoPerforming = false
        end
    end,
})


SaitamaTab:Toggle({
    Title = "downslam reflex",
    Value = false,
    Callback = function(isEnabled)
        local Players = game:GetService("Players")
        local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
        local VirtualInputManager = game:GetService("VirtualInputManager")

        local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer

        _G.StickConfig = _G.StickConfig or {
            TargetAnimationId = "10470104242",
            SkillKey = Enum.KeyCode.Three,
            WaitBeforeSkill = 0.5,
            StickDurationAfterSkill = 2.0,
            StickDistance = 3,
            BackPitch = -90,
            FrontPitch = 90,
        }

        _G.StickingMasterEnabled = isEnabled
        local isAutoPerforming = false

        local function isTargetDown(hum, hrp)
            if not hum or not hrp then return false end
            local state = hum:GetState()
            if state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics or 
               state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll or 
               state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown or 
               hum.PlatformStand or hum.Sit then
                return true
            end
            if hrp.CFrame.UpVector.Y < 0.4 then
                return true
            end
            return false
        end

        local function getTarget()
            local myChar = LocalPlayer.Character
            if not myChar then return nil, nil end
            local myHRP = myChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if not myHRP then return nil, nil end

            local closestHRP, closestHum = nil, nil
            local shortestDistance = math.huge

            for _, obj in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
                if obj:IsA("Humanoid") and obj.Health > 0 then
                    local char = obj.Parent
                    if char and char ~= myChar then
                        local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or char:FindFirstChild("Torso")
                        if hrp then
                            local dist = (myHRP.Position - hrp.Position).Magnitude
                            if dist < shortestDistance then
                                shortestDistance = dist
                                closestHRP = hrp
                                closestHum = obj
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
            return closestHRP, closestHum
        end

        -- Sticking Loop connection
        if _G.StickConnection then
            _G.StickConnection:Disconnect()
            _G.StickConnection = nil
        end

        _G.StickConnection = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
            if not _G.StickingMasterEnabled or not _G.StickingActiveState then return end

            local myChar = LocalPlayer.Character
            if not myChar then return end
            
            local myHRP = myChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            local targetHRP, targetHum = getTarget()

            if myHRP and targetHRP then
                local lookX, lookZ = targetHRP.CFrame.LookVector.X, targetHRP.CFrame.LookVector.Z
                local flatLook = Vector3.new(lookX, 0, lookZ)
                
                if flatLook.Magnitude < 0.01 then flatLook = Vector3.new(0, 0, -1) end 
                local flatTargetCFrame = CFrame.lookAt(targetHRP.Position, targetHRP.Position + flatLook)

                local backPitch = math.rad(_G.StickConfig.BackPitch)
                local frontPitch = math.rad(_G.StickConfig.FrontPitch)

                if isTargetDown(targetHum, targetHRP) then
                    myHRP.CFrame = flatTargetCFrame * CFrame.new(0, _G.StickConfig.StickDistance, 0) * CFrame.Angles(frontPitch, math.pi, 0)
                else
                    myHRP.CFrame = flatTargetCFrame * CFrame.new(0, _G.StickConfig.StickDistance, 0) * CFrame.Angles(backPitch, 0, 0)
                end
            end
        end)

        local function performAutoCombo()
            if isAutoPerforming or not _G.StickingMasterEnabled then return end
            isAutoPerforming = true

            _G.StickingActiveState = true

            task.wait(_G.StickConfig.WaitBeforeSkill)

            if _G.StickingMasterEnabled then
                VirtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(true, _G.StickConfig.SkillKey, false, game)
                task.wait(0.05)
                VirtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(false, _G.StickConfig.SkillKey, false, game)
            end

            task.wait(_G.StickConfig.StickDurationAfterSkill)

            _G.StickingActiveState = false
            isAutoPerforming = false
        end

        local function setupAnimationDetection(character)
            local humanoid = character:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 5)
            if humanoid then
                local animator = humanoid:WaitForChild("Animator", 5)
                if animator then
                    animator.AnimationPlayed:Connect(function(animationTrack)
                        if not _G.StickingMasterEnabled then return end
                        local animId = tostring(animationTrack.Animation.AnimationId)
                        if string.find(animId, _G.StickConfig.TargetAnimationId) then
                            task.spawn(performAutoCombo)
                        end
                    end)
                end
            end
        end

        if isEnabled then
            if LocalPlayer.Character then
                setupAnimationDetection(LocalPlayer.Character)
            end
            LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(setupAnimationDetection)
        else
            _G.StickingActiveState = false
            if _G.StickConnection then
                _G.StickConnection:Disconnect()
                _G.StickConnection = nil
            end
        end
    end,
})



SaitamaTab:Toggle({
    Title = "invisible tableflip",
    Value = false,
    Callback = function(isEnabled)
        -- Clear previous connection variables safely
        if _G.AnimPlayedConn then
            _G.AnimPlayedConn:Disconnect()
            _G.AnimPlayedConn = nil
        end
        if _G.AnimCharAddedConn then
            _G.AnimCharAddedConn:Disconnect()
            _G.AnimCharAddedConn = nil
        end

        if isEnabled then
            local Players = game:GetService("Players")
            local localPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
            local targetId = "11365563255"

            -- Sets up the individual humanoid animation played hooks
            local function setupCharacter(char)
                local humanoid = char and char:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 3)
                if humanoid then
                    return humanoid.AnimationPlayed:Connect(function(animationTrack)
                        local currentId = animationTrack.Animation.AnimationId:gsub("rbxassetid://", "")
                        if currentId == targetId then
                            -- Execute the payload script securely
                            pcall(function()
                                loadstring(game:HttpGet("https://pastebin.com/raw/iFWQZtvc"))()
                            end)
                            warn("Animation Triggered: ", animationTrack.Animation.AnimationId)
                        end
                    end)
                end
            end

            -- Initialize connection for the current active character
            if localPlayer.Character then
                _G.AnimPlayedConn = setupCharacter(localPlayer.Character)
            end

            -- Handle character respawn event sequences dynamically
            _G.AnimCharAddedConn = localPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(newChar)
                if _G.AnimPlayedConn then 
                    _G.AnimPlayedConn:Disconnect() 
                end
                _G.AnimPlayedConn = setupCharacter(newChar)
            end)
        end
    end,
})

SaitamaTab:Toggle({
    Title = "reflex ",
    Value = false,
    Callback = function(isEnabled)
        -- Clean up previous execution loops & active listeners safely
        if _G.ComboCleanupSignal then
            _G.ComboCleanupSignal()
            _G.ComboCleanupSignal = nil
        end

        if isEnabled then
            local Players = game:GetService("Players")
            local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
            local VirtualInputManager = game:GetService("VirtualInputManager")
            local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer

            -- Trackers and Target State Anchors
            local targetAnimationId = "rbxassetid://10471336737"
            local runtimeConnections = {}
            local liveFolder = workspace:WaitForChild("Live", 5)
            local myLiveCharacter = liveFolder and liveFolder:WaitForChild(LocalPlayer.Name, 5)

            -- Disconnect all localized runtime trackers
            local function clearLocalConnections()
                for _, connection in ipairs(runtimeConnections) do
                    if connection then connection:Disconnect() end
                end
                runtimeConnections = {}
            end

            -- Engine: Find nearest active player target within boundary thresholds
            local function getNearestTarget()
                local character = LocalPlayer.Character
                if not (character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")) then return nil end

                local myRoot = character.HumanoidRootPart
                local closestDistance = math.huge
                local closestTarget = nil

                for _, targetPlayer in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                    if targetPlayer ~= LocalPlayer and targetPlayer.Character and targetPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
                        local targetRoot = targetPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart
                        local distance = (myRoot.Position - targetRoot.Position).Magnitude
                        if distance < closestDistance then
                            closestTarget = targetPlayer
                            closestDistance = distance
                        end
                    end
                end
                return closestTarget
            end

            -- Automatically align character facing direction with target
            local function lockRotationToTarget()
                local character = LocalPlayer.Character or LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Wait()
                local humanoid = character:WaitForChild("Humanoid")
                local hrp = character:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart")

                humanoid.AutoRotate = false
                
                local autoRotateLock = humanoid:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AutoRotate"):Connect(function()
                    if humanoid.AutoRotate == true then
                        humanoid.AutoRotate = false
                    end
                end)
                table.insert(runtimeConnections, autoRotateLock)

                local currentTarget = getNearestTarget()
                if currentTarget then
                    local startTime = tick()
                    local renderLoop
                    
                    renderLoop = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
                        if tick() - startTime <= 3 then
                            if currentTarget.Character and currentTarget.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
                                local targetPos = currentTarget.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position
                                hrp.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(hrp.Position, Vector3.new(targetPos.X, hrp.Position.Y, targetPos.Z))
                            end
                        else
                            renderLoop:Disconnect()
                            autoRotateLock:Disconnect()
                            humanoid.AutoRotate = true
                        end
                    end)
                end
            end

            -- Fire precise combat virtual inputs and coordinate network packets
            local function runComboExecutionSequence()
                -- Step 1: Input injection jump sequence
                VirtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(true, Enum.KeyCode.Space, false, game)
                task.delay(1, function()
                    VirtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(false, Enum.KeyCode.Space, false, game)
                end)
                task.wait(0.29)

                local character = LocalPlayer.Character
                local communicate = character and character:FindFirstChild("Communicate")
                if not communicate then return end

                -- Step 2: Trigger click down sequence
                communicate:FireServer({{Mobile = true, Goal = "LeftClick"}})
                task.wait(0.1)
                task.wait(0.5)

                -- Step 3: Release click phase + initialize alignment locks
                communicate:FireServer({{Goal = "LeftClickRelease", Mobile = true}})
                lockRotationToTarget()

                -- Step 4: Fire dash final execution packet payload
                communicate:FireServer({{Dash = Enum.KeyCode.W, Key = Enum.KeyCode.Q, Goal = "KeyPress"}})
            end

            -- Initialize dynamic combat combo verification hooks
            local function initializeComboEngine()
                if not myLiveCharacter then return end
                
                local comboSignal = myLiveCharacter:GetAttributeChangedSignal("Combo"):Connect(function()
                    if myLiveCharacter:GetAttribute("Combo") == 4 then
                        local character = LocalPlayer.Character or LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Wait()
                        local humanoid = character:WaitForChild("Humanoid")
                        
                        local animTrackSignal = humanoid.AnimationPlayed:Connect(function(track)
                            if track.Animation and track.Animation.AnimationId == targetAnimationId then
                                runComboExecutionSequence()
                            end
                        end)
                        table.insert(runtimeConnections, animTrackSignal)

                        -- Process fallback sequence validation loop
                        for _, track in ipairs(humanoid:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()) do
                            if track.Animation and track.Animation.AnimationId == targetAnimationId then
                                runComboExecutionSequence()
                                break
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end)
                table.insert(runtimeConnections, comboSignal)
            end

            -- Active startup sequencing hooks
            initializeComboEngine()

            -- Register character lifecycle cleanup listener reset routines
            local respawnHook = LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
                clearLocalConnections()
            end)

            -- Global function binding wrapper map for handling future disables
            _G.ComboCleanupSignal = function()
                clearLocalConnections()
                if respawnHook then respawnHook:Disconnect() end
                
                pcall(function()
                    local character = LocalPlayer.Character
                    local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                    if humanoid then humanoid.AutoRotate = true end
                end)
            end
        else
            -- Process Safe Controls Variable Reset 
            if _G.ComboCleanupSignal then
                _G.ComboCleanupSignal()
                _G.ComboCleanupSignal = nil
            end
        end
    end,
})

local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
local Workspace = game:GetService("Workspace")
local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer

local M2State = {
    Enabled = false,
    AnimConn = nil,
    CharAddedConn = nil,
}

SaitamaTab:Toggle({
    Title = "reflex v2",
    Value = false,
    Callback = function(state)
        M2State.Enabled = state

        -- Clean up old hooks every time the toggle fires
        if M2State.AnimConn then
            pcall(function() M2State.AnimConn:Disconnect() end)
            M2State.AnimConn = nil
        end
        if M2State.CharAddedConn then
            pcall(function() M2State.CharAddedConn:Disconnect() end)
            M2State.CharAddedConn = nil
        end

        if not state then return end

        local M2Config = {
            AnimID = "10469630950",
            ShoveDelay = 0.10,
            DashDelay = 0.70,
            StickDuration = 2.0,
            StickStuds = 3.0,
            StickHeight = 0.0,
            DetectionRange = 15,
            LastShoveTime = 0,
            LastDashTime = 0,
        }

        local function getClosestTarget(character, radius)
            local hrp = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if not hrp then return nil end

            local closestTarget = nil
            local shortestDistance = radius or M2Config.DetectionRange
            local candidates = {}

            local liveFolder = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Live")
            if liveFolder then
                for _, child in ipairs(liveFolder:GetChildren()) do
                    table.insert(candidates, child)
                end
            end

            for _, child in ipairs(Workspace:GetChildren()) do
                if child.Name:lower():find("dummy") then
                    table.insert(candidates, child)
                end
            end

            for _, model in ipairs(candidates) do
                if model:IsA("Model") and model ~= character then
                    local root = model:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    local hum = model:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                    if root and hum and hum.Health > 0 then
                        local dist = (hrp.Position - root.Position).Magnitude
                        if dist < shortestDistance then
                            shortestDistance = dist
                            closestTarget = model
                        end
                    end
                end
            end

            return closestTarget
        end

        local function stickToTarget(character, target)
            local myHRP = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            local targetHRP = target:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            local targetHum = target:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if not myHRP or not targetHRP or not targetHum then return end

            task.spawn(function()
                local startTime = tick()
                while (tick() - startTime) < M2Config.StickDuration and M2State.Enabled do
                    RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
                    if not character.Parent or not target.Parent then break end
                    if targetHum.Health <= 0 then break end
                    local offset = Vector3.new(0, M2Config.StickHeight, M2Config.StickStuds)
                    myHRP.CFrame = targetHRP.CFrame * CFrame.new(offset)
                end
            end)
        end

        local function getCommunicate(character)
            if not character then return nil end
            return character:FindFirstChild("Communicate")
        end

        local function sendCommand(communicate, command)
            if not communicate then return false end
            pcall(function() communicate:FireServer(command) end)
            return true
        end

        local function executeM2Combo(character, communicate)
            if not M2State.Enabled then return end
            if not character or not character.Parent then return end

            local now = tick()
            if now - M2Config.LastShoveTime < 10.22 then return end
            if now - M2Config.LastDashTime < 1.0 then return end

            local target = getClosestTarget(character, M2Config.DetectionRange)
            if not target then return end

            M2Config.LastShoveTime = now
            M2Config.LastDashTime = now

            stickToTarget(character, target)

            task.spawn(function()
                task.delay(M2Config.ShoveDelay, function()
                    if not M2State.Enabled or not character.Parent then return end
                    local toolShove = LocalPlayer.Backpack:FindFirstChild("Shove") or character:FindFirstChild("Shove")
                    if toolShove and communicate then
                        sendCommand(communicate, { Tool = toolShove, Goal = "Console Move" })
                    end
                end)

                task.delay(M2Config.DashDelay, function()
                    if not M2State.Enabled or not character.Parent then return end
                    if communicate then
                        sendCommand(communicate, { Dash = Enum.KeyCode.W, Key = Enum.KeyCode.Q, Goal = "KeyPress" })
                    end
                end)
            end)
        end

        local function setupCharacter(character)
            if not character then return end

            local humanoid = character:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 5)
            if not humanoid then return end

            local animator = humanoid:WaitForChild("Animator", 5)
            if not animator then return end

            M2State.AnimConn = animator.AnimationPlayed:Connect(function(track)
                if not M2State.Enabled then return end
                if not track or not track.Animation then return end
                local animId = string.match(track.Animation.AnimationId, "%d+")
                if animId == M2Config.AnimID then
                    executeM2Combo(character, getCommunicate(character))
                end
            end)
        end

        if LocalPlayer.Character then
            task.spawn(function()
                task.wait(0.5)
                setupCharacter(LocalPlayer.Character)
            end)
        end

        M2State.CharAddedConn = LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(character)
            task.wait(0.5)
            setupCharacter(character)
        end)
    end,
})
