-- Anti Lag Tab
local AntilagTab = Window:Tab({ Title = "Anti Lag", Icon = "sparkles", Desc = "makes your gameplay better" })

-- Map Hiding Logic
local MapFolder = workspace:FindFirstChild("Map")
if MapFolder then
    local HiddenParts = {}
    
    local function ToggleMapPart(partName, visible)
        local part = MapFolder:FindFirstChild(partName)
        if visible then
            if not part and HiddenParts[partName] then
                HiddenParts[partName].Parent = MapFolder
            end
        elseif part then
            HiddenParts[partName] = part
            part.Parent = nil
        end
    end

    AntilagTab:Section({ Title = "Hide Parts", Icon = "package" })

    AntilagTab:Toggle({
        Title = "Trees",
        Value = true,
        Callback = function(state)
            ToggleMapPart("Trees", state)
        end
    })

    AntilagTab:Toggle({
        Title = "Walls",
        Value = true,
        Callback = function(state)
            ToggleMapPart("Walls", state)
        end
    })

    AntilagTab:Toggle({
        Title = "Grass",
        Value = true,
        Callback = function(state)
            ToggleMapPart("Grass", state)
            ToggleMapPart("GrassBottom", state)
        end
    })

    AntilagTab:Toggle({
        Title = "Benches",
        Value = true,
        Callback = function(state)
            ToggleMapPart("Benchs", state)
        end
    })

    -- Default Visibility Initialization
    ToggleMapPart("Trees", true)
    ToggleMapPart("Walls", true)
    ToggleMapPart("Grass", true)
    ToggleMapPart("GrassBottom", true)
    ToggleMapPart("Benchs", true)
end

AntilagTab:Toggle({
    Title = "debris remover",
    Value = false,
    Callback = function(state)
        local Players = game:GetService("Players")
        local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
        local Workspace = game:GetService("Workspace")
        local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer

        getgenv().FastTechEnabled = state

        -- Cleanup active connections and metamethod state
        if _G.FastTechConns then
            for _, conn in ipairs(_G.FastTechConns) do
                if typeof(conn) == "RBXScriptConnection" then
                    conn:Disconnect()
                end
            end
        end
        _G.FastTechConns = {}

        -- Restore original hooks if state is set to OFF
        if not state then
            if _G.FastTechOldNamecall then
                hookmetamethod(game, "__namecall", _G.FastTechOldNamecall)
                _G.FastTechOldNamecall = nil
            end
            if _G.FastTechOldNewindex then
                hookmetamethod(game, "__newindex", _G.FastTechOldNewindex)
                _G.FastTechOldNewindex = nil
            end
            return
        end

        --------------------------------------------------------------------------------
        -- Settings / Configuration
        --------------------------------------------------------------------------------
        getgenv().Settings = getgenv().Settings or {}
        Settings.Limb = Settings.Limb or {}
        Settings.Limb.Arms = true
        Settings.Limb.Legs = true
        Settings.Shiftlock = true

        --------------------------------------------------------------------------------
        -- Respawn / Refresh Utilities
        --------------------------------------------------------------------------------
        local function respawn(plr)
            local char = plr.Character
            if not char then return end

            local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if hum then 
                hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead) 
            end

            char:ClearAllChildren()
            local newChar = Instance.new("Model")
            newChar.Parent = Workspace
            plr.Character = newChar
            task.wait()
            plr.Character = char
            newChar:Destroy()
        end

        --------------------------------------------------------------------------------
        -- Metamethod Hooks
        --------------------------------------------------------------------------------
        if not _G.FastTechOldNamecall then
            local oldNamecall
            oldNamecall = hookmetamethod(game, "__namecall", function(self, ...)
                local method, args = getnamecallmethod(), {...}
                if getgenv().FastTechEnabled and self.Name == "Communicate" and method == "FireServer" and type(args[1]) == "table" and args[1]["Goal"] == "Reset" then
                    task.spawn(function()
                        respawn(LocalPlayer)
                    end)
                end
                return oldNamecall(self, ...)
            end)
            _G.FastTechOldNamecall = oldNamecall
        end

        if not _G.FastTechOldNewindex then
            local oldNewindex
            oldNewindex = hookmetamethod(game, "__newindex", function(self, k, v)
                if getgenv().FastTechEnabled then
                    local char = self:FindFirstAncestorWhichIsA("Model")
                    if k == "Parent" and (v == Workspace:FindFirstChild("Thrown") or self:IsA("ParticleEmitter")) then
                        self:Destroy()
                        return nil
                    end
                end
                return oldNewindex(self, k, v)
            end)
            _G.FastTechOldNewindex = oldNewindex
        end

        --------------------------------------------------------------------------------
        -- Thrown Folder Cleanup
        --------------------------------------------------------------------------------
        local thrownFolder = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Thrown")
        if thrownFolder then
            local thrownConn = thrownFolder.ChildAdded:Connect(function(instance)
                if not getgenv().FastTechEnabled then return end
                task.wait()
                if instance and instance.Parent then
                    instance:Destroy()
                end
            end)
            table.insert(_G.FastTechConns, thrownConn)
        end

        --------------------------------------------------------------------------------
        -- Dodge & Velocity Process Logic
        --------------------------------------------------------------------------------
        local function Process()
            if not getgenv().FastTechEnabled or not LocalPlayer.Character then return end
            
            local hrp = LocalPlayer.Character:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 5)
            if not hrp then return end

            local childAddedConn = hrp.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
                if not getgenv().FastTechEnabled then return end

                local Dodge = false
                if child.Name == "dodgevelocity" then
                    task.spawn(function()
                        Dodge = true
                        local screenGui = LocalPlayer.PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("ScreenGui")
                        local Glow = screenGui and screenGui:FindFirstChild("MagicHealth") and screenGui.MagicHealth.Health:FindFirstChild("Glow")
                        if Glow then
                            for i = 1.975, 0, -1 do
                                if not Dodge or not getgenv().FastTechEnabled then break end
                                Glow.ImageColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
                                task.wait(1)
                                Glow.ImageColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
                            end
                        end
                        Dodge = false
                    end)
                elseif child.Name == "moveme" or (child.Name == "Sound" and Dodge) then
                    task.spawn(function()
                        Dodge = false
                        local screenGui = LocalPlayer.PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("ScreenGui")
                        local Text = screenGui and screenGui:FindFirstChild("MagicHealth") and screenGui.MagicHealth:FindFirstChild("TextLabel")
                        if Text then
                            for i = 3.975, 0, -1 do
                                if not getgenv().FastTechEnabled then break end
                                Text.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 50, 50)
                                task.wait(1)
                                Text.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
                            end
                        end
                    end)
                end
            end)
            table.insert(_G.FastTechConns, childAddedConn)
        end

        Process()

        local charAddedConn = LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
            task.wait(0.1)
            Process()
        end)
        table.insert(_G.FastTechConns, charAddedConn)

        --------------------------------------------------------------------------------
        -- RenderStepped Loop (Limb Removal & Shiftlock)
        --------------------------------------------------------------------------------
        local cam = Workspace.CurrentCamera
        local renderConn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
            if not getgenv().FastTechEnabled then return end

            local char = LocalPlayer.Character
            if not char then return end

            local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if not hrp then return end

            if char:FindFirstChild("Left Arm") and char:FindFirstChild("Right Arm") then
                if not Settings.Limb.Arms then
                    char:FindFirstChild("Left Arm"):Destroy()
                    char:FindFirstChild("Right Arm"):Destroy()
                end
                if not Settings.Limb.Legs then
                    if char:FindFirstChild("Left Leg") then char:FindFirstChild("Left Leg"):Destroy() end
                    if char:FindFirstChild("Right Leg") then char:FindFirstChild("Right Leg"):Destroy() end
                end
            end

            if not char:FindFirstChild("Ragdoll") and Settings.Shiftlock then
                local pos = hrp.Position + Vector3.new(cam.CFrame.LookVector.X, 0, cam.CFrame.LookVector.Z)
                hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(hrp.Position, pos)
            end
        end)
        table.insert(_G.FastTechConns, renderConn)
    end,
})

    Title = "Limited Emote Store",
    Value = false,
    Callback = function(isEnabled)
        local Players = game:GetService("Players")
        local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
        local MarketplaceService = game:GetService("MarketplaceService")

        local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer

            CloseBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(192, 192, 192)
            CloseBtn.BorderSizePixel = 1
            CloseBtn.BorderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
            CloseBtn.Text = "X"
            CloseBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
            CloseBtn.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSans
            CloseBtn.TextSize = 14
            CloseBtn.ZIndex = 3
            CloseBtn.Parent = TopBar

            local ScrollingFrame = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame")
            ScrollingFrame.Name = "ScrollingFrame"
            ScrollingFrame.Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 1, -35)
            ScrollingFrame.Position = UDim2.new(0, 5, 0, 30)
            ScrollingFrame.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
            ScrollingFrame.BorderSizePixel = 1
            ScrollingFrame.BorderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
            ScrollingFrame.ScrollBarImageColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(192, 192, 192)
            ScrollingFrame.ScrollBarThickness = 12
            ScrollingFrame.AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
            ScrollingFrame.ScrollingDirection = Enum.ScrollingDirection.Y
            ScrollingFrame.VerticalScrollBarInset = Enum.ScrollBarInset.Always
            ScrollingFrame.Parent = MainFrame

            local UIListLayout = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
            UIListLayout.Padding = UDim.new(0, 4)
            UIListLayout.Parent = ScrollingFrame

            CloseBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                ScreenGui.Enabled = false
            end)

            local updateListFuncs = {}

            local function createItem(itemData)
                local ItemFrame = Instance.new("Frame")
                ItemFrame.Size = UDim2.new(1, -4, 0, 80)
                ItemFrame.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
                ItemFrame.BorderSizePixel = 1
                ItemFrame.BorderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(192, 192, 192)
                ItemFrame.Parent = ScrollingFrame

                local Image = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
                Image.Size = UDim2.new(0, 60, 0, 60)
                Image.Position = UDim2.new(0, 8, 0.5, -30)
                Image.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(192, 192, 192)
                Image.BorderSizePixel = 1
                Image.BorderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
                Image.Image = "rbxassetid://" .. tostring(itemData.Image)
                Image.Parent = ItemFrame

                local NameLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
                NameLabel.Size = UDim2.new(0.35, -80, 0, 20)
                NameLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0, 76, 0, 8)
                NameLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                NameLabel.Text = itemData.Name
                NameLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
                NameLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
                NameLabel.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSans
                NameLabel.TextSize = 14
                NameLabel.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
                NameLabel.Parent = ItemFrame

                local PriceLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
                PriceLabel.Size = UDim2.new(0.35, -80, 0, 18)
                PriceLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0, 76, 0, 28)
                PriceLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                PriceLabel.Text = "Price: " .. tostring(itemData.Price) .. " Robux"
                PriceLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
                PriceLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
                PriceLabel.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSans
                PriceLabel.TextSize = 12
                PriceLabel.Parent = ItemFrame

                local IDLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
                IDLabel.Size = UDim2.new(0.35, -80, 0, 18)
                IDLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0, 76, 0, 46)
                IDLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                IDLabel.Text = "ID: " .. tostring(itemData.ID)
                IDLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(128, 128, 128)
                IDLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
                IDLabel.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSans
                IDLabel.TextSize = 12
                IDLabel.Parent = ItemFrame

                local DropdownFrame = Instance.new("Frame")
                DropdownFrame.Size = UDim2.new(0, 130, 0, 24)
                DropdownFrame.Position = UDim2.new(1, -240, 0.5, -12)
                DropdownFrame.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
                DropdownFrame.BorderSizePixel = 1
                DropdownFrame.BorderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
                DropdownFrame.ZIndex = 100
                DropdownFrame.Parent = ItemFrame

                local DropdownBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
                DropdownBtn.Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 1, 0)
                DropdownBtn.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                DropdownBtn.Text = LocalPlayer.Name
                DropdownBtn.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSans
                DropdownBtn.TextSize = 13
                DropdownBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
                DropdownBtn.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
                DropdownBtn.Parent = DropdownFrame

                local DropdownArrow = Instance.new("TextButton")
                DropdownArrow.Size = UDim2.new(0, 20, 1, 0)
                DropdownArrow.Position = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 0)
                DropdownArrow.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(192, 192, 192)
                DropdownArrow.BorderSizePixel = 1
                DropdownArrow.BorderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
                DropdownArrow.Text = "v"
                DropdownArrow.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSans
                DropdownArrow.TextSize = 10
                DropdownArrow.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
                DropdownArrow.Parent = DropdownFrame

                local ListContainer = Instance.new("Frame")
                ListContainer.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 120)
                ListContainer.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0)
                ListContainer.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
                ListContainer.BorderSizePixel = 1
                ListContainer.BorderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
                ListContainer.Visible = false
                ListContainer.ZIndex = 1000
                ListContainer.Parent = DropdownFrame

                local ListScroll = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame")
                ListScroll.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
                ListScroll.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                ListScroll.BorderSizePixel = 0
                ListScroll.ScrollBarThickness = 8
                ListScroll.ScrollBarImageColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(192, 192, 192)
                ListScroll.ZIndex = 1000
                ListScroll.Parent = ListContainer

                local ListLayout = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
                ListLayout.Parent = ListScroll

                local selectedUserId = LocalPlayer.UserId

                local function refreshDropdown()
                    for _, child in pairs(ListScroll:GetChildren()) do
                        if child:IsA("TextButton") then
                            child:Destroy()
                        end
                    end
                    
                    local heightCount = 0
                    
                    local selfBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
                    selfBtn.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 22)
                    selfBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(200, 200, 255)
                    selfBtn.BorderSizePixel = 0
                    selfBtn.Text = LocalPlayer.Name .. " (You)"
                    selfBtn.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSans
                    selfBtn.TextSize = 13
                    selfBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
                    selfBtn.ZIndex = 1000
                    selfBtn.Parent = ListScroll
                    
                    selfBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                        DropdownBtn.Text = LocalPlayer.Name
                        selectedUserId = LocalPlayer.UserId
                        ListContainer.Visible = false
                    end)
                    
                    heightCount = heightCount + 22
                    
                    for _, p in pairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                        if p ~= LocalPlayer then
                            local pBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
                            pBtn.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 22)
                            pBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
                            pBtn.BorderSizePixel = 0
                            pBtn.Text = p.Name
                            pBtn.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSans
                            pBtn.TextSize = 13
                            pBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
                            pBtn.ZIndex = 1000
                            pBtn.Parent = ListScroll
                            
                            pBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                                DropdownBtn.Text = p.Name
                                selectedUserId = p.UserId
                                ListContainer.Visible = false
                            end)
                            
                            heightCount = heightCount + 22
                        end
                    end
                    
                    ListScroll.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, heightCount)
                end

                local function toggleDropdown()
                    if ListContainer.Visible then
                        ListContainer.Visible = false
                    else
                        refreshDropdown()
                        ListContainer.Visible = true
                    end
                end

                DropdownBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(toggleDropdown)
                DropdownArrow.MouseButton1Click:Connect(toggleDropdown)
                table.insert(updateListFuncs, refreshDropdown)

                local PurchaseBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
                PurchaseBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 100, 0, 26)
                PurchaseBtn.Position = UDim2.new(1, -105, 0.5, -13)
                PurchaseBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(192, 192, 192)
                PurchaseBtn.BorderSizePixel = 1
                PurchaseBtn.BorderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
                PurchaseBtn.Text = "PURCHASE"
                PurchaseBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
                PurchaseBtn.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSans
                PurchaseBtn.TextSize = 13
                PurchaseBtn.ZIndex = 2
                PurchaseBtn.Parent = ItemFrame

                PurchaseBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                    local targetId = selectedUserId or LocalPlayer.UserId
                    local giftArgs = {
                        {
                            Goal = "Gift Gamepass",
                            GiftData = {
                                Receiver = targetId,
                                Gamepass = itemData.ID
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    pcall(function()
                        LocalPlayer.Character:WaitForChild("Communicate"):FireServer(unpack(giftArgs))
                    end)
                end)

                return ItemFrame
            end

            local function loadStoreItems()
                for _, child in pairs(ScrollingFrame:GetChildren()) do
                    if child:IsA("Frame") then
                        child:Destroy()
                    end
                end
                
                table.clear(updateListFuncs)
                
                local success, Info = pcall(function() return require(ReplicatedStorage.Info) end)
                if not success or not Info or not Info.Limited then return end

                local limitedList = {}
                for _, item in pairs(Info.Limited) do
                    if type(item) == "table" and item.Name and item.ID then
                        table.insert(limitedList, { Name = item.Name, ID = item.ID })
                    end
                end

                local devProducts = {}
                pcall(function()
                    for _, prod in ipairs(MarketplaceService:GetDeveloperProductsAsync():GetCurrentPage()) do
                        devProducts[prod.Name] = prod
                    end
                end)

                local totalCount = 0
                for _, limitedItem in ipairs(limitedList) do
                    local prodMatch = devProducts[limitedItem.Name]
                    local data = {
                        Image = prodMatch and prodMatch.IconImageAssetId or 0,
                        Name = limitedItem.Name,
                        Price = prodMatch and prodMatch.PriceInRobux or 0,
                        ID = prodMatch and prodMatch.ProductId or limitedItem.ID
                    }
                    createItem(data)
                    totalCount = totalCount + 1
                end

                TitleLabel.Text = "Emote Store (" .. totalCount .. ")"
            end

            loadStoreItems()
            task.spawn(function()
                while ScreenGui and ScreenGui.Parent do
                    task.wait(30)
                    loadStoreItems()
                end
            end)
        end

        ScreenGui.Enabled = isEnabled
    end,
})


shopSection:Toggle({
    Title = "buy limited emotes v2",
    Value = false,
    Callback = function(state)
        local Players = game:GetService("Players")
        local TweenService = game:GetService("TweenService")
        local UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")
        local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
        local MarketplaceService = game:GetService("MarketplaceService")

        local Player = Players.LocalPlayer

        -- Cleanup previous instance if it exists
        if _G.KhengieHubGui and _G.KhengieHubGui.Parent then
            _G.KhengieHubGui:Destroy()
            _G.KhengieHubGui = nil
        end

        if not state then 
            return 
        end

        local UIContainer = nil
        if gethui then
            local success, result = pcall(gethui)
            if success and result then
                UIContainer = result
            end
        end
        if not UIContainer then
            UIContainer = Player:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")
        end

        local khengieHub = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
        khengieHub.Name = "khengieHub"
        khengieHub.ResetOnSpawn = false
        khengieHub.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling
        khengieHub.Parent = UIContainer
        _G.KhengieHubGui = khengieHub

        local Library = {}

        function Library:CreateWindow()
            local Window = {}
            
            local ToggleButton = Instance.new("TextButton")
            ToggleButton.Size = UDim2.new(0, 45, 0, 45)
            ToggleButton.Position = UDim2.new(0, 15, 0.5, 0)
            ToggleButton.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(20, 20, 20)
            ToggleButton.Text = "Khengie"
            ToggleButton.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
            ToggleButton.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
            ToggleButton.TextSize = 14
            ToggleButton.Parent = khengieHub
            
            local ToggleCorner = Instance.new("UICorner")
            ToggleCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
            ToggleCorner.Parent = ToggleButton
            
            local ToggleStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
            ToggleStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(45, 45, 45)
            ToggleStroke.Thickness = 1
            ToggleStroke.Parent = ToggleButton

            local MainFrame = Instance.new("Frame")
            MainFrame.Size = UDim2.new(0, 450, 0, 300)
            MainFrame.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -225, 0.5, -150)
            MainFrame.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(15, 15, 15)
            MainFrame.ClipsDescendants = true
            MainFrame.Parent = khengieHub
            
            Window.MainFrame = MainFrame
            Window.GUI = khengieHub

            local MainCorner = Instance.new("UICorner")
            MainCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)
            MainCorner.Parent = MainFrame
            
            local MainStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
            MainStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(45, 45, 45)
            MainStroke.Thickness = 1
            MainStroke.Parent = MainFrame

            local TopBar = Instance.new("Frame")
            TopBar.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 40)
            TopBar.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            TopBar.Parent = MainFrame

            local Title = Instance.new("TextLabel")
            Title.Size = UDim2.new(0, 100, 1, 0)
            Title.Position = UDim2.new(0, 15, 0, 0)
            Title.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            Title.Text = "khengie hub"
            Title.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
            Title.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
            Title.TextSize = 14
            Title.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
            Title.Parent = TopBar

            local TabContainer = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame")
            TabContainer.Size = UDim2.new(1, -120, 1, -10)
            TabContainer.Position = UDim2.new(0, 110, 0, 5)
            TabContainer.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            TabContainer.ScrollBarThickness = 0
            TabContainer.ScrollingDirection = Enum.ScrollingDirection.X
            TabContainer.Parent = TopBar

            local TabListLayout = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
            TabListLayout.FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal
            TabListLayout.Padding = UDim.new(0, 5)
            TabListLayout.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
            TabListLayout.Parent = TabContainer

            TabListLayout:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteContentSize"):Connect(function()
                TabContainer.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, TabListLayout.AbsoluteContentSize.X, 0, 0)
            end)

            local ContentContainer = Instance.new("Frame")
            ContentContainer.Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 1, -50)
            ContentContainer.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 40)
            ContentContainer.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            ContentContainer.Parent = MainFrame

            ToggleButton.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                MainFrame.Visible = not MainFrame.Visible
            end)

            local toggleDragging = false
            local toggleDragInput, toggleMousePos, toggleFramePos

            ToggleButton.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
                if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                    toggleDragging = true
                    toggleMousePos = input.Position
                    toggleFramePos = ToggleButton.Position
                    
                    input.Changed:Connect(function()
                        if input.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then
                            toggleDragging = false
                        end
                    end)
                end
            end)

            ToggleButton.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
                if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                    toggleDragInput = input
                end
            end)

            local mainDragging = false
            local mainDragInput, mainMousePos, mainFramePos

            TopBar.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
                if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                    mainDragging = true
                    mainMousePos = input.Position
                    mainFramePos = MainFrame.Position
                    
                    input.Changed:Connect(function()
                        if input.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then
                            mainDragging = false
                        end
                    end)
                end
            end)

            TopBar.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
                if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                    mainDragInput = input
                end
            end)

            UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
                if input == toggleDragInput and toggleDragging then
                    local delta = input.Position - toggleMousePos
                    ToggleButton.Position = UDim2.new(toggleFramePos.X.Scale, toggleFramePos.X.Offset + delta.X, toggleFramePos.Y.Scale, toggleFramePos.Y.Offset + delta.Y)
                elseif input == mainDragInput and mainDragging then
                    local delta = input.Position - mainMousePos
                    MainFrame.Position = UDim2.new(mainFramePos.X.Scale, mainFramePos.X.Offset + delta.X, mainFramePos.Y.Scale, mainFramePos.Y.Offset + delta.Y)
                end
            end)

            local Tabs = {}
            local firstTab = true

            local function MakeTabButton(name, targetPage)
                local TabButton = Instance.new("TextButton")
                TabButton.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0)
                TabButton.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.X
                TabButton.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(25, 25, 25)
                TabButton.Text = "  " .. name .. "  "
                TabButton.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(150, 150, 150)
                TabButton.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium
                TabButton.TextSize = 12
                TabButton.Parent = TabContainer

                local TabCorner = Instance.new("UICorner")
                TabCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6)
                TabCorner.Parent = TabButton

                if firstTab then
                    TabButton.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
                    TabButton.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(40, 40, 40)
                    targetPage.Visible = true
                    firstTab = false
                else
                    targetPage.Visible = false
                end

                table.insert(Tabs, {Button = TabButton, Page = targetPage})

                TabButton.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                    for _, t in pairs(Tabs) do
                        t.Page.Visible = false
                        t.Button.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(150, 150, 150)
                        TweenService:Create(t.Button, TweenInfo.new(0.2), {BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(25, 25, 25)}):Play()
                    end
                    targetPage.Visible = true
                    TabButton.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
                    TweenService:Create(TabButton, TweenInfo.new(0.2), {BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(40, 40, 40)}):Play()
                end)
            end

            function Window:CreateTab(name)
                local Tab = {}
                
                local Page = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame")
                Page.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
                Page.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                Page.ScrollBarThickness = 2
                Page.ScrollBarImageColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(40, 40, 40)
                Page.Parent = ContentContainer
                
                Tab.Page = Page 

                local PageLayout = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
                PageLayout.Padding = UDim.new(0, 8)
                PageLayout.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
                PageLayout.Parent = Page

                PageLayout:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteContentSize"):Connect(function()
                    Page.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, PageLayout.AbsoluteContentSize.Y + 10)
                end)

                MakeTabButton(name, Page)

                function Tab:CreateParagraph(title, desc)
                    local ParaFrame = Instance.new("Frame")
                    ParaFrame.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)
                    ParaFrame.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
                    ParaFrame.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(25, 25, 25)
                    ParaFrame.Parent = Page

                    local ParaCorner = Instance.new("UICorner")
                    ParaCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6)
                    ParaCorner.Parent = ParaFrame

                    local ParaStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
                    ParaStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(45, 45, 45)
                    ParaStroke.Thickness = 1
                    ParaStroke.Parent = ParaFrame

                    local ParaLayout = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
                    ParaLayout.Padding = UDim.new(0, 4)
                    ParaLayout.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
                    ParaLayout.Parent = ParaFrame

                    local ParaPadding = Instance.new("UIPadding")
                    ParaPadding.PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 10)
                    ParaPadding.PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 10)
                    ParaPadding.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 10)
                    ParaPadding.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 10)
                    ParaPadding.Parent = ParaFrame

                    local ParaTitle = Instance.new("TextLabel")
                    ParaTitle.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 16)
                    ParaTitle.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                    ParaTitle.Text = title
                    ParaTitle.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
                    ParaTitle.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
                    ParaTitle.TextSize = 13
                    ParaTitle.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
                    ParaTitle.Parent = ParaFrame

                    local ParaDesc = Instance.new("TextLabel")
                    ParaDesc.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)
                    ParaDesc.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
                    ParaDesc.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                    ParaDesc.Text = desc
                    ParaDesc.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(170, 170, 170)
                    ParaDesc.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
                    ParaDesc.TextSize = 12
                    ParaDesc.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
                    ParaDesc.TextWrapped = true
                    ParaDesc.Parent = ParaFrame
                end

                function Tab:CreateButton(text, callback)
                    local BtnFrame = Instance.new("Frame")
                    BtnFrame.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 35)
                    BtnFrame.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(25, 25, 25)
                    BtnFrame.Parent = Page

                    local BtnCorner = Instance.new("UICorner")
                    BtnCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6)
                    BtnCorner.Parent = BtnFrame

                    local BtnStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
                    BtnStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(45, 45, 45)
                    BtnStroke.Thickness = 1
                    BtnStroke.Parent = BtnFrame

                    local Button = Instance.new("TextButton")
                    Button.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
                    Button.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                    Button.Text = text
                    Button.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
                    Button.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium
                    Button.TextSize = 12
                    Button.Parent = BtnFrame

                    Button.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
                        TweenService:Create(BtnFrame, TweenInfo.new(0.2), {BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(35, 35, 35)}):Play()
                    end)

                    Button.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
                        TweenService:Create(BtnFrame, TweenInfo.new(0.2), {BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(25, 25, 25)}):Play()
                    end)

                    Button.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                        pcall(callback)
                    end)
                end

                function Tab:CreateImage(source)
                    local ImgFrame = Instance.new("Frame")
                    ImgFrame.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 160)
                    ImgFrame.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(20, 20, 20)
                    ImgFrame.Parent = Page

                    local ImgCorner = Instance.new("UICorner")
                    ImgCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6)
                    ImgCorner.Parent = ImgFrame

                    local ImgStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
                    ImgStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(45, 45, 45)
                    ImgStroke.Thickness = 1
                    ImgStroke.Parent = ImgFrame

                    local ImageLabel = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
                    ImageLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, -16, 1, -16)
                    ImageLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0, 8, 0, 8)
                    ImageLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                    ImageLabel.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Fit
                    ImageLabel.Parent = ImgFrame

                    local isHttp = string.find(source, "http")
                    
                    if isHttp then
                        task.spawn(function()
                            local s, req = pcall(function() return game:HttpGet(source) end)
                            if s and req and writefile and getcustomasset then
                                local filename = "khengie_" .. tostring(math.random(11111, 99999)) .. ".png"
                                writefile(filename, req)
                                ImageLabel.Image = getcustomasset(filename)
                            end
                        end)
                    elseif tonumber(source) then
                        ImageLabel.Image = "rbxassetid://108649453985291"
                    else
                        ImageLabel.Image = source
                    end
                end

                return Tab
            end

            function Window:CreateCustomTab(name)
                local Tab = {}
                
                local Page = Instance.new("Frame")
                Page.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
                Page.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                Page.Parent = ContentContainer
                
                Tab.Container = Page
                MakeTabButton(name, Page)
                
                return Tab
            end

            return Window
        end

        local khengieWindow = Library:CreateWindow()

        local MainTab = khengieWindow:CreateTab("Main")

        MainTab:CreateParagraph("Welcome to khengie Hub", "This is a tsb (the strongest battlegrounds ) script, made by zuri.")

        MainTab:CreateButton("Copy Discord to clipboard", function()
            setclipboard("https://discord.gg/ZXSuYC3upG")
        end)

        MainTab:CreateImage("https://raw.githubusercontent.com/KHATARSISZX/1/refs/heads/main/07185f8b-eb56-4e04-9e55-82c1903028f1.png")

        local plr_1a = Player
        local c_tbl1 = {
            B1 = Color3.fromRGB(15, 15, 15),
            S1 = Color3.fromRGB(25, 25, 25),
            H1 = Color3.fromRGB(40, 40, 40),
            T1 = Color3.fromRGB(240, 240, 240),
            A1 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255),
            K1 = Color3.fromRGB(45, 45, 45)
        }

        local function f_c1(c_1, p_1)
            local i_1 = Instance.new(c_1)
            for k_1, v_1 in pairs(p_1) do i_1[k_1] = v_1 end
            return i_1
        end

        local function f_r2(p_1, r_1)
            f_c1("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, r_1), Parent = p_1 })
        end

        local function f_s3(p_1)
            f_c1("UIStroke", { Color = c_tbl1.K1, Thickness = 1, Parent = p_1 })
        end

        local BuyTab = khengieWindow:CreateCustomTab("BUY Limites Emotes")

        local sf_l1 = f_c1("ScrollingFrame", {
            Size = UDim2.new(0, 200, 1, -10),
            Position = UDim2.new(0, 5, 0, 5),
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            ScrollBarThickness = 4,
            ScrollBarImageColor3 = c_tbl1.H1,
            AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
            CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0),
            ScrollingDirection = Enum.ScrollingDirection.Y,
            Parent = BuyTab.Container
        })

        f_c1("UIListLayout", {
            Padding = UDim.new(0, 6),
            SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder,
            Parent = sf_l1
        })

        local vp_m1 = f_c1("ViewportFrame", {

            Position = UDim2.new(0, 215, 0, 5),

            Ambient = Color3.fromRGB(180, 180, 180),
            LightColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255),

        })
        f_r2(vp_m1, 8)
        f_s3(vp_m1)

        local cam_m1 = Instance.new("Camera")
        vp_m1.CurrentCamera = cam_m1

        local mdl_c1 = nil
        local trk_a1 = nil
        local tbl_c1 = {}
        local b_r1 = false
        local v_l1 = Vector2.zero

        local mod_e1 = nil
        local mod_i2 = nil
        local tbl_e3 = {}
        local tbl_a4 = {}

        pcall(function()
            local f_o1
            f_o1 = hookfunction(TweenService.Create, function(self, inst_1, t_i1, p_1)
                if inst_1 and typeof(inst_1) == "Instance" and (inst_1:IsA("Camera") or inst_1.Name == "Camera") then
                    if p_1 and (p_1.CFrame or p_1.FieldOfView or p_1.Focus) then
                        return f_o1(self, Instance.new("NumberValue"), t_i1, {Value = 0})
                    end
                end
                return f_o1(self, inst_1, t_i1, p_1)
            end)
        end)

        pcall(function()
            mod_e1 = require(ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Emotes"))
            local emote_result = mod_e1:Play(plr_1a.Character, nil, true, nil, true)
            tbl_e3 = (type(emote_result) == "table") and emote_result or {} 
            
            if tbl_e3["BigSlash"] then
                tbl_e3["World Cutting Slash"] = tbl_e3["BigSlash"]
                tbl_e3["BigSlash"] = nil

                for _, v_1 in pairs(mod_i2.Limited) do
                    if type(v_1) == "table" and v_1.Name and v_1.ID then
                        tbl_l5[v_1.Name] = v_1.ID
                    end
                end

                return MarketplaceService:GetDeveloperProductsAsync():GetCurrentPage()

            local tbl_p6 = {}
            if s_2 and p_2 then
                for _, prod_1 in ipairs(p_2) do
                    tbl_p6[prod_1.Name] = prod_1
                end
            end
            
            for n_1, id_1 in pairs(tbl_l5) do
                local prod_2 = tbl_p6[n_1]
                tbl_a4[n_1] = {
                    ID = id_1,
                    ProductId = prod_2 and prod_2.ProductId or id_1,
                    Image = prod_2 and prod_2.IconImageAssetId or 0,
                    Price = prod_2 and prod_2.PriceInRobux or 0
                }
            end
        end)

        local function f_c2()
            for _, i_1 in pairs(tbl_c1) do
                if typeof(i_1) == "Instance" and i_1.Parent then
                    i_1:Destroy()
                elseif typeof(i_1) == "RBXScriptConnection" then
                    i_1:Disconnect()
                end
            end
            tbl_c1 = {}
        end

        vp_m1.InputBegan:Connect(function(i_1)
            if i_1.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or i_1.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                b_r1 = true
                v_l1 = Vector2.new(i_1.Position.X, i_1.Position.Y)
            end
        end)

        UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(i_1)
            if b_r1 and (i_1.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or i_1.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
                local p_1 = Vector2.new(i_1.Position.X, i_1.Position.Y)
                local d_1 = p_1 - v_l1
                v_l1 = p_1
                
                if mdl_c1 then
                    mdl_c1:PivotTo(mdl_c1:GetPivot() * CFrame.Angles(0, math.rad(-d_1.X * 0.7), 0))
                end
            end
        end)

        UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(i_1)
            if i_1.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or i_1.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                b_r1 = false
            end
        end)

        local function f_u1(id_1)
            if mdl_c1 then
                mdl_c1:Destroy()
                f_c2()
            end
            
            local s_1, m_1 = pcall(function() return Players:CreateHumanoidModelFromUserId(id_1) end)
            if s_1 and m_1 then
                m_1:PivotTo(CFrame.new(Vector3.zero))
                m_1.Parent = wm_m1
                
                local h_1 = m_1:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                if h_1 and not h_1:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator") then
                    Instance.new("Animator", h_1)
                end
                
                for _, d_1 in ipairs(m_1:GetDescendants()) do
                    if d_1:IsA("BasePart") then
                        if d_1.Name == "HumanoidRootPart" then
                            d_1.Anchored = true
                        else
                            d_1.Anchored = false
                        end
                    end
                end

                local cf_1 = m_1:GetBoundingBox()
                cam_m1.CFrame = CFrame.new(cf_1.Position + (cf_1.LookVector * 11) + Vector3.new(0, 1.5, 0), cf_1.Position)
                mdl_c1 = m_1
            end
        end

        task.spawn(function() f_u1(plr_1a.UserId) end)

        local function f_p1(str_1)
            if not mdl_c1 or not tbl_e3 then return end
            local h_1 = mdl_c1:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            local a_1 = h_1 and h_1:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator")
            if not a_1 then return end
            
            local d_1 = tbl_e3[str_1]
            if not d_1 then return end

            if trk_a1 then trk_a1:Stop() end
            f_c2()

            local id_1 = d_1.Animation or 0
            if id_1 ~= 0 then
                local an_1 = Instance.new("Animation")
                an_1.AnimationId = "rbxassetid://108649453985291"
                trk_a1 = a_1:LoadAnimation(an_1)
                trk_a1.Looped = d_1.Looped or false
                trk_a1:Play()
            end
        end

        local fr_p1 = f_c1("Frame", {
            Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0),

            Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 45),

        f_r2(btn_b1, 4)

            Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 32),

            TextColor3 = c_tbl1.B1,

        })
        f_r2(btn_s2, 4)

        local fr_s1 = nil
        local str_e1 = nil
        local n_u1 = plr_1a.UserId

        local function f_cp1()
            fr_p1.Visible = false
            if fr_s1 then fr_s1:Destroy() fr_s1 = nil end
        end

        local function f_os1(str_1)
            if fr_s1 then 
                fr_s1:Destroy() 
                fr_s1 = nil 
            end
            
            fr_s1 = f_c1("Frame", {
                Size = UDim2.new(0, 220, 0, 260),
                AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5),
                Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0),
                BackgroundColor3 = c_tbl1.B1,
                ZIndex = 60,
                Active = true,
                Parent = khengieWindow.MainFrame
            })
            f_r2(fr_s1, 10)
            f_s3(fr_s1)

            local top_bar = f_c1("Frame", {
                Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 40),
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                ZIndex = 61,
                Parent = fr_s1
            })

            f_c1("TextLabel", {
                Size = UDim2.new(1, -40, 1, 0),
                Position = UDim2.new(0, 15, 0, 0),
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                Text = "Select Player",
                TextColor3 = c_tbl1.T1,
                Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold,
                TextSize = 13,
                TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
                ZIndex = 61,
                Parent = top_bar
            })

            local cls_btn = f_c1("TextButton", {
                Size = UDim2.new(0, 24, 0, 24),
                Position = UDim2.new(1, -32, 0, 8),
                BackgroundColor3 = c_tbl1.S1,
                Text = "×",
                TextColor3 = c_tbl1.T1,
                Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold,
                TextSize = 16,
                AutoButtonColor = false,
                ZIndex = 61,
                Parent = top_bar
            })
            f_r2(cls_btn, 6)
            
            cls_btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                if fr_s1 then fr_s1:Destroy() fr_s1 = nil end
            end)

            local drag_b = false
            local drag_p, drag_s

            top_bar.InputBegan:Connect(function(i_1)
                if i_1.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or i_1.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                    drag_b = true
                    drag_p = i_1.Position
                    drag_s = fr_s1.Position
                end
            end)

            UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(i_1)
                if drag_b and (i_1.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or i_1.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
                    local d_1 = i_1.Position - drag_p
                    fr_s1.Position = UDim2.new(drag_s.X.Scale, drag_s.X.Offset + d_1.X, drag_s.Y.Scale, drag_s.Y.Offset + d_1.Y)
                end
            end)

            UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(i_1)
                if i_1.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or i_1.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                    drag_b = false
                end
            end)

            local sf_l2 = f_c1("ScrollingFrame", {
                Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 1, -50),
                Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 40),
                BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                ScrollBarThickness = 2,
                ScrollBarImageColor3 = c_tbl1.H1,
                AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
                CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0),
                ZIndex = 61,
                Parent = fr_s1
            })
            f_c1("UIListLayout", { Padding = UDim.new(0, 6), Parent = sf_l2 })

            local function f_cp2(n_1, id_1)
                local b_1 = f_c1("TextButton", {
                    Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 30),
                    BackgroundColor3 = c_tbl1.S1,
                    Text = n_1,
                    TextColor3 = c_tbl1.T1,

                    AutoButtonColor = false,
                    ZIndex = 62,
                    Parent = sf_l2

                end)
                b_1.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
                    TweenService:Create(b_1, TweenInfo.new(0.2), {BackgroundColor3 = c_tbl1.S1}):Play()
                end)
                
                b_1.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                    n_u1 = id_1
                    f_u1(id_1)
                    f_p1(str_1)
                    if fr_s1 then fr_s1:Destroy() fr_s1 = nil end
                end)
            end

            f_cp2(plr_1a.Name .. " (You)", plr_1a.UserId)
            for _, p_1 in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                if p_1 ~= plr_1a then
                    f_cp2(p_1.Name, p_1.UserId)
                end
            end
        end

        btn_b1.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()

            if d_1 then

                    {
                        Goal = "Gift Gamepass",
                        GiftData = {
                            Receiver = n_u1,
                            Gamepass = d_1.ID
                        }
                    }
                }
                pcall(function()
                    plr_1a.Character:WaitForChild("Communicate"):FireServer(unpack(a_1))
                end)
            end
            f_cp1()
        end)

        btn_s2.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            f_os1(str_e1)
            fr_p1.Visible = false
        end)

        local function f_le1()
            if type(tbl_e3) ~= "table" then return end 
            
            for n_1, _ in pairs(tbl_e3) do
                local d_1 = tbl_a4[n_1]
                if d_1 or next(tbl_a4) == nil then
                    local fr_i1 = f_c1("Frame", {
                        Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 45),
                        BackgroundColor3 = c_tbl1.S1,
                        Parent = sf_l1
                    })
                    f_r2(fr_i1, 6)
                    f_s3(fr_i1)

                    local img_i1 = f_c1("ImageLabel", {
                        Size = UDim2.new(0, 35, 0, 35),
                        Position = UDim2.new(0, 5, 0.5, -17.5),
                        BackgroundColor3 = c_tbl1.H1,
                        Image = "rbxassetid://108649453985291",
                        Parent = fr_i1
                    })
                    f_r2(img_i1, 4)

                    f_c1("TextLabel", {
                        Size = UDim2.new(1, -85, 1, 0),
                        Position = UDim2.new(0, 45, 0, 0),
                        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                        Text = n_1,
                        TextColor3 = c_tbl1.T1,
                        Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold,
                        TextSize = 11,
                        TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
                        TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd,
                        Parent = fr_i1
                    })

                    local btn_a1 = f_c1("TextButton", {
                        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0),
                        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                        Text = "",
                        Parent = fr_i1
                    })

                    btn_a1.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
                        TweenService:Create(fr_i1, TweenInfo.new(0.2), {BackgroundColor3 = c_tbl1.H1}):Play()
                    end)
                    btn_a1.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
                        TweenService:Create(fr_i1, TweenInfo.new(0.2), {BackgroundColor3 = c_tbl1.S1}):Play()
                    end)

                    btn_a1.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                        str_e1 = n_1
                        f_p1(n_1)
                        if fr_s1 then fr_s1:Destroy() fr_s1 = nil end
                        fr_p1.Visible = true
                    end)
                end
            end
        end

        f_le1()
    end,
})

-- Universal
