universalTab:Toggle({
    Title = "minecraft death",
    Value = false,
    Callback = function(state)
        local Players = game:GetService("Players")
        local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer

        _G.DeathEffectEnabled = state

        -- Cleanup active connections from previous runs
        if _G.DeathEffectConns then
            for _, conn in ipairs(_G.DeathEffectConns) do
                if typeof(conn) == "RBXScriptConnection" then
                    conn:Disconnect()
                end
            end
        end
        _G.DeathEffectConns = {}

        if not state then return end
        if not LocalPlayer then return end

        --------------------------------------------------------------------------------
        -- Death Effect Logic Setup
        --------------------------------------------------------------------------------
        local function setupDeathEffect(character)
            if not _G.DeathEffectEnabled or not character then return end
            
            local humanoid = character:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 5)
            local rootPart = character:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 5)
            if not humanoid or not rootPart then return end
            
            local isDead = false

            local diedConn = humanoid.Died:Connect(function()
                if not _G.DeathEffectEnabled or isDead then return end
                isDead = true

                local deathPosition = rootPart.Position
                local lookVector = rootPart.CFrame.LookVector
                
                local flatLook = Vector3.new(lookVector.X, 0, lookVector.Z)
                if flatLook.Magnitude > 0 then
                    flatLook = flatLook.Unit
                else
                    flatLook = Vector3.new(0, 0, 1)
                end
                local targetCFrame = CFrame.lookAt(deathPosition, deathPosition + flatLook)

                task.wait(0.9)
                if not _G.DeathEffectEnabled then return end

                for _, object in ipairs(character:GetDescendants()) do
                    if object:IsA("BasePart") or object:IsA("Decal") then
                        object.Transparency = 1
                    elseif object:IsA("Accessory") or object:IsA("Shirt") or object:IsA("Pants") then
                        object:Destroy()
                    end
                end

                local p = Instance.new('Part')
                p.Anchored = true
                p.CanCollide = false
                p.Transparency = 1
                p.CFrame = targetCFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0, -5)
                p.Parent = workspace

                local a0 = Instance.new('Attachment', p)
                local e1 = Instance.new('ParticleEmitter', a0)
                
                e1.Name = 'Break'
                e1.Texture = 'rbxassetid://8733226116'
                e1.Color = ColorSequence.new({ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, Color3.new(1, 1, 1)), ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, Color3.new(1, 1, 1))})
                e1.Size = NumberSequence.new({NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 2, 2), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 2, 2)})
                e1.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
                    NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 1, 0),
                    NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.0935, 0.2562, 0),
                    NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.4048, 0.1875, 0),
                    NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.7048, 0.4375, 0),
                    NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1, 0)
                })
                e1.ZOffset = 3
                e1.EmissionDirection = Enum.NormalId.Right
                e1.Lifetime = NumberRange.new(1, 1.5)
                e1.Rate = 0
                e1.Enabled = false
                e1.Speed = NumberRange.new(0, 10)
                e1.Rotation = NumberRange.new(0, 0)
                e1.RotSpeed = NumberRange.new(0, 0)
                e1.SpreadAngle = Vector2.new(360, 360)
                e1.Acceleration = Vector3.new(0, -4, 0)
                e1.Drag = 1
                e1.LockedToPart = false
                e1.LightEmission = 0
                e1.LightInfluence = 1
                e1.Orientation = Enum.ParticleOrientation.FacingCamera
                e1.Squash = NumberSequence.new({NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0, 0), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 0, 0)})
                e1.Shape = Enum.ParticleEmitterShape.Box
                e1.ShapeInOut = Enum.ParticleEmitterShapeInOut.Outward
                e1.ShapeStyle = Enum.ParticleEmitterShapeStyle.Volume
                e1.FlipbookMode = Enum.ParticleFlipbookMode.OneShot
                e1.FlipbookFramerate = NumberRange.new(50, 300)
                e1.FlipbookLayout = Enum.ParticleFlipbookLayout.Grid4x4
                e1.TimeScale = 1
                e1.Brightness = 20
                
                e1:Emit(20)

                task.wait(2)
                if p and p.Parent then
                    p:Destroy()
                end
            end)
            table.insert(_G.DeathEffectConns, diedConn)
        end

        if LocalPlayer.Character then
            setupDeathEffect(LocalPlayer.Character)
        end

        local charAddedConn = LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(newChar)
            task.wait(0.1)
            if _G.DeathEffectEnabled then
                setupDeathEffect(newChar)
            end
        end)
        table.insert(_G.DeathEffectConns, charAddedConn)
    end,
})

universalTab:Toggle({
    Title = "Extra Slot Emote",
    Value = false,
    Callback = function(state)
        local Players = game:GetService("Players")
        local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer

        if not LocalPlayer then return end

        if state then
            LocalPlayer:SetAttribute("ExtraSlots", true)
            LocalPlayer:SetAttribute("VIPServer", true)
        else
            LocalPlayer:SetAttribute("ExtraSlots", false)
            LocalPlayer:SetAttribute("VIPServer", false)
        end
    end,
})


universalTab:Toggle({
    Title = "get all emote",
    Value = false,
    Callback = function(state)
        local Players = game:GetService("Players")
        local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
        local UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")
        local Debris = game:GetService("Debris")
        local HttpService = game:GetService("HttpService")

        local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer

        _G.AllEmotesEnabled = state

        -- Cleanup active connections from previous runs
        if _G.AllEmotesConns then
            for _, conn in ipairs(_G.AllEmotesConns) do
                if typeof(conn) == "RBXScriptConnection" then
                    conn:Disconnect()
                end
            end
        end
        _G.AllEmotesConns = {}

        if not state then 
            -- Stop active emote if toggled off
            if _G.AllEmotesStopCurrent then
                _G.AllEmotesStopCurrent()
            end
            return 
        end
        if not LocalPlayer then return end

        --------------------------------------------------------------------------------
        -- All Emotes Logic Setup
        --------------------------------------------------------------------------------
        local success, EmotesModule = pcall(function()
            return require(ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Emotes"))
        end)
        local successVFX, VFXModule = pcall(function()
            return require(ReplicatedStorage.Emotes:WaitForChild("VFX"))
        end)

        if not success or not successVFX then return end

        local playingEmotes = EmotesModule:Play(LocalPlayer.Character, nil, true, nil, true)

        if playingEmotes["BigSlash"] then
            playingEmotes["World Cutting Slash"] = playingEmotes["BigSlash"]
            playingEmotes["BigSlash"] = nil
        end

        local emoteNamesList = {}
        for emoteName, _ in pairs(playingEmotes) do
            table.insert(emoteNamesList, emoteName)
        end
        LocalPlayer:SetAttribute("Emotes", HttpService:JSONEncode(emoteNamesList))

        local activeTrack = nil
        local isEmotingActive = false
        local cleanupItems = {}

        local function stopCurrentEmote()
            if not isEmotingActive then return end
            isEmotingActive = false
            
            local character = LocalPlayer.Character
            local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if humanoid then
                humanoid.WalkSpeed = 16
                humanoid.JumpPower = 50
            end
            if character then character:SetAttribute("ForcedCFrame", nil) end
            
            for _, item in pairs(cleanupItems) do
                if typeof(item) == "Instance" and item.Parent then 
                    item:Destroy() 
                elseif typeof(item) == "RBXScriptConnection" then
                    item:Disconnect()
                end
            end
            cleanupItems = {}
            
            if activeTrack then 
                activeTrack:Stop() 
                activeTrack:Destroy() 
                activeTrack = nil 
            end
        end
        _G.AllEmotesStopCurrent = stopCurrentEmote

        local function playEmote(emoteName)
            if not _G.AllEmotesEnabled then return end
            local character = LocalPlayer.Character
            local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if not humanoid then return end
            
            local emoteData = playingEmotes[emoteName]
            if not emoteData then return end

            stopCurrentEmote()
            isEmotingActive = true
            humanoid.WalkSpeed = 0
            humanoid.JumpPower = 0

            local runtimeParams = {}
            local cancelState = {interrupted = false}
            local folder = Instance.new("Folder")

            local animInstance = Instance.new("Animation")
            animInstance.AnimationId = "rbxassetid://" .. (emoteData.Animation or "")
            activeTrack = humanoid:LoadAnimation(animInstance)
            
            local lowerName = emoteName:lower()
            activeTrack.Looped = emoteData.Looped or lowerName:find("idle") or lowerName:find("loop") or lowerName:find("pose") or lowerName:find("rest") or false
            activeTrack:Play()
            
            local moveCheckConn = task.spawn(function()
                while isEmotingActive and task.wait() do
                    if not _G.AllEmotesEnabled then break end
                    if humanoid.MoveDirection.Magnitude > 0 or character:GetAttribute("Dashing") or character:GetAttribute("Dash") then
                        cancelState.interrupted = true
                        stopCurrentEmote()
                        break
                    end
                end
            end)

            if emoteData.Startup then
                task.spawn(function()
                    pcall(function() emoteData.Startup(cleanupItems, activeTrack, runtimeParams, emoteData, cancelState, folder) end)
                end)
            end
            
            task.spawn(function()
                pcall(function()
                    local accessory = Instance.new("Accessory", character)
                    accessory.Name = "LocalVFX"
                    table.insert(cleanupItems, accessory)
                    
                    local targetVFXName = (emoteName == "World Cutting Slash" and "HugeSlash" or emoteName)
                    local vfxFolder = ReplicatedStorage.Emotes.VFX
                    local specificMod = vfxFolder:FindFirstChild("RealAssets") and vfxFolder.RealAssets:FindFirstChild(targetVFXName) 
                                  or vfxFolder:FindFirstChild("VfxMods") and vfxFolder.VfxMods:FindFirstChild(targetVFXName) 
                                  or vfxFolder
                    
                    VFXModule:MainFunction({
                        Character = character,
                        vfxName = targetVFXName,
                        SpecificModule = specificMod,
                        AnimSent = tonumber(emoteData.Animation),
                        RealBind = accessory,
                        CanRotate = true,
                        DirectData = emoteData
                    })
                end)
            end)

            if emoteData.Keyframes then
                for keyframeName, keyframeFunc in pairs(emoteData.Keyframes) do
                    local conn = activeTrack:GetMarkerReachedSignal(keyframeName):Connect(function()
                        if isEmotingActive and not cancelState.interrupted then
                            task.spawn(function()
                                pcall(function() keyframeFunc(runtimeParams, cleanupItems, activeTrack, cancelState) end)
                            end)
                        end
                    end)
                    table.insert(cleanupItems, conn)
                end
            end

            if emoteData.Sounds then
                for delayTime, soundInfo in pairs(emoteData.Sounds) do
                    task.delay(tonumber(delayTime) or 0, function()
                        if isEmotingActive and not cancelState.interrupted then
                            local soundObj = Instance.new("Sound")
                            soundObj.SoundId = soundInfo.SoundId
                            soundObj.Volume = soundInfo.Volume or 1
                            soundObj.Looped = soundInfo.Looped or false
                            soundObj.Parent = soundInfo.ParentTorso and character:FindFirstChild("Torso") or character.PrimaryPart
                            soundObj:Play()
                            table.insert(cleanupItems, soundObj)
                            if not soundObj.Looped then Debris:AddItem(soundObj, soundObj.TimeLength + 2) end
                        end
                    end)
                end
            end

            local stopConn = activeTrack.Stopped:Connect(function()
                if isEmotingActive and not activeTrack.Looped then stopCurrentEmote() end
            end)
            table.insert(cleanupItems, stopConn)
        end

        local jumpConn = UserInputService.JumpRequest:Connect(function()
            if isEmotingActive then stopCurrentEmote() end
        end)
        table.insert(_G.AllEmotesConns, jumpConn)

        local loopTask = task.spawn(function()
            while _G.AllEmotesEnabled and task.wait(0.2) do
                local playerGui = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
                local emotesGui = playerGui and playerGui:FindFirstChild("Emotes")
                if not emotesGui then continue end
                local imageLabel = emotesGui:FindFirstChild("ImageLabel")
                if not imageLabel then continue end

                for i = 1, 8 do
                    local slot = imageLabel:FindFirstChild(tostring(i))
                    local button = slot and slot:FindFirstChild("Button")
                    
                    if button and not button:GetAttribute("HookedUp") then
                        button:SetAttribute("HookedUp", true)
                        local clickConn = button.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
                            if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                                local emoteName = slot:GetAttribute("Emote")
                                if emoteName then playEmote(emoteName) end
                            end
                        end)
                        table.insert(_G.AllEmotesConns, clickConn)
                    end
                end
            end
        end)
    end,
})


universalTab:Toggle({
    Title = "Emote Dash",
    Value = false,
    Callback = function(state)
        local Players = game:GetService("Players")
        local UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")
        local Workspace = game:GetService("Workspace")

        local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
        local Camera = Workspace.CurrentCamera

        getgenv().EmoteDashEnabled = state

        -- Cleanup active connections and UI elements from previous runs
        if _G.EmoteDashConns then
            for _, conn in ipairs(_G.EmoteDashConns) do
                if typeof(conn) == "RBXScriptConnection" then
                    conn:Disconnect()
                end
            end
        end
        _G.EmoteDashConns = {}

        local oldGui = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui") and LocalPlayer.PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("EmoteDashGui")
        if oldGui then
            oldGui:Destroy()
        end

        if not state then return end
        if not LocalPlayer then return end

        --------------------------------------------------------------------------------
        -- Assets & Settings Setup
        --------------------------------------------------------------------------------
        local EMOTE_IDLE = "rbxassetid://83007856881977"
        local EMOTE_PRESS = "rbxassetid://92438163139266"

        local Settings = {
            EmoteDraggable = true
        }

        local isActionActive = false
        local loadedAnims = {}

        --------------------------------------------------------------------------------
        -- Helper Functions
        --------------------------------------------------------------------------------
        local function getCharacterAndHumanoid()
            local Character = LocalPlayer.Character
            local Humanoid = Character and Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            return Character, Humanoid
        end

        local function stopAllAnimations()
            local _, humanoid = getCharacterAndHumanoid()
            local animator = humanoid and humanoid:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator")
            if animator then
                pcall(function()
                    for _, track in ipairs(animator:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()) do
                        track:Stop()
                    end
                end)
            end
        end

        local function playAnimation(animationId)
            local _, humanoid = getCharacterAndHumanoid()
            if humanoid then
                local animator = humanoid:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator") or Instance.new("Animator", humanoid)
                local animation = loadedAnims[animationId] or Instance.new("Animation")
                if not loadedAnims[animationId] then
                    animation.AnimationId = "rbxassetid://" .. tostring(animationId)
                    loadedAnims[animationId] = animation
                end
                local success, track = pcall(function() return animator:LoadAnimation(animation) end)
                if success and track then
                    track.Priority = Enum.AnimationPriority.Action
                    track:Play()
                    pcall(function() track:AdjustSpeed(1.2) end)
                end
            end
        end

        local function turnCamera(degrees)
            if not isActionActive then
                isActionActive = true
                local character, _ = getCharacterAndHumanoid()
                local hrp = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if hrp then
                    pcall(function()
                        hrp.CFrame = hrp.CFrame * CFrame.Angles(0, math.rad(degrees), 0)
                        local cameraPosition = Camera and Camera.CFrame.Position or hrp.Position + Vector3.new(0, 5, 0)
                        local cameraY = cameraPosition.Y
                        local offsetVector = cameraPosition - hrp.Position
                        local horizontalOffset = Vector3.new(offsetVector.X, 0, offsetVector.Z)
                        local rotatedOffset = CFrame.fromAxisAngle(Vector3.yAxis, math.rad(degrees)) * horizontalOffset
                        local newCameraLookAt = hrp.Position + rotatedOffset
                        if Camera and Camera:IsA("Camera") then
                            Camera.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(Vector3.new(newCameraLookAt.X, cameraY, newCameraLookAt.Z), hrp.Position)
                        end
                    end)
                end
                isActionActive = false
            end
        end

        local function applyBodyVelocity(directionX, distanceMultiplier, verticalForce, maxSpeed, duration)
            if not isActionActive then
                isActionActive = true
                local character, _ = getCharacterAndHumanoid()
                local hrp = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if hrp then
                    local bodyVelocity = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
                    local velocityVector = hrp.CFrame.RightVector * directionX * distanceMultiplier + Vector3.new(0, verticalForce, 0)
                    if velocityVector.Magnitude == 0 then velocityVector = Vector3.new(0, 1, 0) end
                    bodyVelocity.Velocity = velocityVector.Unit * maxSpeed
                    bodyVelocity.MaxForce = Vector3.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge)
                    bodyVelocity.Parent = hrp
                    task.delay(duration, function() pcall(function() bodyVelocity:Destroy() end) end)
                end
                isActionActive = false
            end
        end

        local function EMOTE_DASH_FUNCTION()
            if not getgenv().EmoteDashEnabled then return end
            stopAllAnimations()
            pcall(function() playAnimation(10480793962) end)
            pcall(function() turnCamera(90) end)
            pcall(function() applyBodyVelocity(1, 38, 8, 95, 0.27) end)
        end

        --------------------------------------------------------------------------------
        -- Floating Draggable Button Setup
        --------------------------------------------------------------------------------
        local playerGui = LocalPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")
        local ScreenGui = Instance.new("ScreenGui", playerGui)
        ScreenGui.Name = "EmoteDashGui"
        ScreenGui.ResetOnSpawn = false

        local emoteButton = Instance.new("ImageButton", ScreenGui)
        emoteButton.Name = "EmoteDashButton"
        emoteButton.Size = UDim2.new(0, 60, 0, 60)
        emoteButton.Position = UDim2.new(0.85, 0, 0.6, 0)
        emoteButton.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        emoteButton.Image = EMOTE_IDLE
        emoteButton.Visible = true

        local function makeButtonDraggable(btn, settingKey)
            local dragToggle = false
            local dragStart = nil
            local startPos = nil

            local conn1 = btn.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
                if (input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) and Settings[settingKey] then
                    dragToggle = true
                    dragStart = input.Position
                    startPos = btn.Position
                end
            end)
            table.insert(_G.EmoteDashConns, conn1)
            
            local conn2 = btn.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
                if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                    dragToggle = false
                end
            end)
            table.insert(_G.EmoteDashConns, conn2)
            
            local conn3 = UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
                if dragToggle and (input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
                    local delta = input.Position - dragStart
                    btn.Position = UDim2.new(startPos.X.Scale, startPos.X.Offset + delta.X, startPos.Y.Scale, startPos.Y.Offset + delta.Y)
                end
            end)
            table.insert(_G.EmoteDashConns, conn3)
        end

        makeButtonDraggable(emoteButton, "EmoteDraggable")

        local connDown = emoteButton.MouseButton1Down:Connect(function() emoteButton.Image = EMOTE_PRESS end)
        local connUp = emoteButton.MouseButton1Up:Connect(function() emoteButton.Image = EMOTE_IDLE end)
        local connLeave = emoteButton.MouseLeave:Connect(function() emoteButton.Image = EMOTE_IDLE end)
        local connClick = emoteButton.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() pcall(EMOTE_DASH_FUNCTION) end)

        table.insert(_G.EmoteDashConns, connDown)
        table.insert(_G.EmoteDashConns, connUp)
        table.insert(_G.EmoteDashConns, connLeave)
        table.insert(_G.EmoteDashConns, connClick)
    end,
})

local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer

-- Global state management to prevent double-hooking
_G.SemiGodmode = _G.SemiGodmode or {
    Enabled = false,
    Loc = nil,
    Connection = nil,
    Hooked = false
}

local rootPart = nil
local function updateCharacter(character)
    rootPart = character:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart")
end

if LocalPlayer.Character then
    updateCharacter(LocalPlayer.Character)
end
LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(updateCharacter)

-- Hook __index only once
if not _G.SemiGodmode.Hooked then
    _G.SemiGodmode.Hooked = true
    local oldIndex
    oldIndex = hookmetamethod(game, "__index", newcclosure(function(self, key)
        if _G.SemiGodmode.Enabled and not checkcaller() and key == "CFrame" and self == rootPart then
            if _G.SemiGodmode.Loc then
                return _G.SemiGodmode.Loc
            end
        end
        return oldIndex(self, key)
    end))
end

-- Toggle Element
universalTab:Toggle({
    Title = "Invisible Semi Godmode",
    Value = false,
    Callback = function(state)
        _G.SemiGodmode.Enabled = state

        if _G.SemiGodmode.Connection then
            _G.SemiGodmode.Connection:Disconnect()
            _G.SemiGodmode.Connection = nil
        end

        if state then
            _G.SemiGodmode.Connection = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
                if _G.SemiGodmode.Enabled and rootPart then
                    _G.SemiGodmode.Loc = rootPart.CFrame
                    rootPart.CFrame = _G.SemiGodmode.Loc * CFrame.new(0, -6, 0)
                    RunService.RenderStepped:Wait()
                    rootPart.CFrame = _G.SemiGodmode.Loc
                end
            end)
        else
            _G.SemiGodmode.Loc = nil
        end
    end,
})

universalTab:Toggle({
    Title = "FPS & Ping Display",
    Value = false,
    Callback = function(state)
        local Players = game:GetService("Players")
        local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
        local Stats = game:GetService("Stats")
        local CoreGui = game:GetService("CoreGui")
        local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer

        -- Disconnect loop and destroy HUD elements when toggled off or refreshed
        if _G.FPSHudConnection then
            _G.FPSHudConnection:Disconnect()
            _G.FPSHudConnection = nil
        end

        local existingCoreGui = CoreGui:FindFirstChild("CustomFPSPingHUD")
        if existingCoreGui then
            existingCoreGui:Destroy()
        end

        if LocalPlayer and LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui") then
            local topbar = LocalPlayer.PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("TopbarStandard")
            if topbar and topbar:FindFirstChild("Holders") and topbar.Holders:FindFirstChild("Left") then
                local left = topbar.Holders.Left
                if left:FindFirstChild("CustomFPSHUD") then left.CustomFPSHUD:Destroy() end
                if left:FindFirstChild("CustomPingHUD") then left.CustomPingHUD:Destroy() end
            end
        end

        if not state or not LocalPlayer then return end

        local function GetPing()
            local ok, val = pcall(function()
                return LocalPlayer:GetNetworkPing() * 1000
            end)
            if ok and val and val > 0 then
                return math.floor(val)
            end
            
            local ok2, val2 = pcall(function()
                return Stats.Network.ServerStatsItem["Data Ping"]:GetValue()
            end)
            return (ok2 and val2) and math.floor(val2) or 0
        end

        local TextLabel, PingLabel
        local playerGui = LocalPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")
        local topbarStandard = playerGui:FindFirstChild("TopbarStandard")

        if topbarStandard and topbarStandard:FindFirstChild("Holders") and topbarStandard.Holders:FindFirstChild("Left") then
            local Left = topbarStandard.Holders.Left
            
            local Frame = Instance.new("Frame", Left)
            Frame.Name = "CustomFPSHUD"
            Frame.Size = UDim2.new(0, 96, 0, 44)
            Frame.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(18, 18, 21)
            Frame.BackgroundTransparency = 0.08
            Instance.new("UICorner", Frame).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
            
            TextLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel", Frame)
            TextLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
            TextLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            TextLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
            TextLabel.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSansBold
            TextLabel.TextSize = 18

            local Frame2 = Instance.new("Frame", Left)
            Frame2.Name = "CustomPingHUD"
            Frame2.Size = UDim2.new(0, 96, 0, 44)
            Frame2.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(18, 18, 21)
            Frame2.BackgroundTransparency = 0.08
            Instance.new("UICorner", Frame2).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
            
            PingLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel", Frame2)
            PingLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
            PingLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            PingLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
            PingLabel.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSansBold
            PingLabel.TextSize = 18
        else
            local ScreenGui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
            ScreenGui.Name = "CustomFPSPingHUD"
            ScreenGui.Parent = CoreGui

            local Frame1 = Instance.new("Frame", ScreenGui)
            Frame1.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 48)
            Frame1.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 10)
            Frame1.BackgroundTransparency = 1

            local Frame2 = Instance.new("Frame", Frame1)
            Frame2.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 44)
            Frame2.Position = UDim2.new(0, 172, 0, 2)
            Frame2.BackgroundTransparency = 1

            local UIListLayout = Instance.new("UIListLayout", Frame2)
            UIListLayout.Padding = UDim.new(0, 12)
            UIListLayout.FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal
            UIListLayout.HorizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Left
            UIListLayout.VerticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Bottom

            local Frame3 = Instance.new("Frame", Frame2)
            Frame3.Size = UDim2.new(0, 96, 0, 44)
            Frame3.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(18, 18, 21)
            Frame3.BackgroundTransparency = 0.08
            Instance.new("UICorner", Frame3).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)

            TextLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel", Frame3)
            TextLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
            TextLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            TextLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
            TextLabel.TextSize = 18
            TextLabel.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSansBold

            local Frame4 = Instance.new("Frame", Frame2)
            Frame4.Size = UDim2.new(0, 96, 0, 44)
            Frame4.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(18, 18, 21)
            Frame4.BackgroundTransparency = 0.08
            Instance.new("UICorner", Frame4).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)

            PingLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel", Frame4)
            PingLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
            PingLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            PingLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
            PingLabel.TextSize = 18
            PingLabel.Font = Enum.Font.BuilderSansBold
        end

        local timer = 0
        _G.FPSHudConnection = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
            timer = timer + dt
            if timer >= 0.1 then
                timer = 0
                if TextLabel then
                    TextLabel.Text = math.floor(1 / dt) .. " FPS"
                end
                if PingLabel then
                    PingLabel.Text = GetPing() .. " ms"
                end
            end
        end)
    end,
})


-- ==========================================
-- ANIMATION LOGIC & PRESETS
-- ==========================================

-- Global Config and Connections
_G.TSBESPConnections = _G.TSBESPConnections or {}
_G.TSBESPHighlights = _G.TSBESPHighlights or {}
_G.TSBESPState = false

visualTab:Toggle({
    Title = "Character Player ESP",
    Value = false,
    Callback = function(state)
        local Players = game:GetService("Players")
        local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer

        _G.TSBESPState = state

        -- Skill-to-Color Mapping
        local SkillMap = {
            ["shove"] = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 0),          -- Yellow (Strongest)
            ["table flip"] = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 0),     -- Yellow (Strongest)
            ["flowing water"] = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 255),    -- Blue (Hero Hunter)
            ["jet dive"] = Color3.fromRGB(255, 165, 0),       -- Orange (Cyborg)
            ["scatter"] = Color3.fromRGB(128, 0, 128),        -- Purple (Ninja)
            ["beatdown"] = Color3.fromRGB(128, 128, 128),     -- Gray (Demon)
            ["atmos cleave"] = Color3.fromRGB(255, 140, 0),    -- Dark Orange (Blade Master)
            ["windstorm furry"] = Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 0),   -- Green (Psychic)
            ["vanishing kick"] = Color3.fromRGB(150, 0, 255),  -- Purple (Martial Artist)
            ["erase"] = Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 255),          -- Light Blue (Sorcerer)
            ["plasma cannon"] = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0),      -- Black (Tech Prodigy)
            ["ravage"] = Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 0),            -- Red (KJ)
        }

        local function GetHeroColor(player)
            local char = player.Character
            local backpack = player:FindFirstChild("Backpack")
            
            local searchContainers = {}
            if char then table.insert(searchContainers, char) end
            if backpack then table.insert(searchContainers, backpack) end

            for _, container in ipairs(searchContainers) do
                for _, item in ipairs(container:GetDescendants()) do
                    for skill, color in pairs(SkillMap) do
                        if string.find(item.Name:lower(), skill:lower()) then
                            return color
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
            return Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255) -- Default white
        end

        local function removeHighlight(char)
            if _G.TSBESPHighlights[char] then
                _G.TSBESPHighlights[char]:Destroy()
                _G.TSBESPHighlights[char] = nil
            end
        end

        local function createHighlight(player, char)
            if not char then return end
            removeHighlight(char)

            local highlight = Instance.new("Highlight")
            highlight.Name = "TSB_ESP"
            highlight.Adornee = char
            highlight.FillTransparency = 0.5
            highlight.OutlineTransparency = 0
            highlight.DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop
            highlight.Parent = char

            _G.TSBESPHighlights[char] = highlight

            task.spawn(function()
                while _G.TSBESPState and highlight.Parent do
                    local targetColor = GetHeroColor(player)
                    highlight.FillColor = targetColor
                    highlight.OutlineColor = targetColor
                    task.wait(2)
                end
            end)
        end

        -- Cleanup when disabled
        if not state then
            for char, hl in pairs(_G.TSBESPHighlights) do
                if hl then hl:Destroy() end
            end
            table.clear(_G.TSBESPHighlights)

            for _, conn in pairs(_G.TSBESPConnections) do
                if conn then conn:Disconnect() end
            end
            table.clear(_G.TSBESPConnections)
            return
        end

        -- Apply ESP to existing players
        for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if player ~= LocalPlayer then
                if player.Character then createHighlight(player, player.Character) end
                
                local charConn = player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
                    if _G.TSBESPState then createHighlight(player, char) end
                end)
                table.insert(_G.TSBESPConnections, charConn)
            end
        end

        -- Handle joining players
        local playerAddedConn = Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(function(player)
            if player ~= LocalPlayer then
                local charConn = player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
                    if _G.TSBESPState then createHighlight(player, char) end
                end)
                table.insert(_G.TSBESPConnections, charConn)
            end
        end)
        table.insert(_G.TSBESPConnections, playerAddedConn)
    end,
})

-- Global ESP Tracking Setup
_G.ESPConnections = _G.ESPConnections or {}
_G.ESPHighlights = _G.ESPHighlights or {}
_G.ESPState = false

visualTab:Toggle({
    Title = " Death Counter ESP",
    Value = false,
    Callback = function(state)
        local Players = game:GetService("Players")
        local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer

        _G.ESPState = state

        -- Helper functions
        local function addHighlight(char)
            if _G.ESPHighlights[char] then return end
            local hl = Instance.new("Highlight")
            hl.FillColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 0)
            hl.OutlineColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 0)
            hl.FillTransparency = 0.45
            hl.OutlineTransparency = 0
            hl.DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop
            hl.Adornee = char
            hl.Parent = char
            _G.ESPHighlights[char] = hl
        end

        local function removeHighlight(char)
            if _G.ESPHighlights[char] then
                _G.ESPHighlights[char]:Destroy()
                _G.ESPHighlights[char] = nil
            end
        end

        local function hasCounterAccessory(char)
            for _, child in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
                if child:IsA("Accessory") and child.Name == "Counter" then
                    return true
                end
            end
            return false
        end

        local function unwatchCharacter(char)
            removeHighlight(char)
            if _G.ESPConnections[char] then
                for _, conn in pairs(_G.ESPConnections[char]) do
                    if conn then conn:Disconnect() end
                end
                _G.ESPConnections[char] = nil
            end
        end

        local function watchCharacter(char)
            unwatchCharacter(char)
            _G.ESPConnections[char] = {}

            if hasCounterAccessory(char) then 
                addHighlight(char) 
            end

            _G.ESPConnections[char].added = char.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
                if child:IsA("Accessory") and child.Name == "Counter" then 
                    addHighlight(char) 
                end
            end)

            _G.ESPConnections[char].removed = char.ChildRemoved:Connect(function(child)
                if child:IsA("Accessory") and child.Name == "Counter" then 
                    removeHighlight(char) 
                end
            end)
        end

        -- Clean up existing connections & highlights when turning off
        if not state then
            for char in pairs(_G.ESPHighlights) do 
                removeHighlight(char) 
            end
            for char in pairs(_G.ESPConnections) do 
                unwatchCharacter(char) 
            end
            return
        end

        -- Initialize ESP on current players
        for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if p ~= LocalPlayer and p.Character then
                watchCharacter(p.Character)
            end
        end

        -- Hook newly joining players & character respawns
        local function handlePlayer(p)
            if p == LocalPlayer then return end
            p.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(c) 
                if _G.ESPState then watchCharacter(c) end 
            end)
            p.CharacterRemoving:Connect(unwatchCharacter)
        end

        for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do 
            handlePlayer(p) 
        end
        Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(handlePlayer)
    end,
})

-- Global Weakest Dummy ESP Tracking
_G.WeakestESPConn = _G.WeakestESPConn or nil
_G.WeakestESPState = false

visualTab:Toggle({
    Title = "Weakest Dummy ESP",
    Value = false,
    Callback = function(state)
        local Players = game:GetService("Players")
        local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")

        local HIGHLIGHT_NAME = "WeakestDummyESP"
        local ESP_COLOR = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)

        _G.WeakestESPState = state

        -- Disconnect active connection on change
        if _G.WeakestESPConn then
            _G.WeakestESPConn:Disconnect()
            _G.WeakestESPConn = nil
        end

        local function clearESP()
            for _, item in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
                if item:IsA("Highlight") and item.Name == HIGHLIGHT_NAME then
                    item:Destroy()
                end
            end
        end

        if not state then
            clearESP()
            return
        end

        local function getWeakestDummy()
            local weakestDummy = nil
            local lowestHealth = math.huge

            for _, obj in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
                if obj:IsA("Model") and obj:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") then
                    local isPlayer = Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(obj)
                    if not isPlayer then
                        local humanoid = obj:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                        if humanoid and humanoid.Health > 0 and humanoid.Health < lowestHealth then
                            lowestHealth = humanoid.Health
                            weakestDummy = obj
                        end
                    end
                end
            end

            return weakestDummy
        end

        local function applyESP(targetModel)
            if not targetModel then return end

            local highlight = Instance.new("Highlight")
            highlight.Name = HIGHLIGHT_NAME
            highlight.FillColor = ESP_COLOR
            highlight.OutlineColor = ESP_COLOR
            highlight.FillTransparency = 0.5
            highlight.OutlineTransparency = 0
            highlight.DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop
            highlight.Adornee = targetModel
            highlight.Parent = targetModel
        end

        -- Main loop hooked to connection variable
        _G.WeakestESPConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
            if not _G.WeakestESPState then return end
            clearESP()
            local weakest = getWeakestDummy()
            if weakest then
                applyESP(weakest)
            end
        end)
    end,
})

 then
                applyESP(weakest)
            end
        end)
    end,
})
